mucāttu: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattiluḷḷa mūttatukaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ ampalavācikaḷilum uyarntōr. nampi, nampiyār eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷukku uṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ peṇkaḷ maṇa ammā mār eṉappaṭuvar. iḷaiyatukaḷum mūttatukaḷum malaiyāḷattil nayiṉākkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. mūttatukaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷ maṭam, illam eṉap paṭum. nampūtirikaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷukkum ip peyarkaḷ uṇṭu. tirumaṇat tukku muṉ peṇkaḷ tiruvaḷaiyamum, kuḻalumaṇivar. viḻākkālaṅ kaḷil palakkā vaḷaiyamaṇivar. tirumaṇattukkup piṉ kātil cūṭṭum, kaḻuttil tāliyumaṇivar. vitavaikaḷ cūṭṭu māttiramaṇivar. mūttatukaḷ uṭkōyilkaḷiṉ paṭikaḷaik kaḻuvuvar. vikkirakaṅkaḷaip pātukāttuk koḷvar. kōyiṟ piracātam mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭu vāḻvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpattil mūttavaṉ māttiram maṇam ceytu koḷvaṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷ ampalavācip peṇkaḷaic campantam vaippar. āṇkaḷ nāṉku peṇkaḷ varaiyil maṇakkalām. mūtta makaṉukkup pāṭṭaṉiṉ peyariṭappaṭum. iraṇṭāvatu makaṉukkut tāyvaḻippāṭṭaṉiṉ peyariṭappaṭum. pūṇūlaṇital ēḻu vayatu mutal patiṉoru vayatukkiṭaiyil naṭaipeṟum. ivarkaḷukku maraṇattīṭṭu pattu nāḷ. kōyiluḷ iruntu ivarkaḷ uṇṇalām. mūttatukaḷ ampalavācikaḷilum uyarntōrum, iḷaiyatukaḷ pirāmaṇarukkut tāḻntōrumāvar. mucāttu mūttatu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ akappotu val eṉṉum peyarōṭu oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa. taṭampu mītu kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvam vaittu vītivalam ceyyappaṭum. taṭampu eṉpatu kēṭakam pōṉṟu kaviḻnta taṭṭu. aṭikaḷ eṉpōrum piṭārarum oruvareṉat terikiṟatu. piṭārar pūṇūlaṇiyātu kōyiṟ pūcai ceyvar.
muṭavāṇṭi: ivarkaḷ koṅkaṇa vēḷāḷaril oru piriviṉar. āṇṭi eṉpataṟkup paramparaip piccaikkārar eṉpatu poruḷ.
mutuvar: ivarkaḷ kōyamputtūr, maturai, malaiyāḷam pakutikaḷil kāṇappaṭum uḻavarākiya malaiccātiyiṉar, ivarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷait takappaṉmār eṉpar. ivarkaḷukkiṭaiyil kañcaṉ, kaṟuppuk kuñci, kuñcita, kārmēkam mutaliya peyarkaḷ peritum vaḻaṅkum. kaṟuppāyi, kūppi, pēycci mutaliya peyarkaḷ peṇkaḷukku vaḻaṅkum. kaṭaiciyākap peṟum āṇ piḷḷaikaḷukku irāmaṉ, ilakkumaṇaṉ eṉṟum, iraṭṭaip peṇkaḷukku ilaṭcumi, irāmi eṉṟum peyariṭappaṭum. ivarkaḷiṉ talaimaikkāraṉ mēlvākaṉ eṉavum, utavi atikāri mūppaṉ eṉavum paṭuvar. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. nimittaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷukku nampikkaiyuṇṭu. oruvaṉ māmaṉ makaḷai maṇakkalām. matam campantamāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷukku ivarkaḷ kurumārai aḻaippatillai. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ mukam pārkkum paṭiyākap piṇattaip putaippar. tīttaṭṭik kaṟkaḷālum, irumpālum tī mūṭṭuvar. karuṅkuraṅkiṉ iṟaicciyai uṇpar. iruḷarum mutuvarum malaippakkaṅkaḷil toṅkum tēṉ kūṭukaḷiliruntu tēṉeṭuppar.
muttiraiyaṉ: pāḷayakkārarkaḷukku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. itu teluṅkil muttirācaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkum. it teluṅkuc cātiyiṉar kiruṭṭiṇā, vaṭa ārkkāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭap peyarkaḷ tōrāvum, nāyuṭuvum. ivarkaḷ īcal kaḷaip piṭittu vaṟṟaliṭṭu pāṉaikaḷil cēmittu vaittu avaṟṟai uṇavākap payaṉpaṭuttuvar. ivarkaḷukkup piṟapput tīṭṭu pattu nāḷ.
mūtāṉ: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattiluḷḷa vāṇikam puriyum vakuppiṉar. peṇkaḷ ceṭṭiccikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ nāyariṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟuvar. pālakkāṭu, vaḷḷuvanāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ perumpālum kāṇappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṟ cilar tamakku eḻuttaccaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭappeyarai vaittu vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ tīṭṭuk kaḻippu koṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum purōkitaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭum.
meykāval: itu paṇṭāraṅkaḷukku oru peyar. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ meyyaik kāppavarkaḷ.
mēstiri: itu pōrccukkēya (Meytro) eṉṉum col. itu intiya nāṭṭu moḻikaḷil ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. mēstiri eṉpa taṟkut tiṟamaiyāṉa vēlaiyāḷ eṉpatu poruḷ. itu tamiḻnāṭṭil cemmārukkum vēṟu cila toḻilāḷarukkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkum.
mēḷakkāraṉ: ivarkaḷ vāttiyakkārar. tōriya mēḷakkāraṉ periya mēḷa cēvai māttiram cēvippaṉ. tamiḻ mēḷakkārar tēvaṭi yāṭkaḷōṭu campantappaṭṭa ciṉṉa mēḷa cēvaṉaiyum ceyvar. tēvaṭiyāḷiṉ makaḷ tāy ceytu vanta toḻilaiyē ceyvāḷ. naṭṭuvaṉ eṉpōr tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷukku naṭaṉam paḻakkuvōr.
moṇṭi: ilaṇṭā, kallaṭiccittaṉ, kallaṭimaṅkaṉ eṉpaṉa orē kūṭṭattiṉaraik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ piccai eṭukkum paramparaip paṇṭāraṅkaḷ. piccaiyiṭāviṭṭāl ivarkaḷ tamatu toṭaiyai veṭṭuvārkaḷ; kallil talaiyai uṭaippārkaḷ; vānti eṭuppārkaḷ.
moyili: ivarkaḷ teṉkaṉṉaṭattil kōyilkaḷil vēlai purivōr. peṇkaḷ tamatu kaṇavarōṭu vāḻa virumpāviṭilum, vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇam ceyya muṭiyāmaliruntālum avarkaḷ kōyilukkuc ceṉṟu kōyiṟ palic cōṟṟil cila uṇṭaikaḷ peṟṟu uṇpārkaḷ. piṉpu avarkaḷ araciṉar uttiyōkattariṭam koṇṭu pōkappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ avvāṟu ceyyat tuṇintamaikkuk kāraṇam vicārikkappaṭum. pirāmaṇap peṇkaḷāyiṉ kōyiliṉuḷ irukka aṉumatikkappaṭuvārkaḷ. avarkaḷukkut tiṉam uṇavum āṇṭil oru tuṇiyum kiṭaikkum. avarkaḷ kōyilaip perukkavum cāmarai vīcavum vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ perumpālum pirāmaṇariṉ allatu uttiyākattariṉ vaippāṭṭikaḷāka viruppārkaḷ. avarkaḷ peṟum piḷḷaikaḷ moyir eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ nūlaṇintu kōyilaip perukkik kōyiṟ paṇiviṭai ceyvar. avarkaḷ tēvaṭikar eṉavum paṭuvar.
morācu: ivarkaḷ maicūril kāṇappaṭuvar. maṇam pēcum paruvam vantatum peṇkaḷ valakkaiyiṉ mūṉṟām nālām viralkaḷai veṭṭiviṭuvar. iv vaḻakku āstirēliya, polinīciya, amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiṭaiyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
mōker: ivarkaḷ tuḷuppēcum teṉ kaṉṉaṭa mīṉpiṭikkārar. ivarkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppu paṭṭiṉameṉappaṭum. ceṉṉaiyiluḷḷa mīṉ piṭikkārar paṭṭaṇavar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ talaimaikkāraṉ kuṟikkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ip patavi talaimuṟaiyāka varuvatu. ivar kaḷiṉ urimai peṇvaḻi. kēvā eṉṉum tuḷu ampaṭṭaṉ ivarka ḷukkuc ciraikka māṭṭāṉ; koṅkaṇi ampaṭṭar ciraippar. kuḻantai piṟantu ēḻāvatu nāḷ vaṇṇātti kuḻantaiyiṉ araiyil nūl kaṭṭip peyariṭuvāḷ. ip peyar cilanāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ kaiviṭappaṭum. piṉ vēṟu peyariṭappaṭum. ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar marakkāvēru.
yūtar: koccittīvil kaṟuppu yūtar, veḷḷai yūtar eṉa iru vakai yūtarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ neṭunāṭkaḷukku muṉ palastīṉ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantōrāvar. pāskara iravivarmaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ muciṟik kōṭṭil vāḻnta yūtaṉoruvaṉukku aḷitta paṭṭaiya moṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu av varacaṉiṉ āṭciyil 36vatu āṇṭu yōcēp irappāṉ añcuvaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum yūtaṉukku aḷikkappaṭṭatu. ivaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭa urimaikaḷ varumāṟu: avaṉ aintu koṭikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttalām. ulāvac cellumpōtu vēlaiyāṭkaḷ tīpam piṭittuc cellalām. yāṉai kutiraikaḷil ēṟic cellalām. aracaraippōla pavaṉi varalām. pakaṟkālattil tīvart tiyaip payaṉpaṭuttalām. palavakai vāttiyaṅkaḷ, periya mēṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai olippikka urimaiyuṇṭu. nila pāvāṭaiyiṟ cella lām. aracaraippōl mēṟkaṭṭiyiṉ kīḻ irukkalām. irappāṉiṉ kīḻuḷḷa eḻupattiraṇṭu kuṭumpaṅkaḷum avaṉukkuk kīḻ aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭum. ic cācaṉam kaliyukam 3481il (ki.pi.370l) eḻutap paṭṭatu. yūtar cālamaṉ aracaṉ kālam mutal (ki.mu. 900) malaiyāḷak karaikaḷukku vantu vāṇikam purintārkaḷ. ki.mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil cairacu (Cyrus) eṉṉum pāracīka aracaṉiṉkīḻ aṭimaikaḷāka vāḻa virumpāta yūtamakkaḷ intiyāvil vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar.
haṉṭar (E.W.Hunter) yūtar ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. ceṅkaṭali luḷḷa mēōs, hēmasiliruntu arāpiyar ilaṅkai, malaiyāḷam mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟa ōr urōmāṉiyar malaiyāḷattil oru yūtar kuṭiyiruppaik ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaṇṭār uviṣ (Wish) eṉpār. tōmas ñāṉiyār ki.pi. 5il intiyāvai aṭaintā reṉṟum yūtar ki.pi. 69il intiyāvukkuc ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kaṟuppu yūtar, tām muṉ vantavarkaḷeṉṟum veḷḷai yūtar piṉ vantavarkaḷeṉṟum kūṟuvar. kaṟuppu yūtar yūtarallareṉṟum avarkaḷ yūta matattukkut tiruppappaṭṭa intiyareṉṟum veḷḷai yūtar kūṟuvar. erucalēm aḻikkappaṭuvataṟkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aṅku niṉṟu turattappaṭṭavarkaḷē tām eṉṟu kaṟuppu yūtar kūṟuvar. avar kaḷḷikkōṭṭaikku vantu piṉ karaṅkaṉūrai aṭain tārkaḷ. kaṟuppu yūtar iṉṉum ceppup paṭṭaiyattiṟ collappaṭṭa urimaikaḷai aṉupavikkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tamatu kuḻantaikaḷaip piṟantapiṉ eṭṭām nāḷ kōyilukkuk koṇṭu cellum pōtu paṭṭukkuṭai, tīpam mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉar. maṇamakkaḷ vītivalam varumpōtu nilapāvāṭai virittut terukkaḷ tōraṇaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nālu taṭikaḷil veḷḷāṭai kaṭṭappaṭṭa mēṟkaṭṭi avarkaḷ mītu piṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. tīvarttiyum koṇṭu cellappaṭukiṟatu. veḷḷai yūtar ivai yoṉṟaiyum kaiyāḷuvatillai. muṉ vantu kuṭiyēṟiya yūtar aṭimaikaḷai vāṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ avarkaḷukku virutta cētaṉañ ceytu avarkaḷai isravēlaraip pōl naṭattiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ camayak kiriyaikaḷāl yūtarākkappaṭṭa peṇ allatu āṇ aṭimaikaḷ oru pōtum viṟkappaṭavillai. yūtar avvaṭimaikaḷuṭaṉ kalappatāl kaṟuppu yūtar tōṉṟiṉārkaḷeṉa cilar karutiṉārkaḷ. itaṉāl kaṟuppu yūtar muṟṟākak kalappu yūtar eṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. veḷḷai yūtar paratēcikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. yūta maṇamakkaḷ kaliyāṇam muṭikkak kōyilukkuc celvataṉ muṉ maṇamakaḷiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāḷ maṇamakaḷ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvāḷ.
yūrēciyar: ivarkaḷ airōppiyat tantaikkum intiyat tāymārukkum piṟantōr. caṭṭaikkārar eṉpatum ivarkaḷukku maṟṟoru peyar. paṟaṅki eṉpatum ivarkaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkum peyar. paṟaṅki eṉpatu piṟāṅk (Frank) eṉpataṉ tiripu. piṟāṅk eṉpataṟku airōppiyaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. ivarkaḷ valaṇṭis (Walladez) allatu ullāṇṭi (Oollandy) eṉavum paṭuvar. ivai ollāntis (Hollandis) eṉpataṉ tiripu. ip peyar 17ām , 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ollāntar vaḻiyāka vantatu.
yōki: teluṅkup piccai yeṭukkum paṇṭāraṅkaḷ yōkikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. tamiḻnāṭṭil ivar tōṭṭiyāṉ eṉappaṭuvar.
vaṭukaṉ: teluṅku nāṭṭavar vaṭukar eṉappaṭuvar. tamiḻnāṭṭil teluṅku pēcukiṉṟavarkaḷum vaṭukar eṉappaṭuvar.
vaṭṭakkāraṉ: ivarkaḷ vaṉṉiyar, cekkāruḷ oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ vaṭṭakkāṭṭārkaḷāvar.
vaṇṇattāṉ: ivarkaḷ nāyarukku veḷḷai veḷukkum veḷutteṭātārāvar.
vaṇṇāṉ: ivarkaḷ tām vīrapattira vamicattavar eṉak kūṟuvar. vaṇṇār ampaṭṭarilum tāḻnta vakuppiṉar. peṇkaḷ pūppaṭainta piṉ maṇa muṭippar. tirumaṇattil maṇamakaṉiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ cātit teyvam kurunātaṉ, malaiyāḷa vaṇṇāṉ, maṇṇāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. malaiyāḷa malaiccātiyiṉaruḷ oru kūṭṭattiṉarum maṇṇā reṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷai maṇṇāṉ allatu vaṇṇāṉ eṉakkoṇṭu mayaṅkuta lākātu. maṇṇāruḷ peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavarai maṇappar. malaiyāḷat til pakavati kōyilkaḷil vaṇṇāṉ pūcāriyāka viruppāṉ.
valaiyar: ivarkaḷ valaiyāl mīṉkaḷaiyum, paṟavaikaḷaiyum piṭippar. ampalakkāraṉ. cērvaikkāraṉ, vēṭaṉ, civiyāṉ, kuruvik kāraṉ mutaliyaṉavum ivarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkum. ampalakkārar tām kaṇṇappa nāyaṉāriṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvar. maṇamakaḷiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ peṇṇukkut tāli kaṭṭuvaḷ. maṇat tukku muṉ peṇkaḷ piḷḷaip peṟuvatu kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭa māṭṭātu. avvāṟu piṟakkum piḷḷaikaḷ vēṟupāṭiṉṟic cātiyiṟ cērttukkoḷḷappaṭuvārkaḷ. viyapicārip peṇkaḷ erukkamālai cūṭṭi cēṟṟukkūṭaiyaic cumantu kirāmattaic cuṟṟivarac ceyvārkaḷ. valaiyar teyvaṅkaḷ ciṅkappiṭāri(aiyaṉār), patiṉeṭṭāmpaṭik kaṟuppaṉ mutaliyaṉa.
vallampaṉ: ivarkaḷ tañcāvūr, tirucci, maturai māvaṭṭaṅ kaḷil vāḻum uḻavariṉ oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ vēḷāḷat tantaiyaruk kum valaiyat tāymārukkum tōṉṟiyavarkaḷ eṉak karutappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ cātit talaivaṉ cērvai eṉappaṭuvaṉ. ivarkaḷil āṭavar tāymāmaṉ makaḷai allatu tantaiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ makaḷai maṇappar. cila camayaṅkaḷil pattu vayatup paiyaṉukku irupatu allatu ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vayatuḷḷa peṇ kaliyāṇam ceyyappaṭuvāḷ. ap poḻutu avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ tamaiyaṉ allatu vaḷarnta uṟaviṉaṉaic cērntu piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟuvāḷ.
vaḷḷuvaṉ: ivarkaḷ paṟaiyar, paḷḷikaḷiṉ purōkitarāvar. pirāmaṇarukku muṉ vaḷḷuvar pallavariṉ purōkitarāka viruntaṉar. paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatukku mēṟpaṭṭa āṇkaḷ nūlaṇivar. ivarkaḷiṉ āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum cōciyam colvar. cātittalaivaṉ kōṟ kāraṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. tirumaṇak kālattil maṇamakaṉ pariyam, uṟavu muṟaikkaṭṭu (peṇṇiṉ cuṟṟattārukkuk koṭukkum paṇam) pantal varicai mutaliyavaṟṟaip peṇ vīṭṭārukkuk koṭuppāṉ. maṇavaṟai, kuṭaviḷakku, alaṅkāra viḷakku, pālikai viḷakku mutaliyavaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭum. ataic cuṟṟik kuṭaṅkaḷ vaikkappaṭum. ivai kuṭumpat teyvaṅkaḷaik kuṟippaṉa. vaḷḷuvar vaḷḷuvap paṇṭā raṅkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar.
vaṉṉiyaṉ: ivarkaḷ valaiyaṉ, ampalavar, paḷḷikaḷil oru piriviṉar.
vālaṉ: ivarkaḷ koccip pakkaṅkaḷil vāḻum mīṉ piṭikkum kulattiṉar. ip peyar valaiyaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripu. pūppaṭaiyamuṉ peṇkaḷukkut tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭattappaṭum. āṇkaḷ ettaṉai peṇkaḷai vēṇṭumāṉālum maṇakkalām. vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇam puriyalām. talaippūppeytiya peṇ nālunāḷ taṉiyaṟai yil viṭappaṭuvāḷ; aintāvatunāḷ avaḷ tōyntapiṉ viruntu naṭaipeṟum. ivarkaḷiṉ cātittalaivaṉ arayaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. avaṉ aracaṉiṉ “tīṭṭuṟattiṉālai”t terivippāṉ (tīṭṭu - eḻuttu) ivarkaḷukku makkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. iṟantavaṉiṉ makaṉ oru mātam mayirviṉai ceytukoḷḷa māṭṭāṉ.
vaṉṉiyaṉ: malaiyāḷac cekkāṉ pōṉṟu eṇṇeyūṟṟu vōr vaṉṉiyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivar vīṭukaḷil vaṇṇār uṇpa tillai. ivarkaḷiṉ cātip paṭṭappeyar ceṭṭi. maṇamākātavarkaḷ iṟantāl erukkañ ceṭikkup pōli maṇac caṭaṅku naṭattappaṭum.
vāriyar: ivarkaḷ ampalavācikaḷil oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum nalla vaittiyarum cōtiṭarumāvar. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ nāyarp peṇkaḷaip pōlat talaiyiṉ iṭappakkattē kuṭumi muṭintiruppar. ivarkaḷiṉ tāli māttirā eṉappaṭum. atu mattaḷam pōṉṟa vaṭivu uṭaiyatu. maṟṟa aṇikaḷ entiramum kuḻalum. ivarkaḷ nāyarp peṇkaḷaṇiyum tōṭaṇivar; neṟṟiyil cantaṉat tāl kuṟi yiṭuvar. ivarkaḷ kōyiliṟ ceyyum vēlai kaḻakam eṉappaṭum. itu orupōtu kaḻuvu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantirukkalām. kōyilil vaṟiyatukaḷ collum vēlaiyai ivarkaḷ ceytal vēṇṭum. ivar kaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. tiruvitāṅkūril ōṇattukkārar eṉṉum vaṟiyatukaḷiṉ cottu āṇ, peṇ eṉṉum iru pālārukkum cama mākap pirikkappaṭum. peṇkaḷ pirāyamaṭaiyu muṉ tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅku naṭakkiṟatu. kuṭivaippu muṟaiyil itu ceyyappaṭumāyiṉ campanta muṟaiyil kaliyāṇam maṟupaṭiyum ceyyap paṭa vēṇṭiyatillai. paruva maṭainta peṇkaḷ maṇamākum varai kōyiluḷ nuḻaital kūṭātu. ivarkaḷukku maraṇat tīṭṭup paṉṉiraṇṭu nāḷ.
vāli cukkirīvar: ilampāṭikaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷ tām vāli cukkirīvar vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ eṉpar.
vilkuruppu: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷak kammāḷarukkum ampaṭṭa rukkum kurukkaḷ. nāyariṉ tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇattukku ivarkaḷ oru villum cila ampukaḷum koṭukka vēṇṭum.
vīrapattirar: ivarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṇṇār. ivarkaḷ tammai vīrapattirar vamicattavar eṉak kūṟikkoḷvar.
veṭṭiyāṉ: ivaṉ paṟaiccēriyiluḷḷa oru toṭṭiyāṉ allatu tōṭṭi eṉṉum uttiyōkattaṉ. ivaṉ vayalkaḷukku nīr pāyumpaṭi kālvāykaḷait tiṟantu viṭuvaṉ. piracittappaṭuttum (ceyti kaḷai aṟivikkum) mēḷamaṭippāṉ. ivaṉ cuṭalaikkut talaivaṉ. paṟaiyariṉ maṇaṅkaḷil pāṉaikaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭum. veṭṭi yāṉ vakuppiṉar valaṅkaiyiṉar.
vēṭaṉ: ivarkaḷ vēṭṭaiyāṭum cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷiṟ cilar pōr vīrarkaḷāka viruntaṉar. vēṭṭuvar eṉṉum cātiyiṉar tām vēṭarukkum uyarntōr eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vitavaikaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉai maṇappar. ivarkaḷ tamatu paramparai kaṇṇappa nāyaṉāri liruntu varukiṉṟa teṉak kūṟuvar. vēṭariṉ paṭṭappeyar nāyakkaṉ. cēlam pakutiyil vēṭaṉ tiruvaḷar eṉappaṭuvaṉ. ivarkaḷ kaliyāṇam poruttukiṉṟamaiyāl kaṭṭukkoṭukkiṟa cāti eṉap paṭuvar. ivarkaḷ mēl vāyiṟ pallai arāvik kūrākki viṭuvārkaḷ. kāṭarum ivvāṟu ceyvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ cāttaṉ. ivarkaḷum kuraṅkiṉ iṟaicciyai uṇpar. pūppukkālattil peṇkaḷ taṉik kuṭicaiyil aintu nāṭkaḷukkut taṅkiyiruppar.
vēṭṭuvaṉ: ivarkaḷ cēlam, kōyamputtūr, maturaip pakutikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum uḻutoḻil ceyyum vēṭṭaiyāṭum makkaḷ. vēṭar ilaṅkai vēṭarukku iṉamuṭaiyavar. ivarkaḷukku ampaṭṭar uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ vēṭṭuva ampaṭṭar eṉappaṭuvar. vēṭṭuvariliru piriviṉa ruṇṭu. oru piriviṉar āṭai uṭuppar; maṟṟavar ilaikaḷai uṭuppar.
vēlakkāṭṭāḷvāṉ: ivarkaḷ tiruvitāṅkūr ampaṭṭariṉ talaimaikkārar. ivarkaḷ aracaṉukku mayirviṉai ceyvar. vaṭamalai yāḷattil ivarkaḷ valiñciyāṉ, nāvitaṉ, nācuvaṉ eṉappaṭuvar. vaṭamalaiyāḷattil ivarkaḷukku makkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu.
vēl: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷap paṟaiyaril oru piriviṉar. malai yāḷa malaiccātiyiṉaril oru piriviṉarum vēlaṉmār eṉappaṭuvar.
vēḷaṉ: (vēḷaṉ - kuyavaṉ) tiruvitāṅkūril paṟaiyarum vēḷāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. malaiyāḷattil pēyk kūttāṭum pāṇar pōṉṟa oru cātiyiṉar vēḷaṉ (vēlaṉ) eṉappaṭuvar. peṇkaḷ veḷutteṭātti eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇar kōyilkaḷil nuḻaital kūṭātu. ivarkaḷ taḷakkallukku veḷiyē niṟṟal vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷukkut tālik kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇamum campantamum taṉittaṉiyē naṭattappaṭum. vaṟiya kuṭumpap peṇ paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatākiyirukkum pōtu tālikaṭṭik koḷvāḷ. itu pakavaṉ tāli eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu.
vēḷampar: ivarkaḷ kayiṟṟil ēṟik kūttāṭum kūttāṭikaḷ; kaḻaik kūttar.
vairāvi: vairāvikaḷ paṇṭārattil oru piriviṉar. maturaip pakutiyiṟ vairāvikaḷ mēḷakāraril oru piriviṉar.
vaṭaintiyak kulaṅkaḷum kuṭikaḷum
akarvālā(Agarwala) - mēl (upper) intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum mukkiya vaṇikar; nāka kaṉṉiyiliruntu tōṉṟiyavar; camaṇamatattiṉar.
akāriyar(Agarya) - mattiya intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum ātik kuṭikaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ toḻil irumpu maṇṇai ulaiyiliṭṭu irum peṭuttal.
akār (Ahar) - urokil kāṇṭil (Rohilkhand) vāḻum iṭaiyar.
akir (Akir) - vaṭakku intiyā, mattiya intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum iṭaiyarum payiriṭuvōrum.
akōm - acāmiluḷḷa cāṉ (Shan) kūṭṭattiṉar.
akōripanti - oruvakaip paṇṭāri vakuppiṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ matak koḷkaippaṭi naramāmicattaiyum malattaiyumuṇṇalām.
aṅkami - acām nāṭṭu nākar.
acālā(Hasala) - maicūrilē kaṉṉaṭanāṭṭil kāṭṭut tiraviyaṅ kaḷaic cēkarikkuñ cātiyār.
apatāṉi - piramaputtirāvukku vaṭakkiluḷḷa acām malaic cātiyār.
apōr(Abor) - acām kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉar.
araṭṭā(Aratta) - paḻaiya pañcāp cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷukkut tāyvaḻi urimaiyuṇṭu.
ari - kiḻakku intiyāvil nakar cutti ceyyum cātiyār.
arōrā - pañcāppil vāḻum vaṇika kūṭṭattār.
avāṉ(Awan) - pañcāppil vēḷāṇmaipuriyum kāṇiyāḷa mucilimkaḷ.
avō(Ao) - acām nākar
(i)ṭārci (Darzi) - intiya taiyaltaikkum cātiyār
(i)ṭārṭ(Dard) - tātistāṉiluḷḷa imālayap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ
(i)ṭālvā(Dalfa) - piramaputtirāviṉ vaṭakaraiyiluḷḷa acām cātiyār
(i) ṭāṉkar(Dankar) - mēṟku intiyāvil vāḻum iṭaic cātiyār
(i) ṭi ori(Deori) - acām ātikkuṭikaḷiṉ mantiravātikaḷ
(i)ṭēṭ (Dhed) - mēṟku intiyāvil vayalvēlai ēval vēlai ceyyum veḷiccātiyiṉar.
(i)lapcā (Lacha) - vaṅkāḷattilum imayamalaiyilumuḷḷa malaiccātiyiṉar.
(i)lālpeki(Lalbegi) - mēl intiyāvil cuttam ceyyum toḻilāḷar.
(u) luṣi (Lushci) - ōr acām cātiyār
(u) lōkār (Lohar) - kōtāvari vaṭakkil vāḻum kollar
(u) lōkāṉā - cintu nāṭṭil vāṇikam ceyyum cātiyār.
(u) lōṭā (Lodha) - vēlai purivōrum, payiriṭuvōrum aikkiyamākāṇaṅkaḷ, akrā, avuṭt mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇap paṭuvar.
(u) lōtā (Lhota) - acām nākacātiyār
(u)lōy (Loi) - acāmil maṇippūrppakutiyil vāḻum paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ.
(u) lōri (Lori) - aluccistāṉattil alaintu tiriyum cātiyār. ivarkaḷ vāttiyakārarum takaravēlai ceyvōrumāvar.
eruvā (Yeruva) - kuṟukkaril tāḻnta cātiyiṉarākiya ātik kuṭikaḷ
o (Ho) - mattiya kiḻakku mākāṇattiluḷḷa kolāriyar (muṇṭar cātiyaic cērntavar.)
ocuvāl (Oswal) - irācaputtāṉattil mēvāriluḷḷa vaṇika cātiyiṉar.
orayaṉ (Oraon) - coṭṭanākapuri ātikkuṭikaḷil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcuvōr.
kaccari (Kachari) =- acām ātikkuṭikaḷ
kacci (Kachi) - vaṭaintiyāvil kacakacā payiriṭuvōr
kacciṉ (Kachin) - pārmāvilum vaṭa ellaip puṟattum vāḻum ātikkuṭikaḷ
kañcar (Kanjar) - vaṭa intiyāvilum mattiya intiyāvilum pāymuṭaivōrum, kuṟṟavāḷikaḷumāyuḷḷa nāṭōṭikaḷ.
kaṭapā (Gadaba) - oricāvil vāḻum kolāriyar.
kaṭṭi (Gaddi) - pañcāppil vāḻum iṭaiyar; pakutiyiṉar mucilimkaḷ; pakutiyiṉar intukkaḷ.
kaṇṭayaṭ (Khandait) - oricāvil pakuti kāṇiyāḷarum, pakuti paṭṭāḷac cēvai purivōrumākiya cātiyār
kaṇṭiyōki (Handi jogi) - teṉṉintiyap paṇṭāraṅkaḷil oru piriviṉar; paṉṟi vaḷarttal, cillarai vaittiyam purital, pāmpāṭṭutal mutaliyaṉa ivarkaḷ toḻil.
kattari - pañcāppilum, vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilum vāṇikam ceyyum cātiyār.
kattāri (Kathari) - pampāyk kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉar.
kapūli - apkāṉ allatu paṭṭāṇiyar.
kayastā(Kayastha) - vaṅkāḷattil eḻuttu vēlai ceyyum cātiyār.
karaṉ (Karan) - oricāvil eḻuttāḷa vakuppiṉar.
kalitā (Kalita) - acām vivacāyikaḷ.
kalṭiyā (Haldiya) - akaravālarkaḷiloru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ mañcaḷai uṇṇamāṭṭār.
kaltā - oricā vivacāyikaḷ.
kalvāy (Halwai) - vaṭa intiyāvil miṭṭāy ceyvōr.
kalvār (Kalwar) - vaṭa intiyāvil cārāyam vaṭippōrum viṟpōrum.
kāci (Khasi) - acāmil vāḻum moṉ kemar moḻi pēcum makkaḷ; ivarkaḷukkup peṇvaḻit tāyamuṇṭu.
kācīrā (Kasera) - vaṭaintiyāvil pittaḷai vēlai ceyvōr.
kāti (Khati) - pañcāppil vaṇṭi ceyyum taccar.
kātik (Khatik) - vaṭa intiyāvil vēlaiyāḷar. iṟaicci yaṭippōr, marakkaṟi viyāpārikaḷ.
kātōṇi (Kathoni) - acāmil necavāḷar
kāmi (Kami) - nēpāḷak kollar
kāyāvāl (Gayawal) - pirāmaṇap paṇṭāraṅkaḷiloru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ kāyāvukku yāttirai celvōriṭum tāṉattaip peṟṟu vāḻvar.
kāriyā (Kharia) - coṭṭa nākapuriyilum, mattiya intiyāvilum vāḻum kolāriya ātikkuṭikaḷ.
kāravā (Kharava) - mēṟku intiyāvil uppu viḷaivippōr.
kārō (Garo) - tāyvaḻi urimai peṟum acām paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ.
kārvāli (Garwali) - imayamalaiccātiyār.
kāvir (Kafir) - imālaya cātiyār.
kāṉkar (Khanger) - mattiya intiyāviṟ kāvaṟkārac cātiyār.
kāṉci (Ganchi) - mēṟku intiyāvil cekkāṭṭuvōrum eṇṇey viṟpōrum.
kāhār(Kahar) - vaṭaintiyāvil mīṉpiṭikārar, kuyavar, vīṭṭu vēlaikkārar.
kās (Khas) - nēpāḷap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷil oruvar.
kicāṉ (Kisan) - mēl(Upper) intiyāvil payiriṭuvōr.
kuyār (Gujar) - pañcāppilum vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilum kāṇap paṭum mantai mēyppōr. ivarkaḷ veḷḷai avuṇariṉ (White Huns) cantatiyiṉar.
kāki (Kaki) - acāmilum parmāvilum vāḻum malaic cātiyiṉar.
kēvāṭ (Kewat) - vaṭaintiya mīṉpiṭikkum, payiriṭum cāti.
kaiparttā (Kaibartha) - vaṅkāḷattil mīṉpiṭikkum oru cātiyiṉar.
koc (Kochh) - vaṭa vaṅkāḷattilum acāmilumuḷḷa oru cātiyiṉar.
koṇṭu (Kond) - oricāvil vāḻum malaiccātiyiṉar.
korakā (Koraga) - teṉ kaṉṉaṭattil kūṭaimuṭaiyum veḷic cātiyiṉar.
koṉyāk (Konyak) - acām nākar.
kō (Kho) - imayamalaiccātiyār.
kōcā (Kjoja) - mēṟku intiyāvil vāḻum iraṇṭu vaṇikap piriviṉar.
kōṭakā (Kodaga) - paṭṭāḷattil cērum kūrkkacātiyār.
kōtā (Kota) - nīlakiriyil vāḻum cātiyiṉar, vāttiyakārar.
kōpā (Gopa) - vaṅkāḷa iṭaiyar.
kōrā (Kora) - coṭṭa nākapuriyil kuyavar; kolāriya uṟpattiyiṉar.
kōri(Koiri) - mēl intiyāvil payiriṭum, mantai mēykkum cātiyār.
kōrku(Korku) - mattiya intiyāvil malai allatu kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉar.
kōlā - vaṭaintiyāvil iṭaiyarum pāl viṟpōrum.
kōli - mēṟku intiyāvil vēlaiyāṭkaḷ.
kōṣa - pañcāpil pāl viṟkum iṭaiyar.
caṭkop (Sadgop) - vaṅkāḷattil payiriṭuvōr.
caṇṭāḷar - intu camūkattil tāḻnta cātiyiṉar. vaṅkāḷattil payiriṭuvōr, ōṭamōṭṭuvōr, mīṉ piṭippōr ic cātiyiṉarāvar.
cattarkāy (Chattarkhai) - oricāvil 1886il nērnta pañcattil cattiraṅkaḷiluṇṭatāl cātiyai iḻantavarkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiyavar kaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiya kūṭṭattiṉar.
camarkūr (Chamargaur) - irācaputtira cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷil intu, mucilim pirivukaḷuṇṭu.
carasvat (Saraswat) - pañcāppiluḷḷa pirāmaṇariloru piriviṉar.
carāk (Sarak) - kiḻakkintiyāvil payiriṭuvōrum necavu ceyvōrumākiya cātiyar.
cavarar - oricāviluḷḷa kolāriya vakuppiṉar.
cākā - vaṅkāḷattil cārāyam vaṭippōr, viṟpōr.
cākmā (Chakma) - kiḻakku vaṅkāḷattil caṭṭāl koṅkil vāḻum ātikkuṭikaḷ.
cākiṭpeṣṣā (Shagerdpesha) - oricā nāṭṭil vīṭṭu vēlaikkārar.
cāṅ (Chang) - acām nākar.
cācā (Chasa) - oricāvil vāḻum payiriṭum cātiyār.
cāntāl - coṭṭa nākapuri, pikār, vaṅkāḷam mutaliya nāṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭum kolāriyak kuḻu.
cāṉcilā (Sansila) - irāputtāṉattiluḷḷa kuṟṟam puriyum cāti.
ciṟpāvaṉ (Chitpavan) - koṅkaṇa pirāmaṇariloru piriviṉar.
ciṉ (Chin) - acāmilum parmāvilumuḷḷa cātiyiṉar.
cut (Sut) - acāmil payiriṭuvōr.
cutār (Sutar) - teṟkallāta maṟṟai iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum taccar.
cūttiratār - vaṅkāḷattaccar
cēmā - acām nāka vakuppiṉar.
cērō(Chero) - aikkiya mākāṇaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum payiriṭu vōr; kolāriya iṉattavar.
cōttirā (Chodra) - pampāyil ūr cuṟṟum kūṭṭattiṉar.
cōṉi (Soni) - mēṟku intiyāvil taṭṭār.
caurar - pañcāppilum vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilumuḷḷa cuttañ ceyvōr.
tāṭō (Thado) - acāmiluḷḷa oru cāti.
tātirā(Thathera) - pittaḷai vēlai ceyyum cāti.
tāvaiv (Tawaif) - mucilim intukkaḷuḷ, mucilimkaḷukkiṭai yiluḷḷa naṭaṉamāṭum viyapicārikaḷ.
tāri (Dhari) - vāttiyakārar.
tāṉuk (Dhanuk) - vaṭa intiyāvil vayal vēlai ceyvōr.
tili (Tili) - vaṅkāḷa eṇṇey vāṇikar.
tumāl (Dumal) - oricāvil payiriṭum cātiyār.
tūri (Turi) - cūṭiya nākapuriyil kāṇappaṭum vivacāyam, mūṅkil vēlai, kūṭai muṭaital mutaliya toḻilkaḷ purivōr.
teyvēntiraṉ kulavēḷāḷar - paḷḷaṉ cātikku oru paṭṭap peyar.
telakā (Telaga) - teluṅku nāṭṭu vivacāyikaḷ.
tēli (Teli) - kiḻakku intiyāvil eṇṇeyāṭṭuvōrum, eṇṇey viṟpōrum.
tēṣ āst (Deshasth) - marāṭṭip pirāmaṇariloru piriviṉar.
tokrā (Dogra) - imayamalaic cātiyāril oru kūṭṭattiṉar.
tōm (Dom) - cuttañ ceyyum cātiyār.
nevār (Newar) - kuṟukkarukku iṉamuṭaiyavarkaḷākak kūṟik koḷḷum nēpāḷa cātiyār.
patāṉ - vaṭamēṟku ellaippuṟattil vāḻum mucilimkaḷ.
palucci - āpkāṉistāṉattiloru cātiyār.
paṉiyar (Baniya) - vaṭṭikkup paṇaṅkoṭuppōr; vaṇikarukku irācaputtāṉattilum mēṟku intiyāvilum vaḻaṅkum peyar.
paṉacikā (Banajiga) - kaṉṉaṭa vaṇika cātiyār; teluṅku palicā pōṉṟavar.
paṉyarā (Banyara) - tirintu viyāpārañ ceypavar, mantai vaittiruppavarkaḷākiya nāṭōṭi makkaḷukku vaḻaṅkum peyar.
pāṭ (Bhat) - vaṭa intiya pāṭṭuppāṭum cātiyār.
pāṭṉi (patni) - vaṭavaṅkāḷa mīṉpiṭikārarum kūṭai iḻaip pōrum.
pācavi - kōyilukkut tēvaṭiyāḷāka viṭappaṭṭa kaṉṉaṭap peṇ.
pāci (pasi) - vaṭa intiyāvil kaḷḷiṟakkuvōr.
pāpkaṉ (Babhan) - vaṭa intiyāvil kāṇiyāḷarum payiriṭuvōru mākiya kūṭṭattiṉar.
pāyṭiyā (Baidya) - vaṅkāḷa maruttuva cāti.
pāyti (Baiti) - cuṇṇāmpu cūḷaiyiṭum cātiyār, ivarkaḷ cuṇṇāṟi, ṭōli eṉavum paṭuvar. (ṭōli - mēḷam)
pārakiyā (parahiya) - aikkiya mākāṇa malaic cātiyār.
pārūy (Barui) - oru vaṅkāḷac cātiyiṉar.
pār (Bhar) - aikkiya mākāṇattilum, pikārilumuḷḷa cātiyār.
pārkāy (Barhai) - vaṭa intiya taccaccātiyiṉar.
pārjā (parja) - oricāvilorucātiyiṉar; kolāriya moḻi pēcuvōr.
pārpuñcā - vaṭa intiya tāṉiya viyāpārikaḷ.
pāṉki (Bhangi) - teṉṉintiyā allāta iṭaṅkaḷil aḻuk keṭukkum cātiyār.
pāṉavār (panawar) - irācaputtiraril akkiṉi kulattavar.
pirākūy - paluccistāṉattil vāḻum tirāviṭa cātiyār.
pirāli (pirali) - vaṅkāḷap pirāmaṇariloru piriviṉar.
pirittiyal paṇiyar (Brithiyal Banya) - acāmil kāṇappaṭum veḷiccātiyiṉar.
picōṉi (Besoni) - irājaputtāṉāvil kāṇappaṭum kalappuc cātiyār
pirkōr (Birhor) - cōṭṭa nākapuriyiṟ kāṇappaṭum pakutiyil alaintu tiriyum cātiyār.
piṉṭ (Bind) - aikkiya mākāṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭum payiriṭuvō rum, vayal vēlai ceyvōrum.
purucō (Burusho) - intukkuṣil kāṇappaṭum imayamalaic cātiyār.
puṟokpā (Brokpa) - intukkuṣil kāṇappaṭum imayamalai ātikkuṭikaḷ.
pūjā(Bhuja) - cōṭṭa nākapuriyilum, vaṅkāḷattilum vāḻum kolāriya makkaḷ.
pūyiṉmāli (Bhuinmali) - kiḻakku vaṅkāḷattila pallakkuc cumappōrum ēval vēlai purivōrum.
pekkaṉvālā - paṉṟiyiṟaicci viṟpōr.
villāḷar - villiyarum irācaputtirarum kalanta kūṭṭattiṉar. mēṟku intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭuvar.
peṉ i icirēl - pampāy yūtar .
poṇṭōpārcā (Bondoporja) - oricāviṟ kāṇappaṭum piṟpōkkāṉa kūṭṭattiṉar.
pōttiyā (Bhotiya) - imācalattiṉ kīḻp piratēcattiṟ kāṇappaṭum maṅkōliṭṭu makkaḷ; nēpāḷa uṟpattiyiṉar.
pōkcā (Bhoksa) - imayamalai aṭivārattilum aikkiya mākāṇattilum kāṇappaṭum kūṭṭattiṉar. irācaputtira uṟpatti yiṉar.
pōrā (Bohra) - mēṟku intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum mucilimkaḷ.
māḵli (Mahli) - mattiya intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭum kūli vēlai ceyvōr, kūṭai muṭaivōr.
māk (Magh) - vaṅkāḷattiluḷḷa pautta vakuppiṉar.
mākarā (Mahara) - acāmiluḷḷa oru cātiyiṉar.
mākiṭā - teluṅkar; tōl vēlai ceyvōr; cakkiliyarai ottavar.
mācvār (Majhwar) - mattiya intiyak kāṭṭuc cātiyār.
māpār (Mabar) - cintu mākāṇattiluḷḷa oru cātiyār.
māvar - vaṅkāḷattil, pikāriluḷḷa malaiccāti.
māli - teṟkilallāta iṭattil vīṭṭu vēlaikkārarum kāykaṟit tōṭṭam ceyvōrum.
mālō - vaṅkāḷattil mīṉpiṭikkum, paṭakōṭṭum cātiyār.
mēkār (Mehar) - vayal vēlai, kūli vēlai ceyvōr.
yaṉṉappaṉṭ - kōṇi piṉṉum teluṅkuc cāti.
yasvā - irācaputtāṉa vaṇikacātiyār.
yāṭ (Jat) - vēḷāṇmai ceyyum cāti.
yāliyakai pārtā - vaṅkāḷattil mīṉ piṭikkum cāti.
yāluvā, yāvō - vaṅkāḷattiluḷḷa mīṉ piṭikkum cāti.
yuvāṅ - oricā malaikaḷil vāḻum oriyar.
yūki - acāmil paṭṭuppuḻu vaḷarppōrum paṭṭu necavu ceyvōrum.
vā (Wa) - kiḻakkup parmāvil kāṇappaṭum ātikkuṭikaḷ.
villiyar (Bhil) - mattiya intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭum ātikkuṭikaḷ.
veraṅki(Fering) - vaṅkāḷattiluḷḷa paṟaṅkiyar (pōrccukkēya uṟpatti).
ilaṅkait tamiḻar
(iḵtu iṟṟaikku nūṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ caimaṉ cācic ceṭṭi avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa “The, Castes, Customs, Manners and Literature of the Tamils ” eṉṉum nūliliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu)
paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ
karppatāṉam - itu karppak kuṟi kāṇappaṭṭavuṭaṉ ceyyap paṭuvatu. vīṭu muḻuvatum pacuccāṇiyāl meḻukic cuttañ ceyyappaṭu kiṟatu. talaivāyilil amaikkappaṭṭa pīṭattil piḷḷaiyār piṭittu vaikkappaṭukiṟatu. purōkitar tēṅkāyuṭaittu ōmam vaḷarttup pūcai ceytapiṉ karuppiṇikku ācīrvātañ ceyvār. itaṟkuk kūliyāka paṇam, tuṇi allatu māṭu koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu.
pumcavaṉam - itu mūṉṟāvatu mātattil ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. mēṟkūṟiyatu pōl ceytapiṉ peṇkaḷ karuppavatiyiṉ talaiyaic cuṟṟi ālatti eṭuppārkaḷ. peṇṇiṉ tantai oru kōṭi kāppaip peṇṇukkuk koṭuppār.
cīmantam - itu ēḻāvatu mātattil ceyyappaṭuvatu. karup piṇiyiṉ cuṟṟattavar pantaliṉkīḻ kūṭiyiruppar. avarkaḷ mūṉṟu pāṉaiyil cōṟu camaittut teyvaṅkaḷukku paṭaippārkaḷ. peṇ kaliyāṇa uṭaiyōṭu muṉṉāl vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa uralil vaḷaintu kaiviralāl toṭṭuk koṇṭu niṟpāḷ. kaṇavaṉiṉ pakutiyaic cērnta peṇkaḷ akappaiyil ciṟitu pālai eṭuttu ovvoruvarāka avaḷ tōḷil vārppārkaḷ. cila pakutikaḷil kōyililiruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭa tīrttattai avaḷ uṟiñcik kuṭikkumpaṭi koṭuppārkaḷ.
peṇ karuppamāka virukkumpōtu peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉ tāṭiyai veṭṭuvatillai. virataṅkaḷaṉuṭṭippāṉ. kuḻantai piṟappa taṟku iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷiṉ muṉ kuṭiyirukkum vīṭṭi liruntu peṇ iṉṉoru iṭattukku māṟṟappaṭuvāḷ. itu piḷḷaip pēṟṟāl vīṭu tīṭṭaṭaiyāmaliruppataṟkākavākum. piḷḷaippēṟṟiṉpiṉ 16, 21 allatu 31 nāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ purōkitar vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu tīṭṭuk kaḻippār. kuḻantai piṟantatum tantai cōtiṭariṭam ceṉṟu cātaka palaṉai aṟikiṟār. kuḻantai piṟantu pattu allatu paṉṉiraṇṭu nāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ piḷḷaikkup peyariṭappaṭu kiṟatu. perumpālum peyar tantaiyāliṭappaṭukiṟatu.
12 allatu 16vatu nāḷil kātu kuttappaṭukiṟatu. perumpālum itu 6vatu allatu 8vatu mātattil naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. 6ām allatu 8ām mātattil cōṟu tīṟṟuc caṭaṅku naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. kiriyai muṭivil kuḻantaikkuc ciṟṟāṭaiyuṭutti kuḻantaiyaik kōyilukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟu ciṟṟāṭai kōyilukkuk koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. mutalāvatu allatu mūṉṟāvatu vārattil muṭi iṟakkappaṭukiṟatu. ucciyil allatu neṟṟikku mēl oru piṭi mayir viṭṭu nāvitaṉ talaiyai maḻittu viṭuvāṉ. cila pakutikaḷil uṟumālkaṭṭu eṉṉum viruntuk koṇṭāṭṭam naṭakkum varaiyil iḷaiñar talaiyai maḻittuk koḷḷamāṭṭārkaḷ. ciṟuvar, ciṟumiyaraip paḷḷikku vaittal ēṭu toṭakkutal eṉappaṭukiṟatu. paḷḷikkūṭa āciriyar mūṉṟu paṉai ōlaic caṭṭaṅkaḷil arivariyai eḻuti eḻuttukkaḷukku mañcaḷ pūcit tūpaṅ kāṭṭik koṭuppatu ik kiriyaiyākum. ciṟuvar paḷḷikkuc cellum vayatu 5. peṇ paruvamaṭaintatum cāmarttiyak kaliyāṇam naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. pūppu aṭaintatum avaḷ vīṭṭil maṟaivāṉa oru iṭattil ēḻu allatu 11 nāṭkaḷukku viṭappaṭukiṟāḷ. piṉpu avaḷukku muḻukkāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. appoḻutu naṇparum cuṟṟattavarum aḻaikkappaṭuvārkaḷ. muḻukkāṭṭum pōtu peṇkaḷ oruvar mītoruvar mañcaḷ nīr teḷittuk koḷvar. piṉ peṇṇukku ārātti kāṭṭuvārkaḷ. cila pakutikaḷil mūṉṟāvatu nāḷ kaliyāṇa māṉa peṇkaḷ kūṭiyiruntu cila kiriyaikaḷiṉ piṉ peṇṇaic cuṟṟi niṉṟu kaitaṭṭi āṭuvārkaḷ. (iv vaḻakkaṅkaḷ ippoḻutu perum pālum maṟaintuviṭṭaṉa.)
tirumaṇam
tirumaṇap pēccu maṇamakaṉiṉ tantaiyāl toṭaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. toṭaṅkumuṉ avaṉ iru pakutiyāriṉ cātakaṅkaḷaiyum cōtiṭar mūlam pārttup poruttamirukkiṟatā eṉṟu aṟintu koḷvāṉ. kaliyāṇam niccayamāṉatum maṇamakaṉiṉ tantai, muṉṉāl mēḷam aṭittuc cella cila naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ 7 allatu 9 vāḻaik kulaika ḷuṭaṉum mañcaḷ pūciya tēṅkāykaḷōṭum peṇṇiṉ tantai vīṭṭukkuc cītaṉam niccayippataṟkuc celvāṉ. avarkaḷ oru nipantaṉai allatu paṭṭōlai eḻutikkoṇṭu kaliyāṇattukku nāḷ vaip pārkaḷ. iru pakutiyār vīṭṭu muṟṟaṅkaḷilum pantaliṭappaṭum. ettaṉai pantaṟkālkaḷ naṭavēṇṭumeṉpatil cila camayaṅkaḷil tarkkam eḻuvatuṇṭu. mutaṟ pantaṟkāl vaṭakiḻakkut ticaiyil naṭap paṭum. itu otiya marakkālāka virukkum. naṭummuṉ ataṟkuc cantaṉam, mañcaḷ, kuṅkumam mutaliyaṉa pūci māvilai, taruppaippul mutaliyaṉa kaṭṭappaṭum. naṭuvataṉ muṉ paruttikkoṭṭai kalanta pāl vārttu tēṅkāyuṭaikkappaṭum. pantal veḷḷai kaṭṭi pākkuk kulaikaḷ, teṉṉam pū, ilaikaḷ, kaṇṇāṭi viḷakkukaḷ, kākitap pūkkaḷ, vattit tāḷkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭum. pantaliṉ muṉ vil vaṭivākak kampukaḷ kaṭṭit tōraṇam tūkki vāḻaikaḷ naṭṭu alaṅkarikkappaṭum. pantaliṉ mattiyil kaliyāṇak kāl nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. iḵtu aracāṇikkāl eṉappaṭum. ataṉ pakkattil kiṇṭappaṭṭa ōmakuṇṭam cāṇiyāl meḻukappaṭṭirukkum. itaṉ pakkattil viḷakkukaḷ koḷutti vaikkappaṭṭirukkum. avaṟṟiṉ pakkattil kaḷimaṇṇāl ceyta yāṉaiyiṉ mutukil mūṉṟu allatu ēḻu, niṟam pūciya pāṉaikaḷ oṉṟiṉ mēloṉṟāka aṭukki vaikkappaṭṭirukkum. mēlē irukkum pāṉaiyiṉ vāyil mañcaḷ pūciya tēṅkāy vaikkappaṭum. maṇamakaṉ pakutiyār maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭukkut tāli kūṟaikaḷuṭaṉ celvārkaḷ. celvar pallakkil allatu kutirai mēlēṟip pōvar. vaṇṇāṉ vītiyil nila pāvāṭai virittuc celvāṉ. vēṟu iruvar cāmarai vīcuvar. nālvar mēṟkaṭṭi (mēlāppu) piṭippar. vāttiyakārar vāttiya molippar. iru nāvitar caṅku ūtuvar. nāṭṭiyappeṇkaḷ nāṭṭiya māṭic celvar. pala iḷaiñar tīvaṭṭi, veḷḷaik kuṭai, koṭi, virutu mutaliyaṉa piṭittuc celvar. maṇamakaṉ vantatum maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ ārātti kāṭṭuvar. peṇṇiṉ tāy allatu cakōtari oru kiṇṇattil picainta vāḻaippaḻattaiyum. pālaiyum pantaliluḷḷa pīṭattil vaikkumpaṭi koṭuppar. vīṭṭil maṟaivāka irunta peṇ ippoḻutu pallakkil kōyilukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟapiṉ pantalukku koṇṭu varappaṭuvāḷ. iruvarum kiḻakku nōkkiyirukkumpaṭi aṅkuḷḷa pīṭattil iruttappaṭuvārkaḷ. purōkitar peṇṇiṉ iṭatu kaiyilum maṇamakaṉiṉ valatu kaiyilum kaṅkaṇaṅ kaṭṭiyapiṉ kiriyaikaḷaip purivar. tāliyai maṇamakaṉiṉ cakōtari eṭuttup purōkitariṭam koṭuppāḷ. avar ataṟkut tūpaṅ kāṭṭiyapiṉ capaiyōriṭattil koṭuppār. avarkaḷ ovvoruvarāka atait toṭuvārkaḷ. piṉpu maṇamakaṉ tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. piṉṉāl niṟkum avaṉuṭaiya cakōtari ataṉai naṉṟākak kaṭṭiviṭuvāḷ. piṉpu av viṭattiṟ kūṭiyirukkum kaliyāṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ nellum veṟṟilai yum vaitta nāḻiyāl maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ eṉṉum iruvariṉ talaiyaiyum cuṟṟuvārkaḷ. appoḻutu purōkitar tēṅkā yuṭaippār. piṉ aruntati kāṭṭutal, ammi mitittal mutaliya kiriyaikaḷ naṭaipeṟum.
maraṇam
oruvaṉukku maraṇam aṇukumpōtu purōkitar aḻaikkap paṭukiṟār. avar ciṟitu pañca kauviyattai avaṉ vāyiṉuḷ viṭuvār. piṉpu nōyāḷi pacumāṭṭiṉ vālaip piṭittup purōkitarukku ataṉait tāṉamākak koṭuppāṉ. maraṇamaṭaintatum talai vaṭakkē kiṭakkumpaṭi pirētam kiṭattappaṭukiṟatu. pakkattil niṟpavarkaḷ ‘civā’ eṉṟu mūṉṟu muṟai pirētattiṉ kātil colli neṟṟikkum, neñcukkum tirunīṟu pūcuvar. maraṇac ceyti caṅku ūti ayalavarkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭum. peṇkaḷ cavattaic cuṟṟiyiruntu oppārivait taḻuvar. muṟṟattil nālu kampukaḷ naṭṭu veḷḷaikaṭṭip pantaliṭap paṭum. aṅku piṇam eḷḷeṇṇaiyum, arappum talaiyil vaittu muḻukkāṭṭiyapiṉ pantaliṉ mattiyil kiṭattappaṭum. piṉpu piṇam, pāṭai allatu taṇṭikaiyil vaittut talai muṉpuṟam niṟkat takkatākac cuṭukāṭṭukku eṭuttuc cellappaṭum. cellum vaḻiyil aricippori, paruttivitai, atti ilai, kācu eṉpaṉa eṟiyappaṭum. piṇattai viṟakiṉ mītu kiṭattiyapiṉ vāykkarici pōṭṭuk koḷḷik kuṭamuṭaittut tī mūṭṭappaṭum. īmac caṭaṅkukkuc camukamāyiruntōr ellōrum nīrāṭuvar.
iṟantavaṉiṉ maṉaivi kaimpeṇ eṉappaṭuvāḷ. ivaḷuṭaiya tāli mūṉṟāvatu nāḷ kaḻaṟṟappaṭum.
puttaḷak karaiyār
puttaḷak karaiyārkaḷiṭaiyil mūṉṟu vakaik kaliyāṇaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu mālai maṇam. maṇamakaṉiṉ cakōtari peṇṇaip pūmālai vācaṉaip poruḷkaḷālalaṅkaritta piṉ avaḷ kaḻuttil tāliyaṇivāḷ. kaliyāṇam ceytukoṇṭa iruvarukkum orumittu vāḻap piriyamillāviṭil avarkaḷ pirintu koḷḷalām. pirintukoḷḷa virumpum pakuti kiṭṭa uḷḷa kōyilukku 25 iṟacāl koṭukka vēṇṭum.
iraṇṭāvatu maṇam ciṟutāli. itu yātum kiriyaikaḷillāmal muṉ kūṟiyatu pōlat tāli tarittal. kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷa virumpum pakuti 25 iṟacāl āṟupaṇam kōyilukkut taṇṭam koṭukka vēṇṭum.
mūṉṟāvatu mañcaḷ pūcal. itu tāli kaṭṭāmal maṇañ ceytu koḷvatu. maṇamakaṉiṉ cakōtari peṇṇiṉ uṭaiyil araitta mañcaḷaip pūci viṭuvāḷ. kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷum pakuti āṟu iṟacāl mūṉṟu paṇam taṇṭamākak kaṭṭavēṇṭum.
aṇivakai
āṭavar aṇipavai
oṭṭu - irattiṉam vaikkappaṭṭa poṉ vaḷaiyam. kātil aṇiyappaṭuvatu.
kaṭukkaṉ - kātil aṇiyappaṭuvatu. itil pala vakaikaḷuṇṭu.
ceṭṭik kaṭukkaṉ - 10 mutal 11 aṅkulac cuṟṟaḷavuḷḷatu. poṉ kampikaḷai muṟukkic ceyyappaṭuvatu. naṭuvil irattiṉam vaikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. koḻumpuc ceṭṭikaḷ itil aintu allatu āṟu vaḷaiyaṅ kaḷai ovvoru kātilumaṇivar.
cūṭṭuk kaṭukkaṉ - nīṇṭa vaṭṭamāṉa vaḷaiyam.
arippuccārik kaṭukkaṉ - poṉ pūvarumpukaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa poṉ kampi.
uruttirāṭcak kaṭukkaṉ - uruttirākkam naṭuvil vaikkappaṭṭa kaṭukkaṉ.
pulimukak kaṭukkaṉ - muṉpakkattil pulimukam poṟikkappaṭṭa kaṭukkaṉ.
muruku - kātiṉ mēṟpakutiyil aṇiyappaṭuvatu; kīḻē muttu allatu irattiṉakkal toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruppatu.
carappaḷḷi - poṉ caṅkili.
patakkam - caṅkiliyil kōttuk kaḻuttilaṇiyappaṭuvatu.
araiñāṉ - poṉ allatu veḷḷiyāṟ ceytu araiyilaṇiyappaṭuvatu.
miñci - kāṟperuviralilaṇiyum veḷḷi mōtiram.
kāṟai - itu naṭuvil cantiraṉiṉ vaṭivam toṅkumpaṭiyāka veḷḷi allatu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mālai.
peṇkaḷaṇikaḷ
kuppi - koṇṭaiyil cerukappaṭuvatu; kaṭukkāyaḷavu parumai yuṭaiyatāka irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭatu.
cuṭṭi - mūkkukku nērē neṟṟiyil toṅkumpaṭi talaiyil aṇiyappaṭuvatu.
ciṇukki - caṅkili kōtta taṭṭaiyāṉa poṉ takaṭu; kātaṇiyil māṭṭi koṇṭaiyil koḷuvappaṭuvatu.
piṟaipoḻutu - piṟaiyaiyum cūriyaṉaiyum pōṟ ceyyap paṭṭa iraṇṭu poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷ; oṉṟai oṉṟu pārkkumpaṭi talaiyi laṇiyappaṭuvaṉa.
paṭṭam - neṟṟiyil kaṭṭum poṉ takaṭu.
mūkkutti - mūkkiṉ iṭappakkatti laṇiyappaṭuvatu; muttut toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruppatu.
nattu - mūkkiṉ iraṇṭu tuvāraṅkaḷukku naṭuvil aṇiyap paṭuvatu; muttu allatu irattiṉakkal toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruppatu.
tōṭu - pūviṉ vaṭiviṉatākak kātilaṇiyappaṭuvatu.
tulākku - muttut toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa tōṭu.
koppu - mēṟkātilaṇiyappaṭum taṅka vaḷaiyam.
nākapaṭam - koppukkuk kīḻ aṇiyappaṭuvatu. pāmpiṉ talaiyaip pōl tōṟṟamaḷippatu.
maṇi - pala paṭṭukkaḷil aṇiyappaṭum maṇi kōtta mālai.
cavaṭi - tōḷil poṟuttu niṟkumpaṭi aṇiyappaṭum mālai.
aṭṭikai - irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ patitta kaḻuttaṇi. kaḻuttil oṭṭakkaṭṭappaṭuvatu.
uḷkaṭṭu - kalpatikkāta aṭṭikai.
tāli - kaliyāṇattiṉpōtu kaṇavaṉ poṉ kayiṟṟil allatu mañcaṭ kayiṟṟil kōttu maṉaivi kaḻuttil aṇivatu.
nelli - iraṇṭu poṉ allatu veḷḷik kampikaḷai muṟukkic ceytatu; muḻaṅkaikku mēl aṇiyappaṭuvatu.
kai vaḷaiyalkaḷ - itil kāppu, kaṅkaṇam, vaḷaiyal, cāri. kaṭakam eṉap pala vakaikaḷuṇṭu. ivai veḷḷi allatu poṉṉi ṉāl ceyyappaṭṭu maṇikkaṭṭil aṇiyappaṭuvaṉa. ivai uḷḷē kuḻal uṭaiyaṉavāy oru aṅkulak kuṟukkaḷavuṭaiyaṉavākavirukkum.
oṭṭiyāṇam - poṉ allatu veḷḷiyiṉāṟ ceyta araip paṭṭikai.
taṇṭai - uḷḷē paraliṭappaṭṭa kāppu; veḷḷiyāṟ ceyyappaṭuvatu. kālilaṇiyappaṭuvatu.
cataṅkai - itu taṇṭaikkuk kīḻ aṇiyappaṭum pātacaram.
pāṭakam - kutiraik kaṭivāḷattiṉ mēṟpākam pōṉṟa periya veḷḷic caṅkili. itu kaṇaikkālukku mēl aṇiyappaṭuvatu.
kolucu - muṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu pōṉṟatu.
kāl mōtiraṅkaḷ - ivaṟṟil palavakaikaḷuṇṭu. nakamūṭi, pīli, muṉtāṅki, mayilaṭi, makaramīṉ.
mōtiram - irupālārum irattiṉakkaṟkaḷ patitta veḷḷi allatu taṅka mōtiraṅkaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaiyilaṇivar.
koḻumpuc ceṭṭip peṇkaḷ peṉiccai eṉṉum poṉ koṇṭai ūcikaḷaik koṇṭaiyil kuttik koḷvar. mēṟpakkam poṉ patikkappaṭṭu irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭa ōṭṭu cīppaik koṇṭaiyilaṇivar. avarkaḷ mēṟkātil kappu eṉṉum aṇiyai yum ataṟkuk kīḻ kirāpu eṉṉum aṇikaḷaiyum, urukkumaṇi eṉṉum aintu allatu āṟu vaḷaiyaṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ kīḻ cavaṭik kaṭukkaṉ eṉṉum tōḷvarai toṅkum mūṉṟu periya āparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum aṇivar. avarkaḷ kaḻuttu, kai, pātam eṉpavaṟṟil pala āparaṇaṅ kaḷai aṇivar.

1. It is admitted both by Indian and European scholars that the civilization of the Tamil Nation was, in the main, due to the Aryan Colonists in the South-Tamil studies p.233 - M.Srinivasa Iyangar.M.A., (1914).
itu paḻaiya ārāycci. ik karuttuṭaiyavarkaḷē perumpālum tamiḻar varalāṟṟiṉai eḻuta muyaṉṟu vantārkaḷ.
“When the Aryans entered India they had no script of their own, and their script was the script of their enemies, the Dasus (Dravidians)” - Fr. Heras.
From prehistoric times these Dravids have been the most intellectual, civilized, cultured people of India, famed for arts and architecture, piety and religions with elaborate rites and symbolisms. The last they have left to us in magnificent neolithic cave temples, carved out of rocky mountains, and in the most intractable stone beautiful design, elaborate in rich decoration with grandeur and yet delicacy. The artists are quite unsurpassed by any race in the world. The people are deep thinkers powerful rulers and administrators who never permitted any Aryan interference in Central or Southern India, in its government, religion or occupations, though their upper classes affected a good 
deal of Sanskrit - A short study in the science of comparative religions p. 175
- J.G.R. Forlong.
These last (Early Vedic Hymns) are full of faded decayed, and quite unintelligible words, and forms, and in some points nearer Greek then ordinary Sanskrit - (Maxmuller). So that the Aryan tongue first appeared in India, was not intelligibly put into words of forms. This was a work of several centuries later when civilized Dravidian Pandits wrote out for Aryans in their sacred Devanagari or Dravid character. Necessarily they thought the illiterate colonists how to write their language and reform their faith and mythology. The same process was then going on in the west when Greeks were beginning to write their Aryan tongue the Semito Phoenician characters aided by Egyptians and Syrians they too were organizing an Aryan mythology and faith 
- Ibeid p, 247.
itu naṭunilaimaiyōṭu ceyyappaṭṭa mēṟṟicai aṟiñariṉ ārāyccik karuttu.
1. paḻaṅkaṟkālam: makkaḷ muraṭāṉa kallāyutaṅkaḷaic ceytu payaṉpaṭuttiya kālam.
2. putiya kaṟkālam: makkaḷ aḻuttamāṉa kallāyutaṅkaḷaic ceytu payaṉpaṭuttiya kālam.
3. mēṟku āciya makkaḷum tamiḻ makkaḷum orē makkaṭ kulattaic cērntavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭamaiyālum, iru makkaḷiṉ paṇṭai nākarikamum peritum ottiruntamai
yālum, mēṟku āciya makkaḷē intiya nāṭṭai aṭaintavarkaḷāvar, eṉa ārāycci yāḷar karutuvārāyiṉar. iṉṟaiya ārāycciyil mēṟku āciya makkaḷē intiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷāvar eṉat teḷi vuṟukiṉṟatu. cōḻa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu taikiras, yūpirātas āṟṟiṉ vaṭapāl kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ tam nāṭṭukkuc cāltiyā eṉap peyariṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, ap peyar cōḻatēcam eṉpataṉ tiripākum eṉṟum ē.ci. tās eṉpār tamatu irukku vēta intiyā eṉṉum nūlil naṉku viḷakkip pōntār.
“cintup piratēcattiliruntu namatu nākarikam pāpilōṉiyā ekiptu mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷukkup paraviyatu. avviṭaṅkaḷiliruntu intiyāviṟkup paraviyatu eṉṟa airōppiya ārāycciyāḷariṉ eṇṇam tavaṟeṉṟē karutavēṇṭum. pāpilōṉiyā vil ki.mu. 700il oru tamiḻk kuṭittaṉam iruntatu. teṉṉāṭṭiliruntu tamiḻ nākarikam urōmāpurikkum kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum paraviyatu. iṉṉum takkiṇa pīṭa pūmiyaic cērnta vēṭṭuvar eṉṟa kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ yavaṉa tēcattiṟkuc ceṉṟu taṅkaḷ aracai nilainiṟuttit taṅkaḷ peyaraiyum an nāṭṭiṟkuk koṭuttārkaḷ. ituvē kirīṭ (Crete) tēcam. itaṉ nākarikattiṟkum tamiḻ nākarikattiṟkum oppumai mikutiyāyuḷḷatu. maṇimalarttiraṭṭu - tiru.vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar em.ē. - varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷar palkalaikkaḻakam, ceṉṉai.
The Development of Mohenjo-Daro script, the religion of these two countries and that of Egypt, the titles of kings, the number of zodiacal constellations among the Proto-Indian People and the relative position of these constellations, the changing of the Proto-Indian constellation of the Harp (yal) for Turus (the bull) which might have taken place in Sumar, the tradition of the ancient people of Mesopotamia recorded by Berosus, the parallel by Bible account of Gen. 11 : 1 - 5.
All point to the same conclusion, that the migration of the Mediterranean race commenced from India and extended through south Mesopotamia and North Africa spread through Crete Cyprus, Greece Italy and Spain and across the Prynees reached central Europe and the British Isles. The route starting from Ceylon to Ireland is marked by uninterupted chain of dolmen and other megaliths that seem to be the relics of the enterprising and highly civilized race which is termed the Mediterranean by the anthropologists and which in India, has been quite unreasonably despised under the name Dravidian - Fr. Heras.
1. pāli moḻi, ilaṅkaiyil makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭavillai. āṉāl aṅku atu ilakkiya moḻiyāka iruntuvantatu. pāli, camakkirutac coṟkaḷai ēṟṟukkoṇṭu ilaṅkai makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻi ciṅkaḷamāka māṟiṟṟu. ki.pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil, mutal mutal ciṅkaḷamoḻiyil nūl ceyyappaṭṭatu. ciṅkaḷa moḻiamaipput tamiḻai ottuḷḷatu. itu camakkirutac coṟkaḷ tamiḻuṭaṉ cērntu, ataṉai vēṟupaṭuttiya maikku oru ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
1. The non-Aryan magicians and the Dravidians became Brahmans. South Indian tradition records numerous instances of Brahmans as taking wives from the lower castes. In many cases non-Brahmans were admitted into the Brahmana caste, as sufficient Brahmanas were not available when the reformed Indo-Aryan religion obtained referance. Prehistone ancient and Hindu India P. 38 - R.D. Banerji.

1. By the Pictorial hieroglyphic inscription found and interpreted on the Walls of the temple of the Queen Haslitop at Derel Bahri, we see that this Punt can be no other than India. For many ages the Egyptians traded with their old homes and the reference here made by them to the names of the princes of Punt and its fauna and flora, especially the nomenclature of various precious woods to be found but in India, leaves us scarcely room for the smallest doubt that the old civilization of Egypt is the direct outcome of that of the older India - The Theosophist - March 1881 p. 123 Quoted in “Hindu Superiority” by Har Baeus Sarda.

1. cātikkalappum uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
There was also inevitably at work a process of fusion between the Aryans and the non-Aryans by inter marriage or by alliance - Hindu Civilization - R.K. Mookerji.
The distinction between the Aryan and non-Aryan then grew less sharp while social and political conditions became more complicated. The children of Dasu concubines in the Aryan households and those of Aryan women captured by Dravidian chieftains adopted Aryan customs and religious rites. The fair women of Aryan descent were an irresistible attraction for Dravidian chieftains and racial pride did not prevent Indo-Aryan families from giving a daughter in marriage to a dark skinned neighbour for a sufficient consideration-But as Dravidian society was matriarchal such inter marriages, with or without consent always exerted powerful influence in the Aryanization of India, for in the course of time all the highest Dravidian families both in the north and south claimed Aryan descent on their mothers’ side and adopted Aryan customs and religion - A Short History of India E.B. Havell. - p.p. 1926,

1. ‘That contact between Vedism and the indigenous religion of the Indus valley modified both (a) by making the cult of yoga acceptable to Vedism, which formerly believed only in sacrifice and (b) by the retention of non-vedic elements in popular religion. As to the cult of yoga, its indigeous origin is seen in certain stone statues discovered at Mohenjodaro showing ascetics with eyes half shut in contemplation and fixed on the tip of the nose. These according to Chanda supply the missing links between the prehistoric Indus valley civilization and latter civilization of ‘India’ - Buddhist, Jain or Brahmanical - Memoir No 41 of arch, survey quoted in Hindu India.
2. It is not generally admitted that a great deal of the ancient and medieval myth and legend enshrined in the Sanskrit epics and puranas is of Non-Aryan origin, and that even in vedic mythology certain pre-Aryan elements are present. Puranic myths of the gods and legends of kings, heroes, and sages, in the form in which we find them in the Sanskrit works, represent undoubtedly a considerable amount of modification from other original forms, whether Aryan or non-Aryan. The non-aryan speaking masses of Northern India became Aryanised in language, and their tales and legends were retold as a matter of course in the Aryan language of their adoption.
- Bulletin of the school of oriental studies Vol. VIII (1935 - 1937) S.K. Chatterjee.
1. The Dravidian substratum in Sanskrit has been discovered by several scholars, and Kittel in his Kannada Dictionary has given some 450 Sanskrit words with possible Dravidian connections. A few other words in both Sanskrit and Prakrit and the vernaculars have subsequently been suggested as being of Dravidian origin by other scholars. Sylvain Levi and his pupils, Jean przyluski of Paris, and Prabodh Chandra Bagchi of Calcutta and to some extent the present writer suggested a number of words in the Indo aryan as being Austric (Kol or Munda) origin and affinity - Non-Aryan Elements in Indo aryan S.K. Chatterji.
- Journal of the Greater India Society Vol. 3
The Dravidian names of things and operations connected with all these arts of peace are native and not foreign (not borrowed from Sanskrit) The question has not yet been investigated, but on enquiry it will most probably turn out that many Sanskrit words connected with these arts were borrowed from Dravidian.
- Age of the Mantras P. 15, P.T.S. Iyengar.
1. As regards the material aspects of non-aryan civilization, the Rigveda refers to towns and forts broad and wide, full of kine, of 100 Pillars, built of stone, to autumnal forts as refuge against inundatons and to 100 Cities in a non-Aryan kingdom Even the Vedic God Indra is designated for the occasion as Purandara - sucker of cities - Hindu India - R.K.M. p-30
“Brahmanical legends refer to the strong and wealthy cities on or near the banks of the Indus of which the Aryans took possession after a hard struggle for their adversaries were well armed, possessed horse and chariots, and built castles of stones. Several of the places afterwards celebrated in Indian History, such as Taksha-sela, Mathura and Ujjin were said to have been founded by these non-Aryan people who were probably Dravidian race and perhaps connected with the ancient Sumerians, the people of southern Babylonia whose history has been traced back to the fourth millennium B, C. These Dravidians called by the Aryans Asuras Eaityas, Dasus or Nagas were mostly a maritime and trading people. In the Mahabharata they described as great magicians. They worshipped the sun and the serpent like people of ancient media, with whom, perhaps they had trade connections. The amalgamation was either through military conquest or by peaceful penetration. Aryan and Non-aryan cultures gradually transformed the simple tribal organisatin of Vedic society into powerful political status and finally made ocean instead of the river, the boundary of Aryavartha. - A short History of India p 26 - E.B. Havell.
1. Dravidian place names are sometimes traced to Mesopotamia and Iran, while an ancient language spoken at Mittani reveals striking similarities to modern Dravidian of India - Hindu India p. 38
2. I am however inclined to agree with late Sir Herbert Risely in his view that the Dravidian peoples are autochthons of the Deccan and the South of the Peninsula. Dr Haddon says: The Dravidian may have been always in India as has already been said. H.R. Hail holds the view that the Sumerians came into Western Asia from South India where even their writing may have been invented which in its later developed form in Babylonia was the Cuniform Character in the inscriptions. Bahui only marks a stage in the track of the Dravidians on their way from India to sumer. The Sumerian civilisation is the earliest known in the world and if its Dravidian origin be accepted then a very ancient date for the Dravidian civilization must have to be admitted. The date of Sargon of Akkad, the founder of the dynasty of Akkad has been fixed at 2800 B.C. Now Akkad got her civilization from Sumer allowing for the development of sumerian Civilization. The Dravidian settlement of Sumer may be probably placed at a date not less than 5000 B.C. The bird and serpent Myth - Prof. Kalipada Mitra. - The O.J.O.T. mythic Society Vol XVI.
1. From the recent discovery of the sumerian and Hittite relics in the Indus valley and elsewhere in India it is becoming more and more clear that India never stood isolated from the rest of Asia in the past. but that her Culture and civilization formed not only an integral part of the ancient chaleolithic culture of Asia and Europe but also one of the oldest and the richest treasure house from which all other civilizations derived their largest contributions to which they gave the best of their products in exchange. From the Indus valley one section of people afterwards came to be designated as the Dravidians, marched towards the west both by sea as well as by land, settled down in the province of Sumer in Lower Babylonia under the name of Sumerians and spread Sumero - Dravidian Civilization as far as the Holy land of Palestine and Jerusalem and thence to Europe and Africa.
- Quarterly journal of the mythic society Vol. XIX No. 3

muṉṉurai
mēlnāṭṭavarkaḷ makkaḷ varalāṟṟai oru kalaiyākak koṇṭu ataṉai vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ moḻikaḷil makkaḷ varalāṟṟaip paṟṟi āyirakkaṇakkāṉa nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉarukku varalāṟu eṉpatu putitu. makkaḷ varalāṟukaḷōṭu kaṭavuḷarkaḷait toṭarpu paṭuttik kūṟum paḻaṅkataikaḷ pōṉṟaṉavē uṇmai varalāṟukaḷ eṉa nammavar peritum nampi varukiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷiṉ uḷḷam ivvāṟu paḻakiyiruppatiṉālēyē pēcum paṭak kāṭcikaḷukkum iv vakaik kataikaḷ terintu eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nammavariṉ aṟivu, varalāṟṟut tuṟaiyil mikap piṟpōkkaṭain tiruppatiṉālēyē, mūṭattaṉamāṉa pala paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ nammavarka ḷiṭaiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. mūṭappaḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkuk kāraṇam, avaikaḷiṉ uṇmai aṟiyappaṭātiruttalē yākum. payaṉaṟṟa allatu poruḷaṟṟa paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiṉ toṭakkam allatu kāraṇam aṟiyappaṭiṉ makkaḷ avaikaḷaik kaiviṭap piṉṉaṭaiya māṭ ṭārkaḷ.
namatu nāṭu, tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum nilam. iṅkuk kirāmaṅkaḷ tōṟum, vītikaḷ tōṟum ālayaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vālayaṅkaḷil kurumār cila camakkirutac coṟṟoṭarkaḷai uccarittuk kaṭavuḷ pūcai ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. ac camakkirutac coṟṟoṭarkaḷil teyvīkam allatu kaṭavuḷtaṉmai iruppatākak kaṟṟārum kallātārum nampi varukiṉṟaṉar. camakkirutam eṉpatu namatu nāṭṭu moḻiyaṉṟu. itu cermaṉ, ilāttiṉ, keltu mutaliya airōppiya moḻikaḷukku iṉamuṭaiyatu. iṟṟaikku 4000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ mattiya āciyāvi ṉiṉṟum intiya nāṭṭai aṭainta āriyar eṉṉum aṉṉiya makkaḷ pēciya moḻiyiṉ tiruntiya amaippu. itil kaṭavuḷtaṉmai iruppa tāka nammavar nampivaruvatai varalāṟṟu māṇavaṉ oruvaṉ nōkkiṉ, atu avaṉukku nakaippai uṇṭākkum. camakkirutattil teyvat taṉmai uṇṭu eṉa niṉaikkiṉṟa tamiḻmakkaḷ ellōrum iv vuṇmaiyai aṟivārkaḷāṉāl, avarkaḷē tamatu mūṭattaṉattai niṉaintu nāṇuvārkaḷ allavā?
varalāṟṟuk kalviyiṉāl ituvum itupōṉṟa pala mūṭak koḷkaikaḷum oḻiyum. varalāṟē makkaḷukkuk kaṇ. in nūl nām ellōrum mutalil aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭiya nam muṉṉōriṉ āti varalāṟṟait terivippatākum. iv vārāyccikku ātāram mēlnāṭ ṭaṟiñariṉ ārāycci nūlkaḷē.

tōṟṟuvāy
tamiḻar yār? avarkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam yātu? avarkaḷ intiya nāṭṭil toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷā? allatu piṟa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantu putitākak kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷā? avarkaḷiṉ paḻamai evvakaiyiṉatu? avarkaḷiṉ nākarikam avarkaḷiṭaiyē tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭaintatā? aṉṟēl ataṉaip piṟar avarkaḷukku utaviṉārkaḷā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa kēḷvikaḷukku iṟukkappaṭum viṭaikaḷē tamiḻariṉ paṇṭai varalāṟāka amaiyum. mutalil taṉ nāṭṭiṉaraiyum taṉ iṉattiṉaraiyum paṟṟiya uṇmai varalāṟukaḷai aṟintukoḷḷātu payilappaṭum varalāṟṟuk kalvi payaṉaṟṟatākum. varalāṟṟuk kalviyē makkaḷiṉ pakuttaṟivuk kaṇṇait tiṟakkavallatu.
tamiḻaraiyum, tamiḻaiyum paṟṟiya paḻaiya varalāṟṟu uṇmaikaḷ veḷivarāmaikkup pala taṭaikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkup piṟpaṭṭatu; tamiḻ vaṭamoḻiyaip pārttu amaik kappaṭṭatu; tamiḻar nākarikam āriyarāl tamiḻarukku utavappaṭṭatu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa tavaṟāṉa pala koḷkaikaḷ oru cārār karuttil naṉku patintiruntamaiyēyākum.1 it tavaṟāṉa koḷkaikaḷai mutaṉmutal takartteṟintavar pēraṟiñar. pi. cuntaram piḷḷai avarkaḷāvar. ap perumakaṟku it tamiḻulaku eṉṟum naṉṟi pārāṭṭak kaṭamaippaṭṭatu. ak kālam mutal tamiḻaraiyum, tamiḻaiyum paṟṟiya paḻaiya varalāṟu kaḷai ārāyum ārvam makkaḷiṭaiyē kiḷarvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl tamiḻnāṭṭaip paṟṟiya paṇṭai varalāṟukaḷ ciṟitu ciṟitākap pularvaṉa vāyiṉa. cintu veḷiyil maṟaintu kiṭanta arappā, mokañcatarō eṉṉum paḻaiya intiya makkaḷiṉ nakaraṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya virinta putai poruḷ ārāycci 1931il cēryōṉ mārcal eṉpavarāl veḷi yiṭappaṭṭapōtu, ārāycciyāl muṭivu kāṇap peṟātu aiyattukku iṭamāyirunta tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ paṇṭai varalāṟukaḷ muṭivu kāṇap peṟṟu, veḷiccam eytiṉa. iṉṟu tamiḻariṉ paṇṭai varalāṟu iruḷil irukkavillai. ārāycciyil iv vulaka makkaḷiṉ nākarikat tukku aṭikōliyavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷē eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. ārāycciyāṟ kiṭaitta muṭivukaḷai in nūl naṉku eṭuttu viḷakku kiṉṟatu.
āti makkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ āti varalāṟṟai aṟintukoḷvataṟku, āti makkaḷaip paṟṟiya varalāṟṟu aṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. iṉṟaiya pūkōḷap paṭattil teṟkē atika kaṭalum, vaṭakkē atika nilamum iruppataik kāṇkiṉṟōm. mika mika muṟkālattil pūkōḷap paṭam itaṟku nērmāṟāka iruntatu. oru kālam teṟkē kiṭantu nīruḷ maṟaintupōṉa perunilap parappaippaṟṟi nilanūlārum, purāṇak kataikaḷum, kaṉṉa paramparai varalāṟukaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. teṟkē kiṭantu maṟaintupōṉa nilapparappai mēlnāṭṭup pautika nūlār, lemūriyā eṉa aṟaivar.
aṅku tēvāṅku pōṉṟa uyiriṉam vāḻntatu eṉṉum karuttup paṟṟi lemūriyā eṉṉum peyar iṭappaṭṭatu. lemūr eṉṉum peyar tēvāṅkaik kuṟikkum. lemūriyā eṉṉum nilapparappilēyē āti makkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiṉārkaḷ eṉa hekkal eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟi yuḷḷār. ik karuttu cilakālam ciṟcilarāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭa villai. āṉāl, iṟṟai ñāṉṟai ārāyccikaḷāl, ik koḷkai valiyuṟukiṉṟatu. ec.ci.vels eṉṉum ikkālap pēraṟiñarum mattiya taraikkum teṉ cīṉattukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa nilapparappiṟ ṟōṉṟip perukiya makkaḷē pala cātiyārkaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉat tamatu ulaka varalāṟṟuk kāṭcikaḷ (Outlines of History) eṉṉum nūlakattē kuṟippiṭuvārāyiṉar. lemūriyāk kaṇṭam uyirkaḷ tōṉṟātirunta kālattilēyē maṟaintupōyiṟṟu eṉac cila āciriyarkaḷ koṇṭa karuttu iṉṟu ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭavillai.
iṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭirukkum pala kaṟpaṉaik katai kaḷōṭu piṉṉikkiṭakkum paḻaṅkataikaḷiṉ tokutikaḷ purāṇaṅkaḷ eṉap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar intiya nāṭṭai aṭaintapōtu, intiya nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya paḻaṅkataikaḷai eḻutivaittal iyalpē yākum. mekas tīṉas eṉṉum kirēkkarum, mārkkōppōlō eṉ ṉum ittāliyarum, cīṉa yāttirikarkaḷum intiya nāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷ cilavaṟṟai eḻutivaittamaiyai itaṉōṭu oppiṭalākum. intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkataikaḷākiya purāṇaṅkaḷ, teṟkē periya nilapparap piruntateṉṟum, mēru, pūmikku mattiyiluḷḷateṉṟum, mērumalai yiṉ oru koṭumuṭi ilaṅkait tīvu eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ nūl kaḷum, teṟkē kiṭantu aḻintupōṉa nilap parappaip paṟṟik kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa. ivai mēlnāṭṭu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu mika ot tirukkiṉṟaṉa. teṟkē kiṭantu maṟaintupōṉa nilapparappait tamiḻ makkaḷ nāvalantīvu eṉap peyar iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar.
pūmiyiṉ naṭuvaraiyai aṭutta nāṭukaḷilē, ātiyil makkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiṉārkaḷ eṉa viññāṉikaḷ karutalāṉārkaḷ. mikap paḻaiya maṉita elumpu cāvakat tīviṟ kiṭaittatu. itu viññāṉikaḷ koṇṭirunta karuttukku mika iyaipuṭaiyatākak kāṇappaṭṭatu. itaṉālum piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷālum cāvakat tīvilō, ataṉai aṭut tirunta nilapparappilō, ātimakkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉa viññāṉikaḷ palar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
makkaḷ ōriṭattiṉiṉṟum pala iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṉ ceṉṟārkaḷ?
toṭakkattil makkaḷ vilaṅkukaḷai oppa vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭaikkum kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷai uṇṭu cuṉai nīraip paruki malaikkukaikaḷilum, maraniḻalkaḷilum taṅkivāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ, poruḷ paṇṭaṅkaḷ evaiyum illātavarkaḷāy eṅkum alaintu tirivārāyiṉar. in nilaimaiyil makkaṭ perukkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl ellārukkum ōriṭattil uṇavu kiṭaittal aritāyiṟṟu. ākavē uṇaviṉ poruṭṭup palar tamatu naṭu iṭattai viṭṭu, uṇavu tēṭum poruṭṭuc ciṟitu ciṟitāka akalac ceṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa makkaḷukkut tām muṉṉirunta iṭaṅkaḷukkut tirumpi varum kaṭṭāyam uṇṭākavillai. iv vulakiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil 1paḻaṅkaṟkāla nākarikamuṭaiya makkaḷ vāḻntu vantataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl paḻaṅ kaṟkālattilēyē makkaḷ pirintu ulakiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu taṅkiviṭṭārkaḷ eṉat teri kiṉṟatu. paḻaṅ kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷōṭu kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭa maṉita maṇṭai ōṭukaḷ ellām orē vakaiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, ak kālattil iṉṟu kāṇappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟu niṟattālum uṭaṟ kūṟukaḷālum māṟupaṭṭa makkaṭ kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟavillai eṉak karutappaṭukiṟatu. 2putiya kaṟkālak kallāyutaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭa maṉita maṇṭai ōṭukaḷ vevvēṟu vakaiyāka iruttaliṉ ak kālat tilē makkaṭ kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum. oru makkaṭ kulattiṉar niṟam, uṭaṟkūṟu mutaliyavaikaḷāl māṟupaṭṭa iṉṉoru makkaṭ kulattiṉarāka māṟupaṭuvataṟku 300 talaimuṟaikaḷ varaiyil ākumeṉpatu ārāyccivallār karuttu.
nāvalan tīviṉ aḻivu
pūmiyiṉ naṭukkōṭṭai aṭutta nāṭukaḷil, periya kāṟṟuk kuḻappaṅkaḷum, erimalaik kuḻappaṅkaḷum tōṉṟutal iyalpu. erimalaik kuḻappaṅkaḷāl taraip pākaṅkaḷ kaṭaluḷ maṟaintupōta lum kaṭaluḷ taraikaḷ eḻutalum iyalpu. oru kālattil periya erimalaik kuḻappattiṉāl nāvalan tīvu ciṉṉāpiṉṉamākkappaṭṭatu. intumākkaṭal, pacipik kaṭalkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē tīvuk kūṭṭaṅka ḷāka viḷaṅkuvaṉa nāvalan tīviṉ pakutikaḷē. avai, nāvalan tīvilirunta periya malaikaḷiṉ cikaraṅkaḷ ākalām. nāvalantīvu citaṟuṇṭapōtu aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷil eṇṇillātōr māṇṭaṉar. palar oruvāṟu tappip piḻaittaṉar. ulaka makkaḷ ellōriṭai yum periya kaṭalkōḷ oṉṟaip paṟṟiya paḻaṅkatai vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ovvoru makkaḷiṭaiyum vaḻaṅkum ap paḻaṅkataikaḷ ellām, ciṟcila māṟutalkaḷaiviṭa orēvakaiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av varalāṟu makkaḷ ellōrum oru mattiya iṭattil vāḻntu koṇṭirunta kālattil nērnta periya veḷḷap perukku oṉṟiṉ ñāpakamē eṉṟu naṉku aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉ ṟatu. periya veḷḷap perukkukkut tappip piḻaitta cattiya virataṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ maṉuviliruntu ulaka makkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiṉārkaḷ eṉap purāṇaṅkaḷ navilkiṉṟaṉa. kiṟittuva vētattil ap periya veḷḷap perukkaip paṟṟiya varalāṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. av varalāṟu yūtamakkaḷukkuc cālaṭiya makkaḷ vāyilākak kiṭaittatu. cālaṭiyar cōḻa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu, mēṟku āciyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiya makka ḷāvar.3
nāvalan tīvil nākarikam vaḷarcciyuṟṟirunta teṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu
cintunatip paḷḷattākkukaḷil mokañcatarō, arappā eṉṉum paḻan tamiḻariṉ iru purātaṉa nakaraṅkaḷ aṇmaiyiṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷiṉ kālam iṟṟaikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷ varaiyil. aṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭa muttiraikaḷil ak kāla makkaḷ vaḻaṅ kiya eḻuttukaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iv vakai eḻuttukkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviṉ cila pakutikaḷiṟ kiṭaitta varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷilum ilaṅkaiyil kēkālaip pakutiyi luḷḷa malai oṉṟilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkut tīvukaḷilum pacipik kaṭaṟ ṟīvukaḷilum iv vakai eḻuttukaḷē vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatu av viṭaṅ kaḷiṟ kiṭaitta eḻuttup poṟitta paḻam poruḷkaḷāl naṉku aṟiyap paṭukiṉṟatu. makkaḷiṭaiyē nākarikam mutirntu, moḻi uṇṭāki atu pēccu vaḻakkil nīṇṭakālam nilaviya piṉpē eḻuttukaḷ tōṉṟuvatu iyalpu. nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ intiya nāṭṭai aṭainta āriyar eḻutteḻuta aṟintirukkavillai. āṉāl, nāvalan tīvu makkaḷ eḻutteḻuta aṟintiruntaṉar. nāvalantīvu makkaḷ periya ulaka aḻi viṟku muṉpē nākarika mutircciyuṭaiyavarkaḷāy iruntārkaḷ eṉpa taṟku avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukaḷē cāṉṟākiṉṟaṉa. mokañcata rōviṟ kiṭaitta āyirattu eṇṇūṟu muttiraikaḷiṟ poṟikkap paṭṭa muttiraikaḷai heras pātiriyār naṉku ārāyntuḷḷār. av veḻuttukaḷ cilavaṟṟai avar vācittirukkiṉṟār. mokañcata rōvil vaḻaṅkiya moḻi, tamiḻ eṉa uṟutippaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl nāvalan tīvil vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyum tamiḻēyeṉa uṟutippaṭukiṉṟatu.
kumari nāṭu
nāvalan tīviṉ vaṭakkil arāvali malaikaḷ varaiyum tarai yiruntatu. nāvalan tīvu aḻivuṟṟa kālattil intiyak kuṭānāṭṭait toṭarntu teṟkē periya nilapparappiruntatu. atu kaṉṉiyākumarik kut teṟkē evvaḷavu tūram nīṇṭiruntateṉṟu varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟa iyalātu. paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭuvatai nōkkumiṭattu an nilapparappup periya nīḷamum akalamum uṭaiyatāy iruntateṉa māttiram kūṟalām. kumari nāṭu eṉpatu kumarit teyvattiṉ vaḻipāṭu kāraṇamākat tōṉṟiya peyar. paṇṭaināḷil āḷum aracar allatu vaḻipaṭum kaṭavuḷarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ toṭarpākavē, perumpālum nāṭukaḷukkup peyarkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. iv vulakil ātiyil tōṉṟi yatu peṇ āṭci, ak kālattil āṭavar makaḷirukkuk kīḻppaṭṭirun tārkaḷ. cotturimai peṇkaḷukkē iruntatu. tāyam eṉṉum collukkuc cottut tāyiṭamiruntu piḷḷaikaḷaic cēruvatu eṉṟu poruḷ. iv vaḻakkaip piṉpaṟṟiyē “cītaṉa” vaḻakkum uṇṭā yiṟṟu. āṭavaṉ maṉaivikkuk kīḻppaṭintu naṭappataṟkākavē iv vaḻakkam eḻuntatu. tāyāṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattil, makkaḷ tāyk kaṭavuḷaiyē vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. oru kālattil tirumaṇak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ irukkavillai. ākavē tāy, tirumaṇam peṟātavaḷ allatu kumari eṉappaṭṭāḷ. kumarik kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkattiṟkuriya nāṭātaliṉ atu kumari nāṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
kumari nāṭṭil nāṟpattoṉpatu pirivukaḷ iruntaṉa veṉpatu cilappatikārattiṟku, aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiya uraiyiṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. aṅku, kumari, paḵṟuḷi eṉṉum āṟukaḷum, kumari eṉṉum malaiyum iruntaṉa veṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cilappatikārattiṟ kūṟiyuḷ ḷār. ceṅkōṉ taraiccelavu, eṉṉum nūlāl aṅkirunta maṇimalai, pērāṟu eṉṉum cila malaikaḷ, iṭaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum aṟiya varukiṉṟaṉa. ceṅkōṉ taraiccelavu eṉpatu kumarināṭ ṭiṉkaṇ irunta peruvaḷa nāṭṭaracaṉākiya ceṅkōṉ mītu pāṭap paṭṭa tamiḻ nūl. ataṉ cila pāṭalkaḷē veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. ceṅ kōṉ taraic celavu mikap paḻaiya nūl eṉac cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. mokañcatarō eṉṉumiṭattil vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻiṉ pōkkai nōkku miṭattu, atu mokañcatarō kālattukkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ piṟpaṭṭa nūl eṉpatu naṉku pulappaṭum. ceṅkōṉ taraic celavu eṉṉum nūl, uraiyāciriyarkaḷāl eṭuttāḷappaṭāmaiyiṉ atu piṟkālattavar evarō kaṭṭiya pōlinūl eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. uraiyāciriyarkaḷ āṭcikkut tappiya pala nūṟpeyarkaḷ yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuraiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kumarināṭṭiṉ aḻivum, aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟutalum
kumari nāṭum ciṟitu ciṟitākak kaṭalkōḷuk kuṭpaṭṭatu. ik kaṭal kōḷukkup piḻaitta makkaḷ vaṭakku nōkkic ceṉṟu teṉ ṉintiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. am makkaḷ kaṉṉit teyvattiṉ tiruvuruvait teṟkiṉkaṇ vaittu vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. tām ippo ḻutu kuṭiyēṟiyirukkum nāṭṭai mēlum kaṭal koḷḷātiruttaṟ poruṭṭu at teyvattiṉ tiruvuruvai avarkaḷ aṅku vaittārkaḷ ākalām. intiyāvukku vantu mīṇṭu ceṉṟa urōmar kaṉṉit teyvat tiṉ tiruvuruvam aṅku iruntamaiyai iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
nāvalan tīvu
kumari nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ, tamatu nāṭṭukkuk kaṭalvāyppaṭṭu maṟainta nāvalan tīviṉ peyaraiyē iṭṭārkaḷ. ataṉiṭai iyaṟkaik kuḻappaṅkaḷ yātum nikaḻavillai. makkaḷ amaiti yaṭaintiruntaṉar; tām muṉpu aṟintirunta palvakait toḻil kaḷaip purintaṉar; vēḷāṇmai vāṇikam eṉpaṉa talaiyeṭuttaṉa. nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. avarkaḷ, tammuḷ aṟivum āṟṟalum vīramuñ ceṟinta oruvaṉai aracaṉākat terinteṭuttārkaḷ. avaṉ vāḻnta nakaram teṉ pākattil iruntatu. makkaḷ peruki mēlum vaṭakkē ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. vaṭakkē peruntolaivil makkaḷ nuḻaintu cellamuṭiyāmal ‘taṇṭakam’ eṉṉum neruṅkiya kāṭu taṭuttatu. muṉṉaiyilum ippoḻutu makkaḷ akaṉṟa iṭattiṟ kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntārkaḷ. ap pakutikaḷai āḷvataṟku iṉṉum iraṇṭu aracar terinteṭukkappaṭuvārāyiṉar. ivvāṟu nāvalan tīvil mūvar āṭci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. teṟkē uḷḷa nāṭu, paḻaiya aracaṉukku uriyatāta liṉ, atu paṇṭunāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. paḻamaiyai uṇarttum paṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟē pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum col uṇṭāyiṟṟu. māpāratattiṟ collappaṭum pāṇṭu eṉpavaṉiṉ peyariliruntu pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉuñ col uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. atu peritum tavaṟuṭaittu; pāṇṭuvukku muṉ pāṇṭiyar iruntaṉar. mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkarai, kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷai āṇṭa aracar muṟaiyē cērar, cōḻar eṉappaṭṭārkaḷ. ivvāṟu nāvalan tīvil makkaḷ perukap perukap pala aracāṭcikaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
nāvalan tīviṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu makkaḷ piṟa nāṭukaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟutal
nāvalan tīvil makkaḷ perukat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. oru nāṭṭil makkaḷ atikam perukiṉāl aṅku vāḻkkaikku icaivukaḷ kuṟaivu paṭum. ākavē makkaḷ icaivuṭaṉ vāḻakkūṭiya piṟa nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟuvārkaḷ. teṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil vāḻnta makkaḷ paṟpala kūṭṭat tiṉarāyk kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷait tēṭi veḷiyē ceṉṟaṉar. vaṭakkē vintiya malai varaiyiṟ peruṅkāṭu iruntamaiyāl avarkaḷ tarai vaḻiyē vaṭakku nōkkic cella muṭiyavillai. tamiḻar tiṟamaiyuṭaiya mālumikaḷā yiruntaṉar. ākavē avarkaḷ kaṭal vaḻiyē ēkiṉar.
cintuveḷi makkaḷ
ivvāṟu ceṉṟa oru kūṭṭattiṉar vaṭakkēyuḷḷa cintu nati mukattuvārattai aṭaintaṉar. in nati ōraṅkaḷ makkaḷ kuṭiyēṟi vāḻvataṟ kēṟṟaṉavāy, iruntaṉa. ak kūṭṭattiṉar av vāṟṟōraṅ kaḷil taṅkit tāṉiyaṅkaḷai viḷaivittu iṉitu vāḻntaṉar. makkaḷ perukap peruka avarkaḷ av vāṟṟōraṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟip paravi ṉārkaḷ. ak kāla makkaḷ viyakkattakunta naṉi nākarikam eytiyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷaip paṟṟi 1921ām āṇṭu varaiyil yātum teriyavillai. avarkaḷ amaittu vāḻnta iru periya nakaraṅkaḷ paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷarāṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟkālattaiya makkaḷ, avaikaḷukku mokañcatarō, arappā eṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅki ṉārkaḷ. iv viru nakaraṅkaḷukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa tūram nāṉūṟu mail. āṉāl, iv viru nāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭa nākarikak kuṟikaḷ orē vakaiyiṉa. ataṉāl ak kālac cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ nākarikam mikap paraviyiruntateṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷiṉ nākarikam āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatu. ak kālattil avarkaḷ irumpaippaṟṟi aṟintirukkavillai. avar kaḷiṉ nākarikam kaṟkāla muṭivukkum ulōka kālat toṭakkattukkum iṭaippaṭṭatu. iṟṟaināḷ tamiḻarilum pārkkac cintuveḷit tamiḻar nākarikattiṟ kuṟaintavarkaḷallar. muṟkāla makkaḷ nākarikattālum aṟivālum tāḻntavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷeṉṟum kālañ cellac cella avarkaḷiṉ nākarikamum, aṟivum vaḷarcciyaṭaintaṉa veṉṟum cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. cila vakaikaḷil allāmal, pala vakaikaḷil muṉṉaiya makkaḷē aṟivāṟṟalum maṉavaliyum uṭal valiyum peṟ ṟiruntārkaḷ eṉap paḻaiya varalāṟukaḷai nōkkuṅkāl teḷivāka viḷaṅkum. nākarikam muṉnōkkic celvatu pōlavē, ōr oru kāl piṉnōkkiyum celvatāka aṟiñar ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ekiptiyar kaṭṭi eḻuppiya kūrnutik kōpuraṅkaḷaiyum, cīriya nāṭṭiṉar aṟupatu allatu eḻupatu aṭi uyaramuḷḷa taṉik kaṟṟūṇkaḷai niṟuttik kaṭṭi eḻuppiya “pālpek” ālayattaiyum kaṇṭu iṉṟaiya ulakam viyappaṭaikiṉṟatu. ākavē kālappōkkiṟ piṉnōkkic cellac cella, makkaḷ aṟiyāmai yuṭaiyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ eṉa varalāṟṟu nūlār cilar karututal tavaṟāmeṉka. aṟivum, aṟiyāmaiyum, nākarikamum, anākarikamum ellāk kālaṅkaḷilum uṇṭu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭutal vēṇṭum.
cintuveḷiyilē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa paḻaiya nakaraṅkaḷ uyarnta nakara amaippu muṟaiyaiyum, cuka vāḻkkaik kēṟṟa vitikaḷaiyum taḻuvi amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. an nakarkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē akaṉṟa neṭiya vītikaḷ celkiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷaic ciṟiya pala vītikaḷ kuṟukkē kaṭantu celkiṉṟaṉa. vītikaḷiṉ iru maruṅkukaḷilum vīṭukaḷ niraiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷukku oṉṟu mutal mūṉṟu māṭikaḷ varaiyil uṇṭu. kaḷi maṇṇiṉāl kal aṟuttuk kāyaviṭṭuc cūḷai vaikkappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭ ṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷukku uḷ muṟṟamum, uḷ muṟṟattiṟ kiṇaṟum iruntaṉa. uḷ muṟṟattil muḷaikaḷ aṭittu āṭumāṭukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷuk kuk kuḷikkum aṟai iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntatu. kaḻivunīr ceṅkaṟ patitta vāykkāl vaḻiyē ceṉṟu vītiyiluḷḷa periya vāykkālil viḻun tatu. vītiyiluḷḷa periya vāykkālum ceṅkal patikkappaṭṭiruntatu. vīṭukaḷukkup pala cāḷaraṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. māṭiyiṉiṉṟum nīr kīḻē celvataṟkuc cūḷaiyiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ kuḻāykaḷ porutti vaikkappaṭ ṭaṉa. camaippataṟkum nittirai koḷvataṟkum taṉiyaṟaikaḷ iruntaṉa. makkaḷ palvakaik kaittoḻilkaḷ purivatiṟ ṟiṟamai yaṭaintiruntaṉar. taccar, aḻakiya kaṭṭil, nāṟkāli, mukkāli mutaliya vīṭṭup puḻakkattuk kuriya poruḷkaḷaic ceytārkaḷ. poṟkollar mātar virumpi aṇiyum aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷaip poṉ, veḷḷi, veṇkalam mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāṟ ceytu aḷittaṉar. kuyavar aḻakiya pāṉai, caṭṭikaḷait taṇṭu cakkaraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ am maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṟkup palavakai niṟaṅkaḷ tīṭṭi, avaimītu palavakai ōviyaṅkaḷai eḻutiṉar. muṟkāla maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭi vaṅkaḷ ik kālam mēlnāṭukaḷiliruntu varum kaṇṇāṭip poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟu aḻakuṭaiyaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. ciṟuvar palavakai viḷai yāṭṭup poruḷkaḷai vaittu viḷaiyāṭi ṉārkaḷ. avai ūtukuḻal, kilukiluppai, maṇṇāṟ ceytu cūḷaiyiṭṭa paṟavai, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa.
makaḷir aḻakiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintu, neṟṟiyiṟ ceṅ kāvip poṭṭu iṭṭārkaḷ. mukattukku oru vakai veṇpoṭi pūciṉārkaḷ. mēṉi alaṅkarippukkuriya poruḷkaḷai muttuccippiyiṉ ōṭu patittuc ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya cimiḻkaḷil vaittiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tamatu kūntalaip palavakaiyākak kōti muṭintaṉar. avarkaḷ palavakai vēlaippāṭuṭaiyaṉavum kaṇkavarum niṟam ūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa vumākiya aḻakiya āṭaikaḷai uṭuttaṉar.
kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya kōvilkaḷ iruntaṉa. kōvil kaḷukku arukē uḷḷa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷuṭaiya kēṇikaḷil kāl kaikaḷaic cuttañ ceytapiṉ allatu nīrāṭiyapiṉ vaḻipaṭuvōr ālayaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. āṭavar mukattai maḻittuk koṇṭaṉar. maḻikkum kattikaḷ veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu ak kāla makkaḷ, uyarnta nākarikamum, ullāca vāḻkkaiyum uṭaiyavar kaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. itu iṟṟaikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻariṉ nākarika nilai.
ekiptiyar
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟa iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉar ceṅ kaṭal vaḻiyāka āppirikkāvai aṭaintaṉar. aṅku eṉṟum vaṟṟāta nīrpperukkuṭaiya nail eṉṉum āṟu uṇṭu. ataṉ pakkaṅkaḷiluḷḷa nilam tāṉiyaṅkaḷai viḷaivippataṟku ēṟṟa ceḻip puṭaiyatu. av viṭattē avarkaḷ taṅki vāḻntaṉar; makkaḷ perukiṉar; nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. avarkaḷā lamaikkappaṭṭa periya nakaraṅkaḷ mempis, tīps eṉpaṉa. am makkaḷiṉ paḻaiya nākarikak kuṟikaḷ iṉṟum av viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷar av viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta paḻam poruḷkaḷai ārāyntuḷḷārkaḷ. ekipti yariṉ paḻaṅkataikaḷ, avarkaḷ paṇṭu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟavarkaḷākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭu nāṭu eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭē eṉa varalāṟṟāciri yarkaḷ ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. makkaṭ kula nūlār, tamiḻarum ekiptiyarum orē kula muṟaiyil vantōr eṉak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. paḻaiya ekiptiyaruṭaiya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟai mutaliyaṉa tamiḻ makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ ālayaṅkaḷil iliṅkaṅkaḷai vaittu vaṇaṅkiṉar. nail āṟṟukku mēṟkil civaṉ eṉṉum pālai nilap pacuntarai oṉṟu uḷḷatu. aṅku avarkaḷ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷai, civaṉeṉṉum peyariṭṭu vaḻi paṭṭaṉar.
ekiptiyak kurumār pakalil iru muṟaiyum, iravil iru muṟaiyum nīrāṭiṉārkaḷ; tōyttularttiya āṭaiyai uṭuttārkaḷ; kālil mitiyaṭi tarittārkaḷ. makkaḷ tām parukum kiṇṇaṅkaḷait tiṉamum kālaiyil cuttañ ceytārkaḷ; āṇṭil orumuṟai ālayaṅkaḷilum vīṭukaḷilum tīpaṅkaḷ ēṟṟit tīpa viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉārkaḷ. ekiptiṟ kiṭaitta paḻaiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ intiyāviṟ kiṭaitta av vakaip poruḷkaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. iṟanta aracariṉ uṭalkaḷai aṭakkañ ceytaṟku avarkaḷ kaṭṭi eḻuppiya kūrnutik kōpuraṅkaḷ iṉṟum ulaka viyappukaḷuḷ oṉṟāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīla natiyil vaḷarum nāṇaṟ pullai veṭṭip piḷantu oṭṭiya tāḷkaḷilē avarkaḷ kucciyil mai toṭṭu eḻutiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutuvataṟku ōviyaṅ kaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. tamiḻariṉ paḻaiya eḻuttukaḷ iv vakaiyiṉa eṉpatu paḻaiya cāṉṟukaḷāl aṟiyavarukiṉṟaṉa.
cumēriyar
tamiḻaril iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉar pāracīkak kuṭākkaṭal vaḻiyē ceṉṟu taikiras yūpiratis āṟukaḷiṉ kīḻ ōraṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ āṟṟiṉiṉṟum nīraik kālvāykaḷāl vayalkaḷukkup pāycci vēḷāṇmai ceytaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ talainaka ram cucā. cumēriyāvilē ōr iṭattukkum, oru malaikkum ellam eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. el eṉpatu ñāyiṟṟaik kuṟikkum paḻan tamiḻc col. intiyāviṉ teṉ kōṭiyiluḷḷa ilaṅkait tīviṉ paḻaiya peyarum ellam. ellam eṉpatē piṟkālaṅkaḷil īḻam eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. īḻam eṉpataṉ aṭi el eṉa moḻinūlār ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ellam eṉṉum iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ oṟṟumaiyāl cumēriyar orupōtu ilaṅkait tīviṉiṉṟum ceṉṟa makkaḷ āvārkaḷō? eṉpatu ārāyattakkatākiṉṟatu. muṟkālattu ilaṅkaiyum, teṉṉintiyāvum pirintirukkavillai; nilattāl iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
cumēriyariṉ nākarikam, ekiptiyaruṭaiyavum cintuveḷit tamiḻar kaḷuṭaiyavum nākarikaṅkaḷai otta paḻamaiyuṭaiyatu. ōṭakkōṉ, uvaṇṇā eṉṉum talaivarkaḷiṉ kīḻ ceṉṟu pāracīkattiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷē cumēriyar eṉa avarkaḷiṭaiyē paḻaiya varalāṟu uṇṭu. ōṭakkōṉ eṉṉum col ōṭaṅkaḷukkut talaivaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ tarum tamiḻp peyar. uvaṇṇā eṉṉum peyar, tuḷuva makkaḷiṭaiyē iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṟkup pūviṉ aṇṇaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. cumēriyar, tantaik kaṭavuḷai āṇ eṉṟum, tāyk kaṭavuḷai ammā eṉṟum peyarkaḷ iṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
cintuveḷit tamiḻariṉ tantaik kaṭavuḷ āṇ; tāykkaṭavuḷ ammaṉ. cumēriyāvilē kiṭaitta muttiraikaḷil cilavaṟṟil imil uḷḷa iṭapam poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. imiluḷḷa iṭapaṅkaḷ intiyāvil māttiram kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. makkaṭ kulanūlār tamiḻarum cumēriyarum orē kulamuṟaiyiluḷḷōr eṉa ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. varalāṟṟu āciri yarkaḷiṟ palar, ivarkaḷ intiyāviṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu pāracīkattiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷeṉa eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉiyar
cumēriyāvukku vaṭakkē kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta tamiḻar akkattiyar (Accadians) eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ic col orupōtu antap pakkattil kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷavarkaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil (ap pakkattār) vaḻaṅki yirukkalām. piṉṉar av viṭaṅkaḷil cōḻanāṭṭiṉiṉṟum veḷip pōnta oru kūṭṭattiṉar ceṉṟu taṅki vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ an nāṭṭukkuc cōḻatēcam eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. cōḻatēcam eṉpatē kaltēyā (Chaldia) eṉat tirintu piṟkālattu vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. kaltēyāviṉ talainakar ūr. oru kālattil kaltēyā, cumēriyā eṉṉum irunāṭukaḷum, oruṅkē iṇaikkappaṭṭup pāpilōṉiyā eṉṉum peyar peṟṟaṉa. pāpilōṉ nāṭu, intiya makkaḷāl, pavēru eṉappaṭṭatu. intiya vaṇikar pavēruvukkuk kaṭalvaḻiyākac ceṉṟa varalāṟukaḷ pavēru cātakam (ki.mu. 500) eṉṉum putta nūliṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. pāpilōṉiyar periya kōyilamaittu, aṅkē pakaṟkaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. pakaṟ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṉ kāraṇamākavē avarkaḷ nakarukkup pāpilōṉ eṉṉum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu. avarkaḷ iṭapa vākaṉattiṉ mītu muttalai vēlaip piṭittu niṟkum tantaik kaṭavuḷai yum, ciṅka ūrti mēlirukkum tāyk kaṭavuḷaiyum vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. ki.mu. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāpilōṉil kaṭai vaittu vāṇikam paṇṇiya va.rā. eṉpavariṉ kaṭaikkaṇakkukaḷ eḻutiya kaḷimaṇ ēṭu aṅkuk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.
acīriyar
pāpilōṉukku vaṭakkē kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta tamiḻar acīriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. acīriyā, cīriyā mutaliya peyarkaḷ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu kāraṇamāka cūriyaṉ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantaṉa eṉṟu vaṭal (Waddell) eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟuvar. cūriyaṉ vaṭacol eṉap palar karutuvar. cūrara makaḷir, cūr mā mutal eṉap paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil cūr eṉṉum col payiṉṟu varutalaik kāṇunar cūriyaṉ āriyac col lākumōveṉa ārāyaṟpālar. acīriyar ñāyiṟ ṟuk kaṭavuḷai acur eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. acīriyariṉ paḻaiya kaṭṭaṭa amaippukaḷ, tirāviṭa nāṭṭuk kaṭṭaṭa amaippu kaḷai mika ottirukkiṉṟamaiyiṉ, iv viru makkaḷum oru potu uṟpattiyaic cārntōrāvar eṉap paḻañ carittira ārāycciyāḷar kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. im makkaḷiṉ nākarikam, kaṭavuḷ koḷkai mutaliyaṉa peritum paḻantamiḻariṭaiyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa pōlvaṉa.
yūtar
yūtar tamiḻnāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu mattiyataraik kaṭaliṉ mēṟkuk karaiyōraṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta makkaḷāvar. kaltēyāviṉ talainakarākiya ūriṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu pālattīṉa nāṭṭiṟ ṟaṅkiya apirakām eṉṉum kaltēyariṉ cantatiyiṉarē yūtarum arēpiyarum eṉṉum kaṉṉa paramparai varalāṟu uḷatu. kaltēyar tamiḻarkaḷē eṉpatu muṉ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pālattīṉa makkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ, paḻantamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷai mika ottaṉa. avarkaḷ maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ civaliṅkaṅkaḷai vaittu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. āṉ kaṉṟum avarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. civaṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷai avarkaḷ ciyaṉ (Cian) eṉṉum peyar koṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ: iṟappup piṟappāl uṇṭākum tīṭṭuk kāttaṉar. pūppaṭainta peṇkaḷ ēḻu nāḷ varaiyum taṉittu uṟaintārkaḷ. iṟantavarkaḷ poruṭṭu avarkaḷ muppatu nāḷ varaiyum tukkam koṇṭāṭiṉar. oruvar iṟantāl, avar iṟanta iṭattil ēḻu nāṭkaḷukku viḷakkuk koḷutti vaittu uṇavu paṭaittārkaḷ. iṟantavariṉ maraṇatiṉam, āṇṭil orumuṟai koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu. appoḻutu viḷakkuk koḷutti vaittu, uṇavu paṭaittu viruntu koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu. iṉṉum ivai pōṉṟa yūtariṉ paḻaiya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭu vaṉa pōṉṟaṉa.
piṉīciyar
mattiyataraik kaṭaliṉ mēṟkuk karaiyilē pālattīṉattukku mēṟ pakkattē kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta tamiḻar piṉīciyar eṉappaṭṭārkaḷ. ivar kaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya nāṭṭil īntu eṉṉum paṉaikaḷ atikam. ātaliṉālē avarkaḷ nāṭu, paṉaināṭu eṉappaṭṭa teṉṟum, paṉaināṭu eṉpatē uccarippu vēṟupāṭṭāl piṉīciyā āyiṟṟeṉṟum varalāṟṟu āciri yarkaḷ cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. piṉīciyar paratar eṉavum paṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ mokañcatarō kālattukkup piṉ, mēṟku āciyāviṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ ākalām. ivarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttup pirāmi eḻuttōṭu oṟṟumai yuṭaiyatu. iv viru eḻuttukaḷiṉ neruṅkiya oṟṟumai kāraṇamākap piṉīciya eḻuttukaḷiṉiṉṟē pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ oru kālattiṟ karutiṉar. pirāmi eḻuttu, mokañcatarō eḻuttiṉ tiripu. intiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭuc ceṉṟu mēṟku āciyāviṟ ṟaṅkiya piṉīciyar, pirāmi eḻuttukaḷaiyē vaḻaṅkiṉar. piṉīciyariṭamiruntu kirēkkarum, kirēkkariṭamiruntu urōmarum, urōmariṭamiruntu maṟṟaiya airōppiya cātiyiṉarum eḻutteḻutum muṟaiyai aṟintārkaḷ.
piṉīciya makkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum, kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷum tamiḻ makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa pōlallāmal vēṟu vakaiyāka iruttal muṭiyātu. ivarkaḷ periya ālayaṅkaḷ amaittu avaikaḷil civaliṅkaṅkaḷai vaittu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. iṭapamum ivar kaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭṭukku uriyatā yiruntatu. ivarkaḷiṉ talainakarākiya taiyarp paṭṭiṉattil amaikkappaṭṭirunta pakalavaṉ kōyilil marakatattiṉālum, poṉṉiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭa iraṇṭu civaliṅ kaṅkaḷ niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu heratotacu āciriyar kūṟi yuḷḷār. āṟṟōraṅkaḷil taṅkiya makkaḷaip pōla ivarkaḷ tamatu uṇaviṉporuṭṭu vēḷāṇmaiyiṟ ṟaṅkiyiruntārallar; ulakiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷukku marakkalaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu vāṇikam purintaṉar.
cīriyar
piṉīciyāvukku vaṭakkē kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta tamiḻar cīriyar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. cūriyar eṉpatē cīriyar eṉṟu māṟiṟṟeṉa vaṭel eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. im makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷum, kaṭavuṭ koḷkaikaḷum paḻantamiḻariṭaiyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa pōṉṟaṉa. cīriyāvilē pālpek eṉṉum iṭattil aṟupatu allatu eḻupatu aṭi uyaramuḷḷa taṉik kaṟṟūṇkaḷai niṟuttik kaṭṭappaṭṭa periya pakalavaṉ kōyiliṉ aḻipāṭu iṉṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ekiptiya camātikaḷaip pōla, iv vālayamum viyappaḷippatā yirukkiṉṟatu.
im makkaḷ, muttalai vēlai oru kaiyilum, maḻuvai maṟṟoru kaiyilum piṭittu iṭapa vākaṉattiṉ mītu vīṟṟirukkum tantaik kaṭavuḷaiyum, oru kaiyiṟ kēṭakattaiyum maṟṟa kaiyiṟ ṟaṇṭaiyum ēntic ciṅka vākaṉattiṉ mītirukkum tāyk kaṭavuḷaiyum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷarkaḷākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
ciṉṉa āciya makkaḷ
