piḷḷaip pēṟṟukkup piṉ 3, 5, 9vatu nāṭkaḷil peṇ kuḷittu maṇṇātti koṭukkum māṟṟai uṭuppāḷ. irupatteṭṭāvatu nāḷ piḷḷaikkup peyar iṭappaṭum. piṉpu piḷḷaikku irumpuk kāppukaḷ iṭappaṭum. cōṟu ūṭṭum caṭaṅku āṟāvatu mātam naṭaipeṟu kiṉṟatu. appoḻutu irumpuk kāppukaḷ kaḻaṟṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟukkup patil veḷḷi allatu taṅkak kāppu iṭappaṭum. ēḻu āṇṭukaḷ niṟaivataṟku muṉ piḷḷaikkuk kātu kuttappaṭukiṟatu. iṟanta varkaḷiṉ uṭal cuṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. cuṭalaiyil koḷḷik kuṭam uṭait tal mutaliya kiriyaikaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭāvatu nāḷ piṇṭam vaikkappaṭum. aintāvatu nāḷ cāmpal aḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷukkup patiṉaintu nāṭkaḷ tīṭṭu uṇṭu.
urōṇā: malaikaḷil vāḻum oriya uḻavar toḻil cey vōr ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷuḷ vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷvar. vāḻviḻanta peṇkaḷ perumpālum kaṇavaṉiṉ iḷaiya cakōtaraṉai maṇappar. ivarkaḷiṉ cātit talaivaṉ pātō, nāykkō eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ik kūṭṭattiṉaruḷ uṇṭākum piṇakkukaḷaik cātit talaivaṉ tīrttu vaippāṉ. āṇkaḷ pūṇūlaṇivar. ivarkaḷ vaḻipaṭum kirāma teyvaṅkaḷ takuṟāṇi eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ cātip paṭṭap peyar nāykkō.
eṭṭarai: ivarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭut taṭṭāriloru piriviṉar.
empirāṉ: itu malaiyāḷattiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya tuḷuvap pirāma ṇarukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
eravāḷar: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattilē kāṭukaḷiluḷḷa pati eṉṉuṅ kirāmaṅkaḷil kāṇiyāḷariṉ kīḻ kūli vēlai ceyyum makkaḷ. ivarkaḷil peṇkaḷ talaippūppu eytiṉāl taṉiyē iṭappaṭṭa koṭṭilil ēḻu nāṭkaḷ viṭappaṭuvārkaḷ. ēḻāvatu nāḷ nīrāṭiya piṉpē avarkaḷ kuṭicaikaḷuḷ nuḻaivārkaḷ. eravāḷar veṟṟilai yum pākkum vaittu uyarntavarkaḷukkut tirumaṇattai aṟivippar. uyarntōr avarkaḷiṉ tirumaṇac celavukku vēṇṭiya nel mutaliyaṉa koṭuppar. ivarkaḷ pēy, picācukaḷiruppatai nampuvārkaḷ. mantiravāti pēy piṭittavarkaḷiṭamiruntu pēyai ōṭṭuvāṉ. avarkaḷ mantiravāti paṉai ōlaiyil eḻutik koṭukkum iyantiraṅkaḷai nūlāṟ cuṟṟik kaḻuttil aṇivar. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya teyvaṅkaḷ ēḻu kaṉṉip peṇkaḷum, kaṟuppaṉum.
eḻuttaccaṉ: eḻuttaccaṉ eṉpataṟkup paṇṭitaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. ip peyar malaiyāḷap paḷḷikkūṭa āciriyarukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
hekkāṭi (Heggadi): kaṉṉaṭa iṭaiyarum uḻavarum ip peyar peṟuvar.
ērāṭi: itu iṭaiyaṉaik kuṟikkum peyar. ēr nāṭṭai āṇṭa nāyarc cātiyāriṉ peyar. ēr nāṭu eṉpatu erutunāṭu eṉpataṉ tiripu.
ērāḷaṉ: itu ceṟumāṉ cātiyiṉ oru pirivu. ceṟu - vayal.
ēṉāti: itu mutaṉmaiyuṭaiya ampaṭṭaṉ allatu mantiri yaik kuṟikkum. cāṇārukkum ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. ēṉāti nāyaṉār mūlam tamakku ip peyar vantateṉa avar kūṟuvar.
okkiliyaṉ: payirt toḻil ceyyum kaṉṉaṭat toḻilāḷar okkiliyar eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷiṉ cātit talaivaṉ paṭṭakkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. paruvamaṭaiyāta okkiliyac ciṟuvar paruvamaṭainta peṇṉai maṇakka nērntāl kaṇavaṉiṉ kaṭamaikaḷai avaṉ tantai niṟaivēṟṟuvāṉ. viyapicārak kuṟṟattuk kuṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ oru kūṭai maṇṇait talaiyil vaittuk kirāmattaic cuṟṟi varutal vēṇṭum. appoḻutu ciṉṉap paṭṭakkāraṉ piṉṉāl niṉṟu avarkaḷaip puḷiyam milāṟukaḷāl viḷācuvāṉ. peṇkaḷukku mutaṟpiḷḷai piṟanta piṉ cītaṉam koṭukkappaṭum. iṟantavariṉ piṇattai eṭuttuc cellumpōtu paḻam, kācu, cōṟu mutaliyaṉa eṟiyappaṭum. iṟantava ṉiṉ maṉaivi cuṭalaikkuc ceṉṟu taṉatu kaivaḷaikaḷai uṭait teṟivāḷ. ivarkaḷukku maraṇat tīṭṭu patiṉeṭṭu nāṭkaḷukkuṇṭu.
oṭṭiyar allatu oṭṭar: ivarkaḷ kiṇaṟu tōṇṭutal, kuḷaṅkaḷukku aṇaikaṭṭutal pōṉṟa vēlaikaḷ ceyvar. oricā mākāṇattiṉiṉṟum vanta kāraṇattiṉāl ivarkaḷ oṭṭar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ tirumaṇak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ nukaivuṭaiyavai. peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum virumpiṉāl maṇat toṭarpai nīkkiviṭa lām. iru pāliṉarum patiṉeṭṭu muṟaikku atikam tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷutal kūṭāteṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭuṇṭu.
osṭā: tiruvitāṅkūril vāḻum makamatiyariṉ ampaṭṭar osṭā eṉappaṭuvar.
orunūl: vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyyāta maṟavar vakuppil oru piriviṉar ip peyar peṟuvar.
ōccar: ivarkaḷ piṭāri kōyilukkup pūcai ceyyum kulattiṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ koṭi uṭukkai. ceṅkaṟpaṭṭup pakutikaḷil ōccar tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷai āṭṭum naṭṭuvat toḻil purivar. ōccaṉ eṉṉum col ōcai eṉṉumaṭiyākap piṟantatu. ataṟku mēḷamaṭittut tutip pāṭalkaḷ pāṭuvōṉ eṉpatu poruḷ.
ōṭaṭṭu: malaiyāḷattil kōyilkaḷukkum pirāmaṇariṉ vīṭukaḷukkum ceṅkal ceyyum nāyar vakuppiṉar ip peyar peṟuvar.
ōtuvār: ivarkaḷ paṇṭāraṅkaḷuḷ oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ tēvāram tiruvācakam mutaliya pāṭalkaḷaik kōyilkaḷil paṇ ṇōṭu pāṭuvar.
hōliyar: ip peyar paṟaiyaṉ, pulaiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar kaḷukku nērāṉatu. ivarkaḷ teṉ kaṉṉaṭattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
kaṅkēyar: ivarkaḷ tiruvitāṅkūril vāḻum iṭaiyaril oru piriviṉar.
kañcakārar: ivarkaḷ cempu, pittaḷai, veṇkalam mutaliyavai kaḷil vēlai ceyvōr.
kaṭacaṉ: tirunelvēlip pakkaṅkaḷil cūḷai iṭuvōr ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷuḷ nāṭṭaracaṉ, paṭṭaṅkaṭṭi eṉa iru piriviṉaruṇṭu. ivarkaḷ vēṭṭuvarilum pārkka uyarntōr. ivarkaḷ kōyilkaḷuḷ nuḻaital kūṭātu. ivarkaḷukkut taṉi ampaṭṭaṉum vaṇṇāṉumuṇṭu.
kaṭaiyar: paḷḷaril oru piriviṉar kaṭaiyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ irāmēcuvarattilē cippi ōṭukaḷaic cūḷai vaittuc cuṇṇāmpu ceykiṟārkaḷ. ik kūṭṭattiṉariliruntu muttukuḷippa taṟku āṭkaḷ terinteṭukkappaṭuvārkaḷ. maturai tirunelvēlik kōṭṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ kiṟittuva matattait taḻuviyuḷḷārkaḷ. paravarai oppa ivarkaḷum pirāṉcis cēviyarāl matamāṟṟam ceyyap paṭṭavarkaḷāvar.
kaṇakkaṉ: itu kaṇakku eṉṉum collaṭiyākap piṟanta peyar. ivarkaḷ aracarāl kirāmak kaṇakkarkaḷāka niyamikkappaṭṭavarka ḷāvar. karṇam allatu kaṇakkaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷukkup paṭṭappeyar vēḷāṉ. karṇaṅ kaḷuḷ kai kāṭṭik karṇam eṉṉum oru pirivu uṇṭu. ivarkaḷuḷ marumakaḷ māmiyuṭaṉ (kaṇavaṉiṉ tāy) pēca aṉumatikkappaṭuvatillai. āṉāl, kaikāṭṭi (camikkaiyāl) pēcuvar. peṇṇiṉ tāy peṇ ṇukkut tālikaṭṭuvaḷ.
kaṇiyaṉ: ip peyar kaṇi eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. paḻaiya āvaṇaṅ kaḷil kaṇi eṉṉum col kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṇikaṉ, kaṇi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ paṇikkaṉaiyum aracaṉaiyum kuṟikkum. paṇikkaṉ eṉṉum col paṇi (vēlai) eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. paṇikkaṉukku irāṇuvat toḻil payiṟṟum vēlai uriyatu eṉṟu kēraḷa uṟpatti eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. vaṭakkē kaṇikaṉ nampik kuruppu eṉavum paṭuvāṉ. kaṇiyaril kaṇiyar, tīṇṭar eṉa iru piriviṉaruṇṭu. mutaṟ piṉiviṉar cōtiṭam colvōr. iraṇṭāvatu vakuppiṉar kuṭai cey vōrum pēyōṭṭuvōru māvar. kaṇiyaṉ muṭṭiṉāl kaḷarippaṇik kaṉukkut tīṭṭu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. kaḷarip paṇikkar muṟkālattil pōrp payiṟci aḷikkum āciriyarāka viruntaṉar. piṭiccukaḷi, vāṭcilampam, pāricātam kaḷi, kōlaṭi mutaliya āṭalkaḷ iṉṟum kaḷarikaḷil naṭai peṟukiṉṟaṉa. putu varuṭappiṟappukkup piṉ malaiyāḷattil naṭai peṟum cāl (uḻavu) kiriyaiyil ovvoruvarum avvak kirāmattuk kuriya kaṇikaṉiṭam āṇṭup palaṉaik kēṭṭaṟinta piṉ avaṉukkuc ciṟitu poruḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu marapu. palaṉ paṉai ōlaiyil eḻutikkoṭuk kappaṭum. itu viṭṭuṇupalaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. pāḻūri lirukkum kaṇi mikap pukaḻ peṟṟavaṉ. kaṇikaḷuḷ peṇ pūppaṭaiyu muṉ tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇamum piṉ campantamum naṭaipeṟum. avarkaḷuṭ palar pāṇṭavaraippōla orē maṉaivi uṭaiyavarāka viruppar. vitavai maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷvāḷ.
paṇikkaruḷḷum kaṇikaruḷḷum peṇkaḷ orē kālattil pala kaṇavarai maṇakkum vaḻakkam uṇṭu. kirāmattiṉ nāyarttalaivaṉ kaḷarimūppaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. kaṇikaḷil potuvaṉ allatu kaṇika kuruppukkaḷ ampaṭṭarāvar. ivarkaḷ piṇattōṭu iṭukāṭṭukkuc celvārkaḷ. potuvar tīṇṭāk kaṇikaḷukku mayirviṉai ceyyār. kaṇiyar kōvilkaḷil nuḻaiyak kūṭātu. ivarkaḷ irupattu nāṉkaṭi tūrattil vantāl pirāmaṇarukkut tīṭṭu uṇṭākiṟatu.
kaṇkeṭṭu: ivarkaḷ māṭāṭṭikaḷ; teluṅku pēcuvar; vaiṇava matattiṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ kuru vaiṇava aṭaiyāḷamākiya caṅku cakkaram mutaliyavaikaḷai ivarkaḷ tōḷkaḷmītu cuṭuvar. perumāḷ māṭṭukkāraṉ allatu perumāḷ erutukkāraṉ eṉṉum māṭāṭ ṭikaḷ ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, vaṭa ārkkāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar.
kattiri: paṭṭu necavu ceyvōr kattirikaḷāvar. paṭṭil vēlai ceyvatāl ivarkaḷ paṭṭunūṟkārar eṉavum aṟiyappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ tamatu vamicam kātta vīriya aruccuṉaṉiliruntu varuvatāka kūṟuvar. peṇkaḷ maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷvar. ivarkaḷ tāymāmaṉ piḷḷaiyai maṇappatillai. peṇkaḷ pūppu aṭaiyumuṉ maṇam muṭikkappaṭuvar. maṇamākum pōtu āṇkaḷ pūṇūl tarippar.
kapērar: ivarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭa mīṉ piṭikkum vakuppiṉar. ivarka ḷuḷ kauri (pārvati) mākkaḷ, kaṅkaimākkaḷ eṉa iru piriviṉar uṇṭu. iv viru vakuppiṉariṭaiyum tirumaṇakkalappu naṭappatillai; āṉāl uṇpaṉavu, tiṉpaṉavu uṇṭu. ivarkaḷuḷ peṇkaḷ maṟu maṇañ ceyvatillai. tirumaṇattiṉpōtu pirāmaṇaṉ peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. vitavai maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷḷuva ḷāyiṉ vitavai oruttiyē peṇṇukku tāli kaṭṭuvaḷ. iv vakuppiṉa ruḷ cila peṇkaḷ tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷākak kōyilukku nērntu viṭappaṭu kiṟārkaḷ. iṟantavariṉ uṭal putaikkappaṭukiṟatu.
kampalattāṉ: toṭṭiyāṉ pārkka.
kampaṉ: itu ōccar kulattiṉ peyar.
kammā: ivarkaḷ tamiḻariṉiṉṟum pirintu ceṉṟa kāppukaḷ, ireṭṭikaḷ, vēḷmākkaḷ eṉpōrāvar. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ irācu eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maturai, tirunelvēli mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum kāppukkaḷum, kammākkaḷum vicayanakarat tēcātipatikaḷ kālattil irāṇuva cēvai purintōrāvar. kammāp peṇkaḷuṭ cilar muṭṭāk kiṭuvar. kammākkaḷil maṇamakaṉ perum pālum maṇamakaḷilum iḷaiyavaṉāka viruppāṉ. 22 vayatuḷḷa peṇ taṉatu kuḻantai maṇavāḷaṉai okkalaiyil eṭuttuc ceṉṟa kuṟippu oṉṟu ceṉṉai āṭ kaṇakku (Madras Census) eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. uruciya nāṭṭilum paruvamaṭainta peṇkaḷ tamakkuk kaṇavarāka niccayikkappaṭṭa āṟu allatu ēḻu vayatuḷḷa kaṇavaṉ mārai kūṭṭikkoṇṭu tirivatu vaḻakkam. vitavaikaḷ perumpālum maṟumaṇam purivatillai.
kammāḷar, kamcālar: ivarkaḷ teluṅkuk kollar.
kammāḷaṉ: kammāḷaṉ kaṇṇāḷaṉ eṉavum paṭuvāṉ. pirāma ṇaraip pōlavē kammāḷarum vicuvaku, caṉakaṉ, akimāṉ, yaṉātaṉaṉ, upēntiraṉ mutaliyavarkaḷait tamatu kōttira mutalvarkaḷākak koḷvar. ovvoru kūṭṭattukkum nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ uṇṭu. nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉukku mēlē aintu vīṭṭu nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ allatu aintu vīṭṭup periya kaikkāraṉ uṇṭu. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭal kuntiyirukkum nilaiyil camāti vaikkappaṭum. cila camayaṅkaḷil pirētaṅkaḷ erikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ kulateyvaṅkaḷ mīṉāṭci ammaṉ, kōccaṭaip periya āṇṭavaṉ, periya nayiṉār mutaliyaṉa. ēḻu kaṉṉippeṇ teyvaṅkaḷaiyum ivarkaḷ vaḻipaṭuvar. ēḻu kaṉṉip peṇ teyvaṅkaḷai vaḻipaṭuvōr mātar vakuppu eṉappaṭuvar. kōccaṭaip periya āṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum peyar kōccaṭaip periya pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripu. ivar viṭṭuṇu kōccaṭaip periya nayiṉār civaṉ eṉavum paṭuvar. kammāḷar tām vicuva kaṉmāviṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvar. ki.pi. 1013il uḷḷa ātāraṅkaḷāl avarkaḷ tāḻttappaṭṭavarkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭārkaḷeṉṟum avarkaḷ kirāmattiṉ oru pakutiyil otukkappaṭṭiruntārkaḷeṉṟum terikiṉṟaṉa. cōḻa aracaruḷ oruvaṉ, avarkaḷai vīṭukaḷil caṅku ūtavum, mēḷam aṭikkavum, miritaṭitarikkavum, vīṭṭukkuc cāntu pūcavum aṉumatittāṉ. iv vakaiyaic cērnta kammāḷarukku ācāri eṉṉum paṭṭap peyaruṇṭu.
kammāḷar (malaiyāḷattuk): ivarkaḷ pūṇūlaṇivatillai. ivarkaḷ tammait tīṭṭucceyyum cātiyiṉar eṉa ēṟṟukkoḷvar. kōyilkaḷilum pirāmaṇar illaṅkaḷilum ivarkaḷ nuḻaital ākātu. ivarkaḷil uyarntōr ācārikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷil āṇkaḷ palar oru peṇṇai maṉaiviyākak koḷvar. nāyarkaḷaip pōla ivarkaḷukkum tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇamuṇṭu. tāli kaṭṭukkali yāṇattil peṇṇiṉ cātakattukku ēṟṟa kaṇavaṉ teriyappaṭuvāṉ. tālikaṭṭu naṭanta piṉ kaṇavaṉ taṉatu āṭaiyi liruntu oru nūlai eṭuttu “kaṭṭu aṟuntu viṭṭatu” eṉṟu colliviṭṭut tirumpip pārātu pōyviṭuvāṉ. ivarkaḷukkut taṉippaṭṭa nāvitaṉ uṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ kulateyvaṅkaḷ tīkkuṭṭi, paṟakkuṭṭi, kālapairavaṉ mutaliyaṉa. ivarkaḷuḷ kalācāri, maraācāri, mūcāri (pittaḷai vēlai ceypavaṉ) kollaṉ, taṭṭāṉ, tōṟkollaṉ eṉap pala pirivuka ḷuṇṭu. kallācāri mutaliya mutal aintu vakuppiṉarukkiṭaiyil peṇ koṭuttal uṇpaṉavu tiṉpaṉavu uṇṭu. tōṟkollaṉ ivarkaḷi ṉum tāḻntavaṉāvaṉ. ivarkaḷiṉ urimai vaḻi marumakkaḷ tāyam. ivarkaḷiṉ nāvitaṉukku kuruppu eṉṟum taccar villācāṉ eṉavum aṟiyappaṭuvar. ivar muṟ kālattil tiruvitāṅkūr araciṉar paṭaikku villuc ceytu koṭuttaṉar. ki. pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kammāḷar malaiyāḷattil iruntārkaḷ eṉpataṟku cīriya kiṟittavarkaḷiṉ paṭṭaiyam oṉṟu cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟatu. kammāḷaraip paracurāmar kēraḷat tukkuk koṇṭu vantāreṉṟum perumāḷkaḷuḷ oruvar avarkaḷai vaṇṇāruḷ tirumaṇam ceyyumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭamaiyāl avarkaḷ ilaṅkaikkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭārka ḷeṉṟum kaṉṉaparam paraik kataika ḷuṇṭu. kammāḷaṉiṉ vīṭu koṭṭil eṉappaṭum. avai ōlaikkaṟṟai kaḷāl vēyappaṭṭa ciṟu kuṭicaikaḷākum. peṇkaḷ nāyarppeṇka ḷaṇiyum aṇikaḷaip pōṉṟavaṟṟai aṇivar. mūkkutti, nattu mutaliya mūkku aṇikaḷai aṇivatillai. vīṭu kaṭṭi muṭintatum ācāri mār kuṭipukum kiriyai ceyvārkaḷ. atil pāl kāyccuvatu mutaṉmai yāṉa kiriyai. teṉ tiruvitāṅkūril ivarkaḷ īḻavarilum uyarntavar kaḷākak karutappaṭuvar. peṇkaḷukkut tāli kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭantapiṉ vāḻippu eṉṉum kiriyai naṭattappaṭu kiṟatu. itaṉāl tālikaṭṭiṉavaṉukkum peṇṇukku muḷḷa toṭarpu nīkkappaṭukiṟatu. īḻavaraip pōlavē peṇkaḷ miṉṉu eṉṉum tālitarippar.
karaṇā: kañcam, oricā mākāṇaṅkaḷil vāḻum karṇam (kaṇakkaṉ) cātiyiṉar karaṇā eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ yayātikēcari eṉṉum oricā nāṭṭu aracaṉāl (ki.pi. 447-526) vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟum eḻuttāḷarāka koṇṭuvarap paṭṭavarkaḷ. vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇam ceyvatillai. muṭṭākkiṭum karaṇōppeṇkaḷ kōcā vaḻakkat taik kaikkoḷvar. paruvamaṭainta peṇkaḷ cakōtaraṉukku muṉṉāl tāṉum cella aṉumatikkappaṭamāṭṭārkaḷ.
kalavāntu : ivarkaḷ teṉkaṉṉaṭam teluṅku nāṭukaḷil vāḻum pāṭal āṭal mātarāvar.
kavuṇṭar: cēlam araciṉar aṟikkaiyil kavuṇṭar uḻu toḻil ceyvōr eṉakkuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivarkaḷ tamatu peyariṉiṟutiyil kavuṇṭaṉ eṉṉum peyaraic cērtteḻutuvar.
kaḻaikkūttāṭi: kampaṅkūttāṭi.
kaḷḷamūppaṉ: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷak kammāḷariṉ oru piriviṉar. malaiyāḷattil kammāḷar tīṭṭu uṇṭākkum vakuppiṉar. kaḷḷa mūppaṉ kōyiliṉ matilukku uṭpaṭṭa veḷivīti varaiyiṟ cella lām. ivarkaḷiṉ vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇam purivatillai. ivarkaḷiṉ purōkitaṉ ampaṭṭaṉ. avaṉ maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭiliruntu peṇṇiṉ vīṭuvaraiyum caṅku ūtic celvāṉ.
kaḷḷar: kaḷḷar eṉpataṟkuk koḷḷaiyaṭippōr eṉpatu poruḷ. ivarkaḷ perumpālum tañcāvūr, putukkōṭṭai mutaliya pakutikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum kātil tuḷai ceytu kātu tōḷil muṭṭumpaṭiyāṉa kātaṇikaḷai aṇivar. ivar kaḷiṉ talaimaikkāraṉ ampalakkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ivarkaḷiṉ tāy nāṭu toṇṭaimaṇṭalam allatu pallavar nāṭākum. putuk kōṭṭai aracar iṉṟum toṇṭaimāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. kaḷḷar kuṟumparil oru piriviṉar. paṭaiyiṉiṉṟum kalaikkappaṭṭatum ivarkaḷ koḷḷaiyaṭikkum toḻilai kaikkoṇṭaṉar. tirumaṇat til maṇamakaṉiṉ cakōtari peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvāḷ. kaḷḷaril teṟkattiyār eṉappaṭuvōr putukkōṭṭaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ talaimayirai nīḷamāka vaḷaraviṭuvar. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum kātait tuḷaiyiṭṭu ōlaiccuruḷaic cerukit tuḷai yaip perukkac ceyvar. tañcāvūril vāḻvōrukkuk kaḷḷaṉ, maṟavaṉ, akamuṭaiyāṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum. māyavaram pakuti yil akamuṭaiyāṉ, valaiyaṉ eṉpōrukkum kaḷḷaṉ eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkum. kaḷḷaṉ, maṟavaṉ, akamuṭaiyāṉ eṉpōrukkuḷ neruṅkiya uṟavuṇṭu. kirāmaṅkaḷ koḷḷaiyaṭikkappaṭātiruppataṟku ovvoru kirāmamum avarkaḷukkut tiṟaikoṭuttu vantatu. tiṟai koṭukkappaṭāviṭil māṭukaḷ tiruṭṭup pōyiṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil vīṭukaḷ tīppiṭittu viṭum. kaḷḷarāl peritum pātikkappaṭuvōr iṭaiyar allatu kōṉārāvar. māṭutiruṭṭuk koṭuttavaṉ kaḷḷaṉ oruvaṉukkut tuppukkūli koṭuttāl avaṉ iṉṉa kirāmattil iṉṉa iṭattil māṭukaṭṭi niṟkiṟatu eṉṟu colvāṉ. māṭṭukkāraṉ av viṭattiṟceṉṟu taṉ māṭṭaip peṟṟuk koḷvāṉ. tuppuk kūli māṭṭiṉ pātivilaiyaḷavu ākum. māṭu tiruṭṭup pōṉataip polīcārukkut terivittāl māṭu kiṭaikka māṭṭātu. ivarkaḷ vaḷaitaṭi eṉṉum oruvakai eṟitaṭiyaip payaṉpaṭuttuvar. it taṭi ilakkai nōkki eṟiyappaṭṭāl ilakkilpaṭṭu eṟintavaṉiṭam tirumpi varum. iv vakai vaḷaitaṭi (Boomerang) āstirēliya paḻaṅ kuṭikaḷālum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. vellūrk kaḷḷariṉ peyarkaḷ viṉōtamāṉavai. vēṅkaippuli, veṅkālippuli, cempuli, cammaṭ ṭikaḷ, tirumāṉ, cāyum paṭai tāṅki pōlvaṉa.
ivarkaḷuḷ aṇṇaṉ taṅkai piḷḷaikaḷ maṇam ceytu koḷḷalām; tāymāmaṉ piḷḷaiyai maṇattal kūṭātu. puṟamalai nāṭṭuk kaḷḷar cuṉṉattuc ceytu koḷvar. ciṟu kuṭikkaḷḷar kaṭṭum tāliyil makamatiyariṉ naṭcattiramum piṟaiyumuṇṭu. tirunelvēli yilum maturaiyilum vāḻum kaḷḷappeṇ mūrkkaṅkoṇṭa erutiṉ kompilē kaṭṭiya tuṇiyai eṭuttukkoṇṭu vantavaṉaiyē kaṇava ṉākat terivārkaḷ. māṭṭiṉ kompukaḷil vilaiyuyarnta poruḷ kaḷaik kaṭṭi māṭṭai aviḻttu viṭuvārkaḷ. caṉaṅkaḷiṉ āravārat tukkum mēḷaṅkaḷiṉ olikkum naṭuvē atu aṅkum iṅkum ōṭum. kaḷḷaṉ māṭṭukkup piṉṉāl ceṉṟu ataṉ kompiṟ kaṭṭiyirup patai aviḻtteṭuppāṉ. mēṟkattiya kaḷḷaruḷ perumpālum oru kaḷḷappeṇ pattu, eṭṭu allatu iraṇṭu kaṇavarukku maṉaiviyāka viruppāḷ. piḷḷaikaḷ ellārukkum conta muṭaiyavarkaḷāvar. maṇamakaṉiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāḷ peṇvīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu 21 paṇam koṭuttu peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil kutirai mayiraik kaṭṭi avaḷaiyum avaḷ iṉattavarkaḷaiyum maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭukku aḻaittuc celvāḷ. maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum vaḷaitaṭi māṟṟikkoḷvārkaḷ. kaḷḷariṉ mutaṉmaiyāṉa kaṭavuḷ aḻakarcāmi. maturaiyil aḻakar kaḷḷacāmi eṉappaṭuvar. kaḷḷar māṭṭuccaṇṭai naṭattuvatil viruppamuṭaiyar. itu koḻumāṭu eṉappaṭum. iṉṉoru vakai māṭṭuc caṇṭai pāyccal māṭu eṉappaṭum. kaḷḷariṉ vaḻakkamāṉa paṭṭappeyar ampalakkāraṉ, cilar akamuṭaiyāṉ, cērvai, tēvaṉ eṉavum peyarpeṟuvar.
kaṉṉaṭiyar: ivarkaḷ maicūriliruntu vantu tamiḻnāṭṭil kuṭiyēṟiṉōrāvar
kākkāḷar: ivarkaḷ mattiya tiruvitāṅkūriluḷḷa kākkāk kuṟavar. peṇkaḷ paccai kuttum toḻilpurivar. kātukuttu, kainōkku (irēkai pārttal) kompu vaippu (nōvuḷḷa iṭattil kompā lūtutal) pāmpāṭṭu, vāyppuk kūṟutal (cōciyam collutal) pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷaiyum ivarkaḷ purivar. kākkāḷar kālai ñāyiṟṟai vaṇaṅki ñāyiṟṟu vārattil poṅkaliṭuvar. āṭavar paṉṉiraṇṭu maṉaiviyar varaiyil maṇappar. ivarkaḷiṉ cottu tantai tāyariliruntu piḷḷaikaḷaic cērvatu.
kāṭar: āṉaimalaiyil vāḻum makkaḷ kāṭar eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum paṟkaḷiṉ muṉaikaḷai arāvik kūrākkuvar. peṇkaḷ kūntalil mūṅkiṟ cīppu aṇivar. ivarkaḷ pēcum moḻiyil tamiḻ malaiyāḷac citaivukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷ āḷai āḷi eṉpar; muṭi āḷi - muṭiyuṭaiyavaṉ; katti āḷi - kattiyuṭai yavaṉ; pū āḷi - pū vuṭaiyavaṉ. irāk kālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ tamatu kuṭicaikaḷiṉ muṉ viḷakkerippar. ivvāṟu ivarkaḷ ceytal karaṭi, yāṉai, puli, ciṟuttai mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ tamatu kuṭicaikaḷai aṇukā mal iruppataṟkākum. tīttaṭṭik kaṟkaḷāl ivarkaḷ tī uṇṭākku kiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ tamatu kuḻantaikaḷait tōḷiṟ kaṭṭiya tuṇiyil iṭṭuc celvar. kāṭar vālipaṉ tirumaṇam ceyya virumpiṉāl peṇ irukkum kirāmattukkuc ceṉṟu ōr āṇṭu taṅkit tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷaik koṭuppāṉ. tirumaṇattiṉ pōtu āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum pantalukku muṉṉāl niṉṟu āṭuvārkaḷ. peṇṇiṉ tāy allatu uṭaṉ piṟantāḷ tāliyai avaḷ kaḻuttil kaṭṭuvāḷ. peṇṇiṉ tantai maṇamakaṉiṉ talaiyil talaippākai vaippāṉ. pūppukkālaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ taṉimaiyāka ōriṭattiliruppar. pūppaṭaintapiṉ avarkaḷ tamakku vēṟu peyar iṭṭuk koḷvārkaḷ. kuḻantaiyaip peṟṟa peṇṇukku mūṉṟu mātaṅkaḷukkut tīṭṭu uṇṭu. oru mātamāṉatum peṇkaḷ ellōrum kūṭik kuḻantaikkup peyariṭuvārkaḷ. kaim peṇkaḷ maṟumaṇam ceyvatillai; āṉāl vaippāṭṭikaḷāka irukka aṉumatikkappaṭuvar. tāymār iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ varai kuḻantaikkup pālūṭṭi vaḷarppar. pallai arattiṉāl arāvik kūrākkutal aḻaku eṉṟu kāṭar karutuvar. ciṟuvar pulinakam, mutalaippal mutaliyavaṟṟai aṇintiruppar. peṇkaḷ kātiluḷḷa tuḷaikaḷil ōlaiyaic curuṭṭic cerukuvar. irumpuk kāppu, irumpu mōtiram, maṇikōtta mālaikaḷ eṉpavaṟ ṟaiyum ivarkaḷ aṇivar.
kāṭupaṭṭaṉ: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattiluḷḷa nāyarc cātiyiṉar pōṉṟōr. ivarkaḷiṉ urimai vaḻi tantai tāyariliruntu cottu piḷḷaikaḷaic cērvatu. maṇamakaḷukkup peṇṇiṉ cakōtari tāli kaṭṭuvāḷ. piṇac caṭaṅkukaḷai ampaṭṭaṉ ceyvāṉ. peṇ āṇ cantatiyiṉṟik kaimmaiyāṉāl kaṇavaṉiṟanta 12vatu nāḷ peṟṟōr vīṭṭukkuc celvāḷ. āṇ cantatiyiruntāl kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭilirup pāḷ.
kāṭṭu marātti: kuruvikkārar eṉṉum vakuppiṉarukkuk kāṭṭu marātti eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkum. malaiyāḷattil vāḻum kuṟumpar kāṭṭu nāykkar eṉappaṭuvar.
kāṇikkārar: teṉ tiruvitāṅkūril malaikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar. kāṇikkārariṉ uṭpiriviṉar illaṅkaḷ eṉap paṭuvar. ivarkaḷ pēcum moḻi malaiyāḷam. ivarkaḷiṉ urimai tantaiyiṉ cottu piḷḷaikaḷaic cērvatu. ammaṉ, pūtanātaṉ, veṭikkāṭṭup pūtam, vaṭatalaip pūtam mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷai ivar kaḷ vaḻipaṭuvar. tirumaṇattiṉpōtu peṇ ciṟumiyāyiṉ maṇamakaṉē tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ; paruva maṭaintavaḷāyiṉ peṇṇiṉ cakōtari kaṭṭu vāḷ. peṇ karuppamaṭaintu ēḻāvatu mātam vayiṟṟup poṅkal eṉṉum poṅkal iṭappaṭukiṟatu. ip poṅkal ēḻu aṭuppil ēḻu ulaikaḷai vaittuc ceyyappaṭum.
kātu kuttuk kuṟavar: pala cātiyiṉarukkum kātu kuttum kuṟavar ip peyar peṟuvar.
kāppiliyar: ivarkaḷ kuṟumparil oru piriviṉar. peṇkaḷukkut tirumaṇattiṉ aṭaiyāḷamāka poṭṭu allatu tāli kaṭṭappaṭum. kaṇavaṉ vayatil iḷaiyavaṉāka viruntāl peṇ kiṭṭiya uṟaviṉa ṉaic cērntu peṟum piḷḷai kaṇavaṉiṉ piḷḷaiyākak koḷḷap paṭum. uṭaṉ piṟantāḷiṉ kaṇavaṉōṭu uṟavu pūṇṭiruntāl avaḷ oḻukkattil tavaṟiyavaḷākak karutappaṭamāṭṭāḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ ilakkammā, vīra ilakkammā, teṇṭaiyā, timmappaṉ, ciṅkārap perumāḷ mutaliyavai.
kāppu: ivarkaḷ ireṭṭi vakuppiṉar. ivarkaḷ teluṅku nāṭṭil uḻu toḻil ceykiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇarukku aṭutta paṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
kirāmaṇi: itu cāṇār cilariṉ paṭṭappeyar. ivarkaḷiṉ talaimaikkārarukkuk kāttiri eṉṉum paṭṭappeyar vaḻaṅkum.
kiruṣṇāvaik kākkā: ivarkaḷ iraṇiyal, kalkuḷam (tiruvitāṅkūr) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻvōr. āṇkaḷiṉ peyar iṟutiyil āyaṉ eṉṟum peṇkaḷiṉ peyariṟutiyil āycci yeṉ ṟum muṭivukaḷ cērntu vaḻaṅkum. ivarkaḷ vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa ampātiyiliruntu vantu kāñcīpurattil āyarpāṭiyil vāḻntārkaḷ. makarācā uṭaiya mārttāṇṭavarmaṉ kālattil ivarkaḷ kēraḷat tukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ. mārttāṇṭavarmaṉ kālam kollamāṇṭu 904-933. ivarkaḷiṭaiyē marumakkaḷ tāyam, makkaḷ tāyam eṉṉum iruvakai urimai vaḻikaḷum uṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ kuru kāṇattaṉ allatu kacāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. kurukkaḷkulap peṇkaḷ maṅkali ammā eṉappaṭuvar. marumakkaḷ tāyakkārar malaiyāḷa moḻi pēcuvar. makkaḷ vaḻittāyakkārar koccait tamiḻ pēcuvar. marumakkaḷ tāyakkārarukku iḷamaiyil tāli kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇamuṇṭu. oruttiyiṉ kaṇavaṉ iṟantu pōṉāl avaḷ avaṉ tampiyiṉ maṉaiviyāvāḷ. avaḷ aṇin tirukkum āparaṇaṅkaḷaik kaḷaiyavēṇṭiyatillai. marumakkaḷ tāyakkārariṉ peṇkaḷ tāy vaḻiyāl aṟiyappaṭuvar.
kilāci: ivarkaḷ teluṅku ampaṭṭar. ivarkaḷiṉ urimai peṇ vaḻiyāka varuvatu.
kiḻakkatti: vaṭa allatu teṟku ārkkāṭṭup paṟaiyar ceṉṉaiyil kiḻakkatti eṉappaṭuvar.
kīraikkāraṉ: kōyamputtūril kīrai payiriṭum akampaṭiyār cilarukku ippeyar vaḻaṅkum.
kukavēḷāḷar: cila vēḷāḷarum maṟavarum tām irāmarukku ōṭam viṭṭa kukaṉiliruntu tōṉṟiyavarkaḷ eṉak koṇṭu tammaik kukavēḷāḷar eṉpar.
kucarātti: kūrccarattiṉiṉṟum vantu teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟi ṉōr kucarātti eṉappaṭuvar.
kuṭikārar: ōviyan tīṭṭuvōr, maraṅkaḷil uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭu vōrukkuk kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. kuṭi eṉpatu kōyilaik kuṟikkum.
kuṭikkār: tiruvitāṅkūriluḷḷa tēvatācikaḷukku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. ivarkaḷukku vīṭṭuk kūli illai.
kuṭimakaṉ: itu ampaṭṭaṉukku vaḻaṅkum tamiḻp peyar.
kuṭiyar (kuṭi=malai) : ivarkaḷ teṉ kaṉṉaṭattil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar; koccaittuḷup pēcukiṉṟaṉar. maicūr ellaip puṟaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya kaṭavuḷar pairavar, kāmaṉ tēvāru, pañcapāṇṭavar eṉpōrāvar. peṇṇum maṇamaka ṉum kaiyaip piṭittu niṟka maṇamakaḷiṉ tantai nīrai ūṟṟuvatē avarkaḷiṉ maṇak kiriyaiyākum. cila camayaṅkaḷil maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum kuṭittalaivaṉiṉ muṉṉāl niṉṟu oruvarukku oruvar poṭṭu iṭṭuk koḷvar.
kuṭiyā: oriyānāṭṭu miṭṭāy viṟpōr ippeyar peṟuvar. kuṭō - karuppuk kaṭṭi.
kuṭupiyar: ivarkaḷiṉ cātip peyar kuḷavāṭi. ivarkaḷ peri tum kuṇṭappūr mākāṇattil kāṇappaṭuvar. koṅkaṇi, marāṭṭi mutaliyaṉa ivarkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷākum. ivarkaḷuḷ viyapicāra naṭattai yuḷḷa āṇiṉ talaiyaiyum mukattaiyum ciraittu avaṉaik kuḻiyil niṟutti eccil ilaiyait talai mītu eṟivatu vaḻakku. talaimaikkāraṉ paṇat taṇṭam vitippāṉ. peṇ uṇmai kūṟāviṭil irumpuk kampiyuṭaṉ veyilil niṟuttappaṭuvāḷ. ivarkaḷ tamatu kulatey vattai vīṭṭiṉ oru puṟattil vaittu vaḻipaṭuvar; kuṭumpattiṉaruḷ oruvaṉ pūcāriyāka viruppāṉ. ivarkaḷ pūtaṅkaḷaiyum kālapairavarai yum vaḻipaṭuvar. vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷvar. āṉāl iṟanta kaṇavaṉiṉ kuṭumpattil maṇam muṭikka māṭṭārkaḷ; perum pālum kācuk kaṭṭi ceyvar. kācuk kaṭṭi oruvakai marattai veṭṭi avittu ataṉ cattiliruntu ceyyappaṭukiṟatu.
kuṭumi allatu kuṭumikkārar: ippeyar orē kuṭumpam eṉpataṉ citaivu eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ kuṭiyar eṉavum paṭuvar. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ tām ceṭṭi iṉattaic cērntavarka ḷeṉak kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷil mariyātaikkuriyavaraik kocci aracar mūppaṉ eṉṟu aḻaippar. koṅkaṇa moḻiyiṉ citaivākiya oru vakai moḻiyai ivarkaḷ pēcuvar. peṇkaḷ pūppukkuppiṉ nāṉku nāṭkaḷ tīṭṭuk kāppar. ak kālattil avaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷukku ēḻaṭi tūrattil niṟka vēṇṭu meṉṟum, niḻal maṟṟavarkaḷ mītu viḻutal kūṭāteṉṟum vitikaḷuṇṭu. peṇkaḷukkut tirumaṇam pūppaṭaiyum muṉ naṭakkum. vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇam muṭippar. kuṭumpattil mika mutiyavariṉ piṇam māttiram cuṭappaṭum. ivarkaḷukku iṟappu piṟapput tīṭṭukaḷ patiṉāṟu nāṭkaḷukkuṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya poḻutu pōkku kōlāṭṭam.
kuṭaikaṭṭi: ivarkaḷ pāṇaril oru piriviṉar.
kuṇi: ivarkaḷ āṭal pāṭal puriyum oriya tāci vakuppiṉar.
kummārar: (kumpakārar-kuyavar) avarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭa teluṅku kuyavaril oru piriviṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭuk kuyavaraippōla ivarkaḷ nūl tarippatillai.
kuyavaṉ: maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyvōr ippeyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷ pūṇūlaṇivar. piṭāri kōyilkaḷil ivarkaḷ pūcāriyāka iruppatuṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭap peyar uṭaiyāṉ, vēḷāṉ eṉpaṉa. muṟkālattil iṟantavarkaḷai vaittup putaikkum aḻakiya tāḻikaḷai ivarkaḷ ceytārkaḷ. tirunelvēli, maturai, malaiyāḷam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil tāḻikaḷ kiṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. kuyavaṉ māmiyiṉ makaḷai maṇakkalām. peṇ paruvamaṭaivataṉ muṉ maṇam naṭakkiṉṟatu. maṇamakaṉiṉ cakōtari peṇṇukkut tāli tarippāḷ.
kurukaḷ: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷak kuru vakuppiṉ oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ tamiḻar uṟpattiyaic cērntavarākalām. āṭavar nayiṉār eṉavum makaḷir nācciyār eṉavum paṭuvar. ivarkaḷ tiruvaṉantapuratti luḷḷa patmanāpa cuvāmikku vaḻit toṇṭar. ivarkaḷ, maṭattalaivar kaḷum, tēvāra paṇṭāraṅkaḷum camayak kiriyaikaḷ puriyumpōtu utavi ceypavarkaḷāvar. kollam āṇṭiṉ eṭṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil ivarkaḷ patmanāpacāmi kōviliṉ uḷmaṇṭapaṅkaḷai alakiṭum vēlai ceytu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ illam pavaṉam allatu vīṭu eṉappaṭum. peṇkaḷ kurukkattikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ kaḻuttil aracilait tāliyaiyum, kātil cūṭṭu eṉṉum aṇiyaiyum, mūkkil nattu allatu mūkkuttiyaiyum aṇivar; kaiyilum neṟṟiyilum paccai kuttik koḷvar. kurukaḷiṉ purōkitar upāttiyāyar eṉappaṭuvar. tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam campantam mutaliya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷiṭaiyē uṇṭu. ivarkaḷukku maraṇat tīṭṭu ēḻu nāḷ.
kuruppu: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattiṟ kāṇappaṭum kolla vakup pil oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷiṟ pala pirivukaḷuṇṭu. kāya, palica (kēṭakam) tōl mutaliya peyarkaḷ avarkaḷ peyarkaḷukku muṉṉāl iṭṭu vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
kuruvikkāraṉ: kuruvi piṭippōril marāṭṭi pēcukiṉṟa kūṭṭattiṉar ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷ kāṭṭu marāṭṭikaḷ eṉavum aṟiyappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ tamatu poruḷ paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum kuṭicaikaḷaiyum potimāṭukaḷil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu alaintu tirivar. ivarkaḷ piccai eṭuttum, ūci, maṇi, mutaliyaṉa viṟṟum vāḻkkai naṭattuvar. ivarkaḷ palaraic ceṉṉai nakaril kāṇalām. kuruvik kāraṉ valaiyaik kaṭṭi ataṉuḷḷiruntu nari pōlac cattamiṭuvāṉ. narikaḷ ac cattattaik kēṭṭu ōṭi varum. kuruvikkāraṉ avaṟṟai aṭittuk kolluvāṉ. kaliyāṇamāṉa kuruvikkāri pakal muḻutum alaintu tirintuviṭṭu iravāṉatum kaṇavaṉirukku miṭattukku varavēṇṭum; allāviṭil vēkak kāycciya irumpaip piṭittuk koṇṭu avaḷ patiṉāṟaṭi cellavēṇṭum; allatu kotikkak kāycciya cāṇi nīruḷ kaivaittu aṭiyil irukkum kālaṇāvait taṭavi eṭukka vēṇṭum. avaḷ kuṟṟamaṟṟavaḷāṉāl uṭaṉē uḷḷaṅ kaiyil nellai vaittu uraiñci umiyaip pōkkak kūṭiyavaḷāvaḷ. āṇkaḷ tamatu peyariṉiṟutiyil ciṅ eṉpataic cērttuk koḷvar. tirumaṇattiṉpōtu avarkaḷiṉ talaimaikkāraṉ kaṟuppuk kayiṟṟai allatu maṇikaḷ kōtta mālaiyai maṇamakaṉiṉ kaiyil koṭup pāṉ. avaṉ atai maṇamakaḷiṉ kaḻuttil aṇivāṉ. turkkaiyum kāḷiyum avarkaḷiṉ mutaṉmaiyāṉa teyvaṅkaḷ.
kuṟavar: ivarkaḷ iṭamviṭṭu iṭampeyarntu tiriyum malaic cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷ koccait tamiḻ pēcuvar; kūṭaimuṭaivar. vāyppuk (cōciyam) kūṟuvar. tirumaṇattil maṇamakaḷ mañcaḷnūl tāli tarippar. kuṟap peṇkaḷ paccai kuttuvārkaḷ. kuṟavar pūṉai, kōḻi, mīṉ, paṉṟi, karuṅkuraṅku, nari, eli, māṉ mutaliyavaṟṟi ṉiṟaicciyai uṇpar. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ kaḻuttil kaṟuppup pāciyum kaiyil vaḷaiyalumaṇintiruppar. kaṇavaṉai iḻanta peṇkaḷ avaṟṟaik kaḷaintuviṭuvar; “kuṟatti piḷḷaiyaip peṟak kuṟavaṉ kāyantiṉkiṟatu” eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyuṇṭu. kuṟatti piḷḷai peṟṟāl kuṟavaṉ mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukkup paṭukkaiyiliruntu kāyan tiṉpāṉ.
kuṟavar (tiruvitāṅkūr): tiruvitāṅkūrp pakutiyil 50,000-kkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟavar vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ kuṇṭakkuṟavar, pūṅkuṟavar, kākkāk kuṟavar eṉa mūṉṟu piriviṉarāvar. pāṇṭik kuṟavar tamiḻ pēcuvar. ivarkaḷ peritum nāñcil nāṭṭil (nākar kōyiṟ pakuti) kāṇappaṭuvar. nāñcil kuṟavar eṉpatu avarkaḷiṉ maṟupeyar. kuṇṭakkuṟavar tamatu muṉṉōr ōma kuṇṭattiṉiṉṟum piṟantavar eṉak kūṟuvar. mūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ nāñcil nāṭu nāñciṟ kuṟavarāl āḷappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ iṟanta muṉṉōriṉ āvikaḷai vaṇaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ mēlāṉa kaṭavuḷ kārttikēya aṭikaḷ. ivar kaḷiṉ iṟantupōṉa muṉṉōr cāvār eṉappaṭuvar. cāvārukkuc ciṟukukaik kōyilkaḷuṇṭu. cāvārukkup pūcaiceyyum pūcāri piṇiyāḷi eṉappaṭuvāṉ. irārakkārar allatu viccārakkārar eṉṉum oruvakaiyiṉaruṇṭu. avarkaḷ nōykaḷiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷai ārāyvar. avarkaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷ cāvār, āyiravalli, cāttāṉ, pakavati, māṭaṉ, mūṭi, teyvam, pakavāṉ, appuppaṉ, marutaṉ mutaliyaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ kuṭittalaivaṉ ūrāḷi, paṇikkaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. peṇ kaḷukkut tirumaṇam pūppāvataṉmuṉ naṭaipeṟum. tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇamuṇṭu. campantam koḷḷum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu. tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇattil kuṟatti peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tālikaṭṭuvāṉ. kuṟavaṉ oru peṇṇai maṇakka virumpiṉāl avaṉ peṇṇiṉ tantaikkup paṉṉiraṇṭu paṇam koṭukkavēṇṭum. vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyvar. ivarkaḷiṉ urimai vaḻi marumakkaḷ tāyam. ivarkaḷukku maraṇattīṭṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu nāḷ. tāḻntavar uyarnta cātiyiṉarukku nāṟpatteṭṭaṭi tūrattil niṟṟal vēṇṭum.
kuṟiccāṉ: malaiyāḷattil vēṭṭaiyāṭi vāḻum cāti yiṉarukku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. ip peyar kuṟicci eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantirukkalām. malaiyāḷattil kuṟicci eṉpatu malaiyaik kuṟikkum. kuṟiccāṉkaḷ pirāmaṇariṭattil atika veṟuppuk kāṭṭuvar. pirāmaṇa ṉoruvaṉ kuṟiccāṉ vīṭṭukkucceṉṟu tirumpiṉāl kuṟiccāṉ pirāmaṇaṉ irunta iṭattaic cāṇiyāl meḻukic cuttañ ceyvāṉ. marumakkaḷ tāyamum, makkaḷ tāyamum ivarkaḷiṭaiyē uṇṭu. ivar kaḷiṉ teyvam mūttappaṉ (pāṭṭaṉ). ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ puṉam ceyvar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum vēṇāṭu, kaḷḷikkōṭu, mēṟkut toṭarcci malaiccāralkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. tīyarum, kammāḷarum tīṇṭiṉāl ivarkaḷukkut tīṭṭu uṇṭu. pūppu eytu muṉ peṇkaḷuk kut tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭakkum. viḻākkālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷmītu teyvam ēṟi āṭi veḷippuk kūṟum. cila iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻvōr nalla taṇṇīril vāḻum mīṉkaḷaik kaivillāl ampai eytu kolvar. ampu nīṇṭa kayiṟṟiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. karimpil pakavati, malaik kuṟatti, atir aḷḷaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarai ivarkaḷ vaḻipaṭuvar.
kuṟumō: kuṟumō eṉpār iṟacal koṇṭāp pakutikaḷil vāḻum payirttoḻil puriyum oriya vakuppiṉar. teluṅkar ivar kaḷaik kuṭumō eṉpar. ivarkaḷiṉ kirāma teyvaṅkaḷ takkuṟāṇi eṉappaṭum. pākatēvi, kumpēcuvari, cātapavūṉi mutaliyaṉa avarkaḷ kuṭumpat teyvaṅkaḷākum.
kuṟumpar: āṭṭaik kuṟikkum kuṟu eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac colli liruntu ip peyar uṇṭāṉateṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu. kuṟu eṉpatu koṟi (āṭu) eṉṉuñ colliṉ tiripu. ivarkaḷiṉ talaimaikkāraṉ koṭu eṉappaṭuvāṉ. talaip pūppaṭainta peṇkaḷ vīṭṭiṉ oru mūlaiyil eṭṭu nāṭkaḷ viṭappaṭuvārkaḷ. oṉpatāvatu nāḷ peṇkaḷ avaḷai muḻukkāṭṭip pīṭattiṉmītu irutti, mañcaḷ nīrum cuṇṇāmpu nīrum kalanta taṭṭai avaḷukku muṉṉāl ēnti avaḷiṉ kāl, maṭi, talai mītu arici tūvi uṭukkap putiya āṭai koṭuppārkaḷ. tirumaṇattiṉpōtu aintu peṇkaḷ tāliyait toṭṭa piṉpu kurukkaḷ peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil ataṉaik kaṭṭuvār. piṉṉar maṟṟap peṇkaḷ kaliyāṇattukkut teriyappaṭuvārkaḷ. nalla cuḻikaḷiloṉṟu pāciṅkam. itu neṟṟiyiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu. mikak kūṭāta cuḻikaḷ pēy, yāṉai eṉpaṉa. ivai talaiyiṉ piṉpuṟattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. kūṭāta cuḻiyuṭaiya peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉaikkoṉṟu viṭuvārkaḷ eṉṉum nampikkai ivarkaḷiṭaiyē uṇṭu. ākavē tīya cuḻiyuṭaiya peṇkaḷ maṉaiviyai iḻanta āṇkaḷukku maṇamuṭittuk koṭukkappaṭuvar. maṇam kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭil naṭaipeṟum. pūppaṭainta peṇkaḷukkup pūppup kaliyāṇam muṉṉatāka naṭaipeṟukiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷ maṟu maṇam ceytukoḷvar. ivarkaḷukku iṉṉoru vitavai tāli kaṭṭu vāḷ. iṟantu pōṉa tantaikku mūttamakaṉ koḷḷikkuṭamuṭaippāṉ. iṟantavaṉiṉ maṉaivi patiṉōrāvatu nāḷ kaivaḷaikaḷai uṭaittu viṭuvāḷ. vaṇṇattammā, turkkammā, mutaliya peṇ teyvaṅkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷil peṇ pūcārikaḷ pūcai ceyvar. viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil pūcāri teyvamēṟi āṭi varuṅkāriyaṅ kūṟuvāṉ. ivvāṟu teyva mēṟuvatu kāraṇikam eṉappaṭum. vaḷarntavarkaḷiṉ pirētam erikkap paṭum, ciṟuvariṉ pirētam putaikkappaṭum. ivarkaḷukku maraṇat tīṭṭu pattunāḷ. maṇamākāta peṇkaḷ taṉittaṉi viṭappaṭṭa kuṭicaikaḷil paṭuttu uṟaṅkuvārkaḷ. maṇamākāta ciṟuvarum āṭavarum taṉik kuṭicai kaḷil paṭuttuṟaṅkuvārkaḷ. payirai mēyavarum yāṉaikaḷiṉ mukattukku nērē ivarkaḷ cūḷaikkāṭṭiyum, kāṭṭuppaṉṟikaḷaik kavaṇiṟ kallai vaitteṟintum ōṭṭuvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil teṉkuṟumpar eṉṉum oru piriviṉarum uṇṭu. kuṟumpariṉ cātit talaivaṉ mutali eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ivarkaḷiṭaiyē vēṭṭa, uṟaḷi eṉṉum iru piriviṉa rum uṇṭu. nīlakirik kuṟumparil pala cakōtarar cērntu oru peṇṇai maṇappar. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ camātiyil avarkaḷ nīruḷ irukkum kallai (tēva kōṭṭak kal) eṭuttuk koṇṭu vantu naṭuvārkaḷ. iraṅka cāmik kōṭu, parāliyar (Baraliar) mutaliya kuṉṟukaḷil vāḻvōr iṟantavariṉ uṭalai erittu elumpiṉ oru ciṟu tuṇṭaiyum, ciṟu kallaiyum avariṉ camātiyil (cāvumaṉai) vaippar; cila pakutikaḷil iru periya kaṟkaḷai nāṭṭi mēlē oru pāvu kallai vaippar.
kuṉṟuvar: ivarkaḷ paḻani malaiyil vāḻum payirttoḻil puriyum makkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ moḻi tamiḻ. ivarkaḷ tamatu muṉṉōr vēḷāḷar eṉak kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷ talaivaṉ maṇṇāṭi eṉappaṭuvaṉ. peṇkaḷ veḷḷai āṭai uṭuppārkaḷ. oruvaṉ māmiyiṉ makaḷai maṇakkalām. kaṇavaṉ iḷam vayatiṉaṉāyiṉ avaḷ ac cāti āṭavaṉ oruvaṉōṭu cērntu vāḻvāḷ. avvāṟu piṟakkum piḷḷaikaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ piḷḷaikaḷākavē karutappaṭum. eṭṭu vayatuḷḷa oruvaṉukku iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu piḷḷaikaḷ irukkakkūṭum.
kūṭalar: vicākappaṭṭiṉam kañcam pakutikaḷil vāḻum kūṭaimuṭaiyum vakuppiṉar ip peyar peṟuvar.
kūṭāṉ: malaiyāḷattiṟ kāṇappaṭum kāṇiyāḷariṉ aṭimaikaḷ kūṭāṉeṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ ellā uyarnta cātiyiṉaruk kum nāṟpattiraṇṭaṭi tūrattil niṟṟal vēṇṭum. pulaiyar, nāyāṭi, ullāṭar mutaliyavarkaḷukkup pakkattil niṉṟāl ivarkaḷukkut tīṭṭuṇṭākum. ivarkaḷukku ampaṭṭarum, vaṇṇārum uṇṭu. peṇkaḷ pūppu aṭaintāl nāṉku allatu ēḻu nāṭkaḷukkut tīṭṭuk kāppārkaḷ. kūṭāṉ taṉatu contac cātiyil allatu paṟaiyar vakuppil campantam vaittuk koḷḷalām. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyam uṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṭattil cottu iruppatillai. oru āṭu, oru māṭu allatu cila kōḻikaḷē ivarkaḷiṉ cottākum.
kūṇā: ivarkaḷ vēḷmā vakuppiṉariṉ oru piriviṉar.
kūttāṭi: āriyak kūttaṉ, kaḻaik kūttaṉ mutaliyōr ip peyar peṟuvar.
kūrmāppu: ivarkaḷ vicākappaṭṭiṉap pakutiyil kāṇappaṭum āṭal makaḷirāvar. ivarkaḷ maṇam muṭippatillai; viyapicāratti ṉāl poruḷīṭṭuvar. viruntuk kālaṅkaḷil āṭal purivar. ivarkaḷ vicākappaṭṭiṉattiluḷḷa ciṟī kūrmam eṉṉum kōyilil tēvaṭi yāṭkaḷāka iruntavarkaḷāvar.
kaikkōḷar: teṉṉāṭṭil necavut toḻil puriyum perum piriviṉar ip peyar peṟuvar. maturai nāyakka aracar kaikkōḷar vēlaiyil tiruptiyuṟātu vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṉum paṭṭunūṟkārarai aḻaittārkaḷ eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟu tamiḻ necavāḷarai viṭap paṭṭunūṟkārariṉ eṇṇikkai atikamākum. kaikkōḷar ceṅkuntar eṉavum paṭuvar. paṟaiyarum ivarkaḷum tammai vīrapākuviṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷuḷ cōḻiyar, iraṭṭu ciṟutāli, peruntāli, cīrpātam, cēvuka virutti eṉṉum pirivukaḷuṇṭu. ciṟuntāli peruntāli eṉpaṉa ciṟiya tāliyaiyum periya tāliyaiyum aṇivatu kāraṇamākat tōṉṟiya peyarkaḷ. kaikkōḷariṟ perum pāliṉar caivar. ivar iliṅkaṅ kaṭṭuvar. ciṟu tokaiyiṉar vaiṇava matattiṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ talaimaikkāraṉ periyataṉakkāraṉ allatu paṭṭakkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. periya taṉakkāraṉ makā nāṭṭāṉ eṉavum paṭuvaṉ. ivarkaḷuḷ naṭṭukkaṭṭāta nāyaṉmār eṉṉum paṇṭāraṅkaḷuṇṭu. ivarkaḷ nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu tamatu kulattavarkaḷuk kiṭaiyil tōṉṟum piṇakkukaḷait tīrttu vaippar. kaikkōḷaruḷ poṉṉampalattār eṉṉum paṇṭāraṅkaḷumuṇṭu. oṭṭakkūttap pulavar kaikkōḷa vakuppaic cērntavarāvar. kāñcīpurattiṟ capai kūṭumpōtu kaikkōḷat talaivaṉ talaiyaṇaiyiṟ cāyntu koṇṭiruppāṉ. ākavē avaṉ tiṇṭukkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. kaikkōḷak kuṭumpamovvoṉṟilum ālaya cēvaikkup peṇkaḷ nērntu viṭappaṭavēṇṭum. kaikkōḷar tām tēvatācikaḷōṭu toṭarpaṟṟavar eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tācikaḷukkuk kōyil vikkirakat tukku muṉṉilaiyil pirāmaṇaṉ tāli kaṭṭukiṉṟāṉ. tāli eṉpatu kaṟuppu maṇikaḷ kōttamālai (kaṟuppuppāci). kaikkōḷarukku mutali, nāyaṉmā reṉṟa paṭṭappeyarkaḷuṇṭu.
koṅkaṇi: koṅkaṇariṉ āti iruppiṭam caracuvati āṟṟai aṭutta iṭaṅkaḷ. aṅkiruntum vantu teṟkē kuṭiyēṟiṉōr koṅkaṇikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar.
koṅka vēḷāḷar: ireṭṭimār ivarkaḷuṭaṉiruntu uṇṇa māṭṭārkaḷ. kātil toṅkum vaḷaiyaṅkaḷaiyum mēṟkātil toṅkum murukukaḷaiyum koṇṭu ivarkaḷ matikkappaṭuvārkaḷ. muṟukkit tirikkappaṭāta nūlil peṇkaḷ tāli aṇivārkaḷ. tāyattu eṉṉum aṇiyai iṭatukaiyil aṇivārkaḷ. āṇkaḷ tāymāmaṉ makaḷai maṇappar. ciṟu paiyaṉ vaḷarnta peṇṇukkuk kaliyāṇam muṭikkappaṭu vāṉ. makaṉ vaḷarum varaiyum avaṉ tantai avaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉiṉ kaṭamaikaḷaic ceytu varuvāṉ. tirumaṇak kālattil avarkaḷ kuru arumaikkāraṉ eṉa aḻaikkappaṭuvāṉ. kuruviṉ maṉaivi arumaik kāri eṉappaṭuvaḷ. arumaikkāraṉ peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tālikaṭṭu vāṉ. appoḻutu ampaṭṭaṉ “cantiracūriyar uḷḷaḷavum uṅkaḷ kiḷaikaḷ ālpōl taḻaittuc cuṟṟam mūṅkil pōl peruka” eṉṟu vāḻttuvāṉ. ivarkaḷuṭ piccaikkārar muṭavāṇṭikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar.
koṭikkāl: koṭikkāl - veṟṟilai. veṟṟilai payiriṭu vōrkku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. cāṇāril oru piriviṉarukkum koṭik kāl eṉṉum peyaruṇṭu.
koṭippaṭṭaṉ: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattil vāḻum tamiḻp pirāmaṇaruḷ oru pakutiyiṉar. ivarkaḷ veṟṟilaik koṭikaḷaip payiriṭṭamaiyāl pirāmaṇat taṉmaiyai iḻantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya iruppiṭam vāmaṉapuri.
koṇṭatōrar: vicākappaṭṭiṉattiṟ payiriṭum malaivācikaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷ pāṇṭavaraiyum, talupul ammāvai yum vaḻipaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ tammaip pāṇṭavar kulattiṉareṉakkūṟik koḷvar.
koṇṭar: ivarkaḷ kañcam, vicākappaṭṭiṉam, vaṅkāḷam, mattiya mākāṇaṅkaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ tammai kūy eṉṉum peyar koṭuttum vaḻaṅkuvar. itu kōya allatu kōyāvukkuc camam. ivarkaḷil 58 pirivukaḷuṇṭu. pōr ceyyumpōtu ivarkaḷ erumaik kompum mayiliṟaku maṇivar. peṇkaḷ mukattil paccai kuttikkoḷ var. muṟkālattil koṇṭar pūmik kaṭavuḷukku narapali iṭṭaṉar. narapaliyiṭutaṟkup payaṉpaṭut tappaṭṭa marap palipīṭamoṉṟu ceṉṉai nūtaṉa poruṭkāṭcic cālaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. narapali āṅkila rāl niṟuttappaṭṭatu. tirumaṇak kālattil maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷait tiruṭik koṇṭu pōvatākavum peṇvīṭṭār peṇṇai mīṭṭuk koṇṭu pōvatākavum iru pakutiyāruk kiṭaiyilum pōlip pōr naṭattappaṭuvatuṇṭu.
koṇṭālikar: marāṭṭi uṟpattiyaic cērnta piccai eṭukkum paṇṭāraṅkaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar.
kollar: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷak kammāḷa vakuppiṉar, ivarkaḷuḷ tīkkollaṉ, peruṅkollaṉ, kaṉaciṟkollaṉ, tōṟkollaṉ eṉap pala pirivukaḷuṇṭu.
kōcākkaḷ: ivarkaḷ aṇṇakar (vitaiyaṭikkappaṭṭavar). teṉṉintiyāvil ivarkaḷiṟ palar kāṇappaṭavillai. cila camayaṅ kaḷil intukkaḷ cilarum pirāmaṇar cilarum avarkaḷiṉ cammatattiṉ pēril aṇṇakarākkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. viraiyai eṭuttuviṭum vēlai ampaṭṭa vakuppiṉarālum aṇṇakarālum ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ mucalmāṉkaḷiṉ antappuraṅkaḷil vēlai ceyvārkaḷ.
kōṣṭi allatu kōṣṭā: cūṭiyanākapuriyil necavu, payirt toḻilkaḷ puriyum teluṅku vakuppiṉar ippeyar peṟuvar.
kōṭar: ivarkaḷ muṉpu maicūriluḷḷa kolli malaiyil vāḻntārkaḷ. ippoḻutu nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiluḷḷa ēḻu kirāmaṅ kaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kaṉṉaṭamum teluṅkum kalanta moḻi pēcukiṉṟa ṉar. ellā vakai iṟaiccikaḷaiyum uṇpar. kōṭaruḷ kollar, taccar, taṭṭār,tōl merukiṭuvōr, kuyavar, vaṇṇār, payiriṭuvōr mutaliya pala piriviṉaruḷar. ivarkaḷ erumait tōliṉāṟ piṉṉiya nīṇṭa kayiṟukaḷāl āṭu māṭukaḷaik kaṭṭuvar; āvaracampaṭṭai cuṇṇāmpu mutaliyavaikaḷaik koṇṭu tōlaip pataṉiṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kurumār pūcāri, tēvāti eṉa irupiriviṉarāvar. ivarkaḷ maṇam ceytu koṇṭavarkaḷāyiruttal vēṇṭum. maṉaivi iṟantāl ivarkaḷ kurumārāka irukka muṭiyātu. kāmāṭarāyaṉ, maṅkāḷi, vēṭṭaikkārac cuvāmi, atiral, utiral mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷai ivarkaḷ vaḻipaṭuvar; ōṇa nāḷaip perunāḷākak koṇṭāṭuvar. vaicūri nōy māriyammāvāl uṇṭākiṉṟateṉṟu nampuvar; mākāḷi yaik kaṟṟūṇ vaṭivil vaḻipaṭuvar. maṉaivi karuppamāyiruntāl kōṭaṉ taṉatu talaimayiraiyum mukamayiraiyum vaḷaraviṭuvāṉ. peṇkaḷ piḷḷaiyīṉṟataṉāluṇṭāṉa tīṭṭu aṭutta piṟai kāṇum varaiyum uṇṭu. pūppuk kālattil peṇkaḷukku mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ tīṭṭu uṇṭu. kaṇavaṉai iḻanta peṇkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷaik kaḷaintuviṭu vārkaḷ.
kōṭṭaippattu: ivarkaḷ akampaṭiyāruḷ oru piriviṉar.
kōṭṭai vēḷāḷar: tirunelvēliyiluḷḷa ciṟīvaikuntat til pala kōṭṭai vēḷāḷa kuṭumpaṅkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ irukkum kōṭṭaikkuḷ piṟa āṭavar cellutal kūṭātu; peṇkaḷ cellalām. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉ, tantai, tāymāmaṉ, cakōtaraṉ allāta piṟariṉ mukattaip pārttal kūṭātu. ivarkaḷiṉ talaivaṉ kōṭṭaip piḷḷai eṉappaṭuvāṉ. peṇkaḷ aintu talainākattiṉ paṭam pōṉṟa oru vakai aṇiyai aṇivar.
kōmaṭṭi: ivarkaḷ ceṉṉai mākāṇattil vāṇikam ceyyum cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷ maicūr, pampāy, pīrār(Berar), mattiya mākāṇam, vaṭa mēṟkup parōṭā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. kōmaṭṭic ceṭṭikaḷ tāy māmaṉ makaḷai maṇappar. 18ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭil kōmaṭṭikaḷiṉ tirumaṇattil vayatu mutirnta mātaṅkaṉ tāliyai ācīrvatippatu vaḻakkamāka viruntatu. itaṉāl mātaṅkarukkum kōmaṭṭikaḷukkum yātō toṭarpu irukkiṟateṉak terikiṟatu. mātaṅkaṉ kaliyāṇattai virumpāviṭil avaṉ kaliyāṇap pantaliṟ kaṭṭiyirukkum vāḻai maraṅkaḷai veṭṭi kaliyāṇattai niṟuttalām. ivvāṟē kammāḷar kaliyāṇaṅkaḷai niṟutta veṭṭiyāṉukku urimaiyuṇṭu. iv vaḻakkaṅkaḷāl kōmaṭṭikaḷ, kammāḷarkaḷuṭaiya nilaṅkaḷukku mātaṅkar veṭṭiyāṉ mutaliyōr atipatikaḷāyirun tārkaḷeṉat terikiṟatu. kōmaṭṭikaḷ veṟṟilaiyum pākkum vaittu mātaṅkarait kaliyāṇattukku aḻaippārkaḷ. kōmaṭṭikaḷiṉ kulateyvam kaṉṉikai ammā. it teyvattaik kuṟikkak karakattil taṇṇīr nirappi vaikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. mātaṅkar tamatu teyvam kaṉṉi eṉak kūṟi ataṉai mātaṅki eṉṉum peyar koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvar. tirumaṇattil tāli kurukkaḷ allatu maṇamakaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
kōmaṇāṇṭi: āṇṭikaḷuḷ oru piriviṉar.
kōmāḷi: oṭṭiyaruḷ oru vakuppiṉar
kōyā: ilākkā tīviluḷḷa tōṇic contakkārarākiya cōṉaka māppiḷḷaimār, taṅkaḷ peyarōṭu kōyā eṉṉum peyaraic cērttuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
kōyi: kōyi allatu kōyā eṉpōr kōtāvarikku vaṭakkē yuḷḷa malaikaḷil vāḻvōrāvar. ivarkaḷ koṇṭar vakuppaic cērntavarkaḷākak karutappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ nālu āṇṭukaḷ varaiyil ōr iṭattil taṅkiyiruntu payiriṭuvārkaḷ; piṉ piṟitoru iṭattukkuc ceṉṟu viṭuvar. kōyic cātiyiṉariṉ kaliyāṇa vaḻakkam mika viyappuṭaiyatu. oruvaṉukkup peṇ vēṇṭiyiruntāl avaṉ taṉ peṟṟōraiyum naṇparaiyum peṇ vīṭṭukku aṉuppi muṭivu ceykiṟāṉ. peṇ viṟaku poṟukkavō taṇṇīr eṭukkavō varum camayam pārttuk kaṇavaṉ pakutiyār peṇṇait tūkki maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭukkuk koṇṭu celvārkaḷ. peṇ vīṭṭārukku ic ceytiyai aṟivittapiṉ maṇakkiriyai naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. kōyar perumpālum pala peṇkaḷai maṇappar. tāḻnta vakuppāṉuṭaṉ cērkkai vaittiruk kum peṇṇiṉ nākkil poṉ kampi kāyccic cuṭappaṭum. paṉai ōlaiyiṉāl vil vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭṭa ēḻu vilkaḷukkūṭāka avaḷai nuḻaiyac ceyta piṉ ōlaiyaic cuṭṭu avaḷ cuttañ ceyyappaṭuvāḷ.
kuḻantai piṟantu ēḻāvatu nāḷ ataṟkup peyariṭappaṭum. piṇaṅkaḷ cuṭappaṭum. iṟantavaraic cuṭṭa cāmpalaip putaittu av viṭattil nērāṉa kallai naṭṭu ataṉ mītu taṭṭaik kal vaikkappaṭum. av vaḻiyāṟ celvōr ataṉmītu ciṟitu pukaiyilaiyai vaippar. ivarkaḷ tām vīmacēṉaṉukkum kāṭṭuc cātip peṇṇukkum tōṉṟiya cantatiyiṉar eṉak kūṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ narapali yiṭuvar. narapali ippoḻutu taṭaiceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṉita palikkup patil kuraṅku paliyiṭappaṭukiṟatu. vaicūri nōyai uṇṭākkum tēvatai muṭiyāḷ ammā eṉappaṭum. cāḷammā, kommāḷammā mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷaiyum avarkaḷ vaṇaṅkuvar. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ āvikaḷum vaṇaṅkappaṭum. nōykaḷaip pēykaḷ uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṉum nampikkai ivarkaḷiṭamuṇṭu.
kōyilārpiḷḷai: ivarkaḷ vaṉṉiyaril oru piriviṉar; nūlaṇivar.
kōyiṟṟampirāṉ: ivarkaḷ vaṭa tiruvitāṅkūrilum kocciyi lum kāṇappaṭum cattiriya vakuppiṉar. paḻaiya cācaṉaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷaik kōyilatikārikaḷeṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷ kollamāṇṭu 300il cēramāṉ perumāḷāl pepūri(Beypore)liruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭārkaḷ. āṇkaḷ vēṇāṭṭuc civarūpam eṉṟu aḻaikkap paṭuvar. ivarkaḷ araca kuṭumpap peṇkaḷai maṇantārkaḷ eṉṉum paḻaṅkatai uḷḷatu. ivarkaḷukku urimai peṇ vaḻi. nampūtirip pirāmaṇar, ivar peṇkaḷai maṇappar.
kōlā: teluṅku uḻavar kōlā eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ ettaṉai maṉaiviyarai vēṇṭumāyiṉum maṇakkalām. peṇkaḷ iṟavukkai aṇivatillai. kaṇavaṉai iḻanta peṇkaḷ kaivaḷai kaḷai uṭaippatillai. piḷḷaiyaip peṟṟa kōlāp peṇ 90 nāḷ tīṭṭuk kāppāḷ.
kōlāyaṉ: ivarkaḷ teṉ kaṉṉaṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. vaṭa malaiyāḷattil ivarkaḷ ūrāḷi eṉappaṭuvar. āyaṉ, kōl āyaṉ, māriyaṉ allatu erumāṉ (erumā - erumai) eṉa ivarkaḷuṭ pala pirivukaḷuṇṭu. kōlāyariṉ kuru mūttavaṉ allatu potuvaṉ eṉap paṭuvaṉ. avaṉ perumpālum aracarāl teriyappaṭuvaṉ. kōlāyar peṇkaḷ paruvamaṭaiya muṉ tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭattuvar. tantai tālikaṭṭuvāṉ. pūppaṭainta peṇ mūṉṟu nāḷ tīṭṭuk kāppāḷ.
kōliyaṉ: necavu ceyyum paṟaiya vakuppiṉar ip peyar peṟuvar. iv vakuppiṉar perumpālum tañcāvūr maturaip pakuti kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. cila paṟaiyarukkuc cāmpāṉ eṉṉum paṭṭap peyaruṇṭu. īcaṉ eṉpatum avarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar. tirumaṇak kālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭap peyar collappaṭutal vēṇṭum. kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtari peṇṇukkut tāli kaṭṭuvāḷ.
kauṭō: kañcattil kāṇappaṭum oriya uḻavar ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷil āṇkaḷ tāymāmaṉ makaḷaik kaliyāṇañ ceytu koḷvar; cakōtariyiṉ makaḷaik kaliyāṇañ ceyvatumuṇṭu. ēḻu kaṇavarai maṇantavaḷ pettammā eṉa mariyātai ceyyappaṭuvaḷ.
cakkiliyaṉ: cakkiliyar teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantōrkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ ellāc cātiyiṉarilum pārkkat tāḻntō rāvar. āvaracañ ceṭiyai ivarkaḷ paricuttamuṭaiyatākak koḷvar. tirumaṇattiṉ muṉ tāliyai ac ceṭiyiṉ kiḷaikaḷ oṉṟil kaṭṭuvar. ivarkaḷ cerupput taippar. paṟaiyar tām cakkiliyarilum uyarntava rākak koḷvar. maturai vīraṉ, māriyammaṉ, tiraupati, kaṅkammā mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷai ivarkaḷ vaḻipaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ tirumaṇaṅ kaḷai vaḷḷuvak kurukkaḷ naṭatti vaippar.
cattiriyaṉ : cattiriyar eṉṉum pirivu tirāṭaviṭarukkuriyataṉṟu. āṉāl cattiriyar eṉṉum talaippiṉkīḻ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷōr tirāviṭarkaḷāvar. vaṉṉiyar, cāṇār, payirt toḻilāḷari loru piriviṉar, kaḷḷiṟakkuvōr mutaliṉōrum tammaic cattiriyar eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. teṉkaṉṉaṭatti luḷḷa marāṭṭiyar cilarum tirunel vēlic cāṇārum tammai akkiṉi kulac cattiriyar eṉak kūṟikkoḷ var. maicūr āṭkaṇakku aṟikkaiyil aracukkaḷ, irācaputtirar, kūrkkar, cīkkiyar, ākiyōr cattiriyar eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. aracukkaḷ eṉpōr maicūriluḷḷa araca kuṭumpattiṉar. malaiyāḷatti luḷḷa cattiriyar nāṉku pirivukaḷākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. kōyil paṇṭālā (paṇṭālā- paṇṭacālai) irācā, tampāṉ, tirumuppāt eṉpaṉa ap pirivukaḷākum. tampāṉiṉ peṇkaḷ tampurāṭṭikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. kaṉṉaṭa parapparaik kataiyiṉpaṭi kōyiṟṟam pirāṉmār cēramāṉ perumāṉiṉ marumakkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷil iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu kuṭumpaṅkaḷ piriṭṭiṣ malaiyāḷattiluḷḷa pēcpūril (Bejpore) vāḻntārkaḷ. malaiyāḷam āṇṭu 300 varaiyil ik kuṭumpam oṉṟi lirunta āṇkaḷ vēṇāṭ cuparūpatti (tiruvaṉantapura araca kuṭumpam) ṉiṭaiyē maṇañ ceyvataṟku aḻaikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. kollamāṇṭu 963il tippuc cultāṉ malaiyāḷattiṉ mītu paṭai yeṭuttapōtu āliyamkōṭu kōvilakattaic cērnta aintu peṇ kaḷum mūṉṟu āṇkaḷum tiruvitāṅ kūrukku ōṭicceṉṟu taṅkiṉar. tampurāṉkaḷ, aracarkaḷ vīṭukaḷ koṭṭāram allatu kōvilakam eṉappaṭum. tampāṉ allatu tirumāl pāṭikaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷ kōvilakam, maṭam eṉap paṭum. cattiriyap peṇkaḷiṉ ceruttāli, entiram, kuḻal eṉṉum aṇi kaḷai peṇkaḷ ciṟappāka aṇivārkaḷ. tampāṉ. tirumālpāṭikaḷ araca kuṭumpaṅkaḷil vēlaikkārarāvar. malaiyāḷa cattiriyariṉ vīṭukaḷ tēvārapuram eṉappaṭum. kōyil tampurāṉmār vicuvāmittira kōttirattavar. aracar pārkkava kōttirattiṉar. ivarkaḷ tamatu kūṭṭattuḷ maṇam muṭippatillai. kōyil tampurāṭ ṭikaḷ, nampūtiri āṭavarkaḷai maṇañ ceyvar. kōyil tampurāṉkaḷ aracariṭam peṇ koḷvar. aracar nāyarp peṇkaḷai maṉaiviyarāka vaittirukkalām. tampāṉ. tirumāl pāṭikaḷum ivvāṟē ceyvar. irāṇikaḷum paṇ ṭālaṅkaḷum nampūtiri āṇkaḷai maṇappar. tampāṉ, tirumālpāṭi peṇkaḷ entap pirāmaṇaṉuṭaṉum vāḻvar. tampāṉ, tirumālpāṭi, paṇṭālam peṇkaḷukku āriyappaṭṭar tāli kaṭṭuvar. vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇam purivar. tampurāṭṭiyiṉ tirumaṇattil peṇkaḷ cērntu pirāmaṇiyap pāṭṭup pāṭuvārkaḷ. koḷkaiyaḷa vil peṇkaḷē cottukkuriyavarāvar. mūtta cakōtaraṉē cottai mēṟpārppaṉ. cottup piriviṉai ceyyappaṭutal kiṭaiyātu. cottu ivarkaḷ ellōriṉ vāḻkkaikkāka viṭappaṭum.
camakārar: teṉkaṉṉaṭattil vāḻum tōlil vēlai cey vōr ivarkaḷāvar. tōl pataṉiṭum vēlai perumpālum ivar kaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu.
camaya: ivarkaḷ vaiṇava āccāriyarkaḷāvar. tirumaṇak kiriyai kaḷaiyō camayak kiriyaikaḷaiyō ivarkaḷukkut takkaṇai koṭātu purital kūṭātu. muṟkāla aracar ovvoru paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷilum camayāc cāriyā eṉṉum nilaiyam niṟuviyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ varuvāy perumpālum kaṟpil tavaṟiya peṇkaḷai viṟpatāl kiṭaittatu. ap peṇkaḷ cakkārp peṇkaḷ eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. pirāmaṇap peṇkaḷum kōmaṭṭip peṇkaḷum viṟkappaṭṭilar. cātiyiliruntu vilakkappaṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ kaiyil viyapicārikaḷ eṉṟu cūṭṭukkōliṉāl eḻutappaṭṭatu. maṟṟac cātip peṇkaḷ viṟkappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. paṅkaḷūril airōppiyar kālam varaiyil iv vakaip peṇkaḷ vāḻum periya kaṭṭaṭam paṭṭiṉattilē iruntatu. 1833il iv vaḻakkam araciṉarāl oḻikkappaṭṭatu.
calaṅkukkāraṉ: itu mīṉpiṭikārarukkum muttukkuḷikāraruk kum vaḻaṅkum peyar.
cavarar: kañcam pakutikaḷil vāḻum malaiccātiyiṉar ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ moḻi muṇṭā iṉattaic cērntatu eṉak kirīrcaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷār. āṇkaḷ iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu maṉaiviyarai maṇappar. peṇtāṉ virumpiṉāl kaṇavaṉai viṭṭup pirintu viṭalām. ataṉait taṭukka muṭiyātu. cavarariṉ viruntukaḷil āṇ kaḷum peṇkaḷum cērkkai vaittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. vitavaikaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉai maṇappar. cavara iḷaiñaṉ oruvaṉ maṇañ ceytukoḷḷa virumpiṉāl avaṉ oru kuṭattil kaḷḷai eṭuttuk koṇṭu taṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉ peṇṇiṉ tantai vīṭṭukkuc celvāṉ. peṇṇiṉ tantai kaḷḷai ēṟṟuk koṇṭāl atu peṇṇaik koṭuppataṟku aṭaiyāḷamākum. ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇap purōkitaraik koṇṭu etuvum ceyvippatillai. iṟantavariṉ uṭal cuṭappaṭum; aṭuttanāḷ taṇṇīr teḷittu neruppai aṇaittuk karukiya elumpukaḷai eṭuttu iraṇṭaṭi āḻattiṟ putaittu av viṭattil ciṟu kuṭicaiyiṭuvar. iṟantavaraik kuṟittu maraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻ nēriya kaṟkaḷai nāṭṭuvar. kaṟkaḷ perumpālum oṉṟaṟai aṭi mutal nālu aṭivarai uyarntirukkum. viyapicāram ceypavarkaḷuḷ peṇkaḷ taṇṭikkappaṭa māṭṭārkaḷ; āṇkaḷ taṇṭikkappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷuḷ cātit talaivaṉ uṇṭu. avaṉē vaḻakkukaḷai vicārittut tīrppaḷippāṉ. makuvā (Mahua)eṉṉum oru vakaip pūviliruntu iluppaip (pū?) kaḷḷuc ceyvar.
cavalaikkāraṉ: itu mīṉpiṭikārarukku vaḻaṅkum peyar. cavalai eṉpatu cavaḷ; ōṭakkōlai uṇarttum ivarkaḷ vaṉṉiyar , cempaṭavarkaḷai ottavar. cilar payirt toḻil ceyvōrākavum cilar nākacuram vācippōrākavum irukkiṉṟaṉar. tirunelvēlip pakutiyil vāttiyakkārar cavalaikkārarum paṇikkarkaḷumāvar.
cākciyar: ivarkaḷ ampalavācikaḷuḷ oru piriviṉar. ivar kaḷiṟ peṇkaḷ tamatu cātikkuḷ maṇamuṭittukkoḷvar allatu nampūtirikaḷōṭu campantam vaittukkoḷvar. cākkiyar nampiyār cātiyāruḷ campantam vaittukkoḷḷalām. ivarkaḷukkuk kurumā ruṇṭu. ivarkaḷ vīṭukaḷil piṟappu iṟapput tīṭṭukaḷaip pirāmaṇak kurumār nīkkuvar. vaṭa tiruvitāṅkūril viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil cākkiyar kūttu mutaṉmaiyuṭaiyatu. ivarkaḷ kūttāṭuvataṟkeṉa amaikkap paṭṭa kaṭṭaṭam kūttampalam eṉappaṭum. cākkiyaṉ paḻaṅkāla muṟaiyil uṭuttukkoṇṭu mukkāli mītiruntu pāṭuvāṉ. avaṉukkup piṉṉāl nampiyār muḻavuṭaṉ niṟpār. naṅkaiyār eṉṉum nampiyārp peṇ muṉṉāl iruntu callariyāl tāḷam pōṭuvāḷ.
cāṇāṉ: ivar tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaḷ viṟkum cātiyiṉar. kōyil kaḷuḷ ivarkaḷ nuḻaital kūṭātu. cāṇār kōyilkaḷuḷ nuḻaiya vēṇṭumeṉa vātāṭiyatāl uḷnāṭṭuk kalakaṅkaḷ pala uṇṭāyiṉa. ivarkaḷai etirttōr maṟaccātiyiṉa rāvar. tiruvitāṅkūril kiṟittuva matattait taḻuviya cāṇārap peṇkaḷ mārpil tuṇiyaṇiyāmal irukkum vaḻakkai mīṟiyatāl kalakaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. ceṉṉait tēcātipati peṇkaḷ iṟavukkai aṇiyavum māṟāṭi iṭṭu mārpai maṟaittukkoḷḷavum urimai vaḻaṅkiṉār. nāṭāṉ, kirāmaṇi eṉpaṉa ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar. cāṇār eṉṉum peyar cāṟu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. cāṇār eṉpatu avarkaḷ maram ēṟap payaṉpaṭuttum cāṇ nār kāraṇamāka vanta peyar eṉpāru muḷar.
cāṇi allatu cāṇi vāḷḷu: ivarkaḷ teluṅkuc cātik kulattiṉar. ivarkaḷ kōyiṟ paṇiviṭai ceyvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ teṟkēyuḷḷa tācikaḷaip pōlak kōyilukku nērntu viṭappaṭṭa varkaḷāvar. akampaṭiyāri loru piriviṉarum cāṇi eṉappaṭuvar.
cātāṉi: piccaikkārap paṇṭāraṅkaḷum maṟṟac cātikaḷi liruntu cātiyāl vilakkappaṭṭōrum ip pirivil cērvar. viyapicārip peṇkaḷum iv vakuppil cērvar. peṇkaḷ vaiṇavap peṇkaḷaip pōla uṭuttukkoḷvar. cātāṉiyar iṟavukkai uṟcavam eṉa oru viḻā naṭattuvar. ippoḻutu atu kantappoṭi uṟcavam eṉappaṭu kiṟatu. iv vuṟcavattil muṟkālattil iṭakkarāṉa kiriyaikaḷ naṭattap paṭṭaṉa. ippoḻutu kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkattukkup piṉ cantaṉap poṭiyai oruvar maṟṟavar mītu eṟivar. viḻāvukkup piṉ avarkaḷ vīṭṭukkut tirumpi vantu matu varuntuvārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ tamatu iṟavukkaikaḷaik kaḻaṟṟi oru ēṉattuḷ iṭuvārkaḷ. avaikaḷai āṇkaḷ āḷukku oṉṟāka eṭuppārkaḷ. enta iṟavukkai evaṉuṭaiya kaiyiṟ kiṭaik kiṟatōap peṇ aṉṟu avaṉuṭaiya maṉaiviyāvaḷ.
cāttiri: itu smārtta pirāmaṇariṉ paṭṭappeyar. tēvāṅku vakuppiṉarukkum ip paṭṭappeyar vaḻaṅkum.
cāmantaṉ: malaiyāḷa aracarum perumakkaḷum ic cātiyiṉa rāvar. cāmantaṉ eṉpataṟku oru pakutikku atikāri eṉpatu poruḷ. ivarkaḷ pūṇūlaṇivatillai. ivarkaḷukku makkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. peṇkaḷ pirāmaṇaraiyum cattiriyaraiyum campantam koḷvar. peṇkaḷ kōyil ammāmār eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ ceruttāli, entiram kuḻal eṉpavaikaḷait tirumaṇak kālattil aṇivar. cāmantaṉiṉ vīṭu koṭṭāram (araṇmaṉai) eṉappaṭum. pirāmaṇariṉ vīṭu maṭam eṉappaṭum.
cāliyar: malaiyāḷa necavut toḻilāḷar cāliyar eṉappaṭu var. ivarkaḷukkup potuvāṉ eṉṉum ampaṭṭaṉ uṇṭu. potu vāṉē cāliyariṉ purōkitaṉāvaṉ. ivarkaḷil cilarukku marumakkaḷ tāyamum, cilarukku makkaḷ tāyamum uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ vītikaḷilē vāḻ kiṉṟamaiyiṉ teruvar eṉavum aṟiyappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷukku iṟappup piṟapput tīṭṭu pattu nāṭkaḷukku uṇṭu. peṇ talaippūppu aṭain tāl peṇkaḷ avaḷaik kuḷattukku aḻaittuc ceṉṟu ilaikaḷāṟ ceyta ēṉaṅkaḷāl nīrai aḷḷi avaḷai muḻukkāṭṭuvar. piṉpu avaḷait teṉṉaṅkuruttāl ceyta aṟaiyil iruttuvar. avaḷiruk kum pāyaic cuṟṟi ariciyum nellum, teṉṉampūvum tēṅkāykaḷum vaikkappaṭum. mūṉṟām nāḷ mālai nērattil peruvaṇṇāṉ veḷutta māṟṟut tuṇi koṇṭuvaruvāṉ. avaṉukkuc ciṟitu nellum ariciyum koṭuppārkaḷ. avaṉ avaṟṟai ōr ilaiyil vaittup pūcai ceyvāṉ. piṉpu avaṉ tuṇikaḷai marat taṭṭil vaittut taṭṭait taṉatu talaimītu ēntuvāṉ. avaṉ cila vāḻttup pāṭalkaḷ pāṭiya piṉ at taṭṭai nilattil vaippāṉ. peṇṇiṉ uṟaviṉarākiya cila peṇkaḷ eriyum viḷakku, niṟaikuṭam, oru paṭi arici mutaliyavai kaḷaik koṇṭu palakaiyai mūṉṟu muṟai cuṟṟi varuvārkaḷ. aṭutta nāḷ peṇṇai muḻukkāṭṭuvārkaḷ. pāyil vaikkappaṭṭa ellāp poruḷkaḷum āṟṟil vīcappaṭum.
cāliyarukkut tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅku uṇṭu. pantal iṭappaṭu kiṉṟatu. oru palāp palakaiyiṉ mītu koḷuttiya viḷakku, veṟṟilai, pākku, oru paṭi paccaiyarici mutaliyaṉa vaikkappaṭum. peṇ taṉatu valatu kaiyil oru pōli ammaṉ pāvaiyai vaittuk koṇṭu oru palakai mītu iruppāḷ. potuvaṉukku oru piṭi veṟṟilaiyum oru paṇamum koṭuppārkaḷ. avaṉ maṇavāḷaṉiṭam tāliyai eṭuttuk koṭuppāṉ. avaṉ ataip peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭuvāṉ. kaliyāṇattukku mutalnāḷ maṇavāḷaṉ taṉatu āṇ cuṟṟattārōṭu maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭukkuc celvāṉ. aṅku viruntuk koṇṭāṭṭam naṭaipeṟum. peṇ karuppamaṭaintu ēḻāvatu mātam puḷikkuṭi eṉṉum caṭaṅku naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. peṇṇiṉ cakōtaraṉ puḷiyaṅ kiḷai oṉṟaik koṇṭu varuvāṉ. ilaikaḷai ellām uruviya piṉ atu muṟṟattil naṭappaṭum. puḷiyamilaiyiliruntu piḻintu eṭukkap paṭṭa cāṟu ēḻu tēṅkāykaḷiluḷḷa iḷanīril kalakkappaṭum. piṉpu kuṭumpattil mutiya peṇ atil ciṟitai ak karuppavati kuṭikkumpaṭi koṭuppāḷ. ivvāṟu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. iṟanta varkaḷ putaikkappaṭuvar. iṟantavaṉiṉ makaṉ putuppāṉaiyil taṇ ṇīraic cuṭalaikkuk koṇṭu celvāṉ. avaṉ kuṭattōṭu piṇattaic cuṟṟi vantu ataṉai eṟintu viṭuvāṉ. piṇattaip putaitta iṭattil mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷ vaikkappaṭum.
cāliyar: ivarkaḷ teluṅku necavāḷar. ivar tammaic cēṉāpatiyar eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ cātit talaivaṉ cēṉātipati eṉappaṭuvāṉ. paṭṭuc cāliyar, patuma cāliyar eṉa ivaril iruvakai yiṉaruṇṭu. paṭṭuc cāliyar pūṇūlaṇivar. koru nāṭu, aiyampēṭṭai mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻum necavāḷarum cāliyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ tirunelvēlic cāliyaruṭaiyavum, kaikkōḷaruṭaiyavum paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟuvar. ceṅkaṟpaṭṭiluḷḷa cāliyar peritum kaikkōḷarāvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kulateyvam muttāṭci ammaṉ.
civiyār: ivarkaḷ pallakkuk kāvuñ cātiyiṉar. iṭaiyaril oru piriviṉarukkum ip peyaruṇṭu. viyapicārikaḷ tūṇiṟ kaṭṭip puḷiyam milāṟukaḷāl aṭikkappaṭuvar. paruvamaṭaintu maṇamākātavarkaḷ maṇamāṉāl paṉai ōlaiyil vēṇṭiya āṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷai vaittu avarkaḷukku maṇakkiriyai naṭattappaṭum.
ciṟukuṭi: itu kaḷḷariṉ kirāmam allatu ūr.
ciṟutāli: ivarkaḷ kaikkōḷar maṟavarkaḷuḷ kāṇappaṭum piriviṉar.
ciṟpar: ivarkaḷuḷ pañcammāḷaril oru piriviṉar; kaṟkaḷil vēlai ceypavar.
cīriya kiṟittavar: ki.pi. 52il tōmas ñāṉiyār koṭuṅ kōḷūril vantiṟaṅkiṉār. avviṭam kuciṟi allatu muciṟikkōṭu eṉappaṭum. ak kālattil piṉīciyarum āppirikka vaṇikarum vāṇikat tiṉ poruṭṭu aṅku vantārkaḷ. tōmas ñāṉiyār intiyāvil palaraik kattōlikka matattait taḻuvac ceytār. iṟutiyil ivar pirāmaṇa matattiṉar oruvarāl īṭṭiyāl eṟintu kollappaṭṭu mayilāppūriluḷḷa cāntōmil aṭakkañ ceyyappaṭṭār. ivvāṟu paḻaṅkatai vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iraṇṭām tiyatōyccuk (Theodoisus) kālattil pālastīṉattil yūtar īvu irakkamiṉṟik kollappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. appoḻutu avarkaḷiṟ palar vantu intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟi ṉārkaḷ. iṉṉum cilar ki.pi. 345 varaiyil cīriyāviliruntu vantārkaḷ. vīrarākavac cakkaravartti (774) stāṉuravi kuppataṉ (824) mutaliyō raḷitta paṭṭayaṅkaḷil cīriya kiṟittavarkaḷaip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vīrarākavac cakkaravartti aḷitta paṭṭaiyam cīriya kiṟittavarkaḷiṭattil uḷḷatu. paṭṭaiyattil caṅkumuttirai poṟik kappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ip paṭṭaiyattāl cēramāṉ, ulōkap peruñ cēvaṭi eṉṉum iravik koṟṟaṉukku maṇimaṅkalam eṉṉum paṭṭa maḷittuḷḷāṉ. viḻākkāla uṭai aṇital, vaṇika urimai, parivāram vaittuk koḷḷutal, aintu vāttiyaṅkaḷ, caṅku mutaliya vāttiyaṅ kaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttutal, pakalil pantam piṭittal, nila pāvāṭai virittal, pallakku vaittiruttal, aracaṉaip pōla ulāvarutal, alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa vil vaittiruttal, vīṭṭu vāyilai vil vaṭivāka amaittal pōṉṟa urimaikaḷ avaṉukku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ip paṭṭaiyattai varaintavaṉ cēramāṉ ulōkap perun taṭṭāṉ nampi caṭaiyaṉ. iravik koṟṟaṉ cīriya kiṟittavaruḷ oruvaṉ. cīriya kiṟittavar 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭaiyil perumai peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ uṭaiyap pērūrait talainakarākak koṇṭa kiṟittuva aracaṉāl āḷappaṭṭārkaḷ eṉṉum cevi vaḻic ceyti uṇṭu. iṉṟum cila cīriya kiṟittuvar intu ālayaṅkaḷukkuk kāṇikkaik koṭuppar. pirāmaṇarallāta cila intukkaḷum cīriya kiṟittuva ālayaṅkaḷukkuk kāṇikkai koṭuppar. cīriya kiṟittavar tamatu kuḻantaikaḷukkuc cātakameḻuti vaippar. tirumaṇattil maṇa makaṉ peṇṇukkut tāli tarippāṉ. kaṇavaṉ maraṇamāṉāl maṉaivi tāliyaik kaḷaintu viṭuvāḷ. maraṇattukkup piṉ pattu allatu patiṉaintu nāṭkaḷukkup pular eṉṉum tīṭṭuk kāppārkaḷ. āṇṭil oru muṟai iṟanta nāḷ koṇṭāṭappaṭum.
cūli: kaṉṉaṭat tēvatācikaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar.
cekkaṉ: vaṉṉiyaṉukku itu maṟṟoru peyar.
cekkāṉ: ivarkaḷ teṉmalaiyāḷattil vaṭṭakkāṭar eṉavum vaṭamalaiyāḷattil vāṇiyar eṉavum paṭuvar. cekkār vāṇiyarilum pārkka uyarntavarkaḷāvar. cekkār tīṇṭuvatāl nāyarukkut tīṭṭu uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ nāyar cātiyaṉa ruṭaiyavai pōlvaṉa.
ceṭṭi: necavukārar, cekkār mutaliyōr itaṉait tamakkup paṭṭap peyarākak koḷvar. pēricceṭṭi, nakarac ceṭṭi, kācukkārac ceṭṭi, nāṭṭuk kōṭṭaic ceṭṭi mutaliyōr ceṭṭi vakuppiṟ cilarāvar. (ceṭṭikaḷ tammait taṉavaiciyar eṉak kūṟuvar. taṉavaici yariṉ pirivukaḷ kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuc ceṭṭi, ēḻilaic ceṭṭi, iḷaiyāttuc kuṭicceṭṭi, cōḻapurattuc ceṭṭi, puḷiyaṅkuṭic ceṭṭi, pūvaḷḷuk kuṭic ceṭṭi, tiruvāppūrc ceṭṭi, karuppūrac ceṭṭi, kāvēric ceṭṭi, vaḷaiyal ceṭṭi, mañcappaṭṭuc ceṭṭi eṉac caimaṉ kācic ceṭṭiyavarkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvar.)
cempaṭavaṉ: tamiḻnāṭṭil mīṉ piṭikārar cempaṭavarāvar. ivarkaḷ nāṭṭāṉ, kavaṇṭaṉ, maṇiyakkāraṉ, pākuttaṉ, piḷḷai mutaliya paṭṭappeyarai vaittukkoḷvar. malaiyaṉūril ellāc cempaṭavarum tammaip pūcārikaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. citamparattilē kuṟikkappaṭṭa oru nāḷil cempaṭavar cuvāmiyaic cumantu ūrvalam varuvar. ataṟkāka avarkaḷukkuk kūliyum poṅkalum koṭukkappaṭum. ammaṉ ūrvalam varumpōtu cempaṭavar ammaṉai niṟutti āṭai koṭuppārkaḷ. avarkaḷ talaivaṉ nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ eṉappaṭu vāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya utavikkāraṉ caṅkatip piḷḷai allatu caṅkatik kāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. paṭaku ōṭṭum cempaṭavar kaṅkaiyai vaḻipaṭuvar. pūppaṭainta peṇ kāvalākak kaiyil irumpai vaittiruppāḷ. peṇ karuppamāki ēḻāvatu mātam mutukunīr kuttal eṉṉum caṭaṅku naṭattappaṭum. peṇ kuṉintu niṟkumpōtu uṟaviṉar veṟṟilaiyiṉ nuṉiyāl avaḷ mutukiṉ mītu pāl vārppārkaḷ.
cemmāṉ: ivarkaḷ paṟaiyaril oru piriviṉar; tōlil vēlai ceyvōr. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ ceyyum vēlaiyaic cakkiliyar purikiṉṟaṉar.
cerumāṉ: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattil uḻavuttoḻil ceyyum vēlaiyāḷar. vaṭa malaiyāḷattil ivarkaḷ pulaiyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ kāṇiyāḷarāl aṭimaikaḷāy viṟkavum vāṅkavum paṭṭārkaḷ. 1862il ivvāṟu viṟṟu vāṅkutal caṭṭa virōtamāk kappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ cātimāṉkaḷukku 30 aṭi tūrattil vantāl tīṭṭu uṇṭākum. cerumār, pirāmaṇar kirāmaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷai aṇukutal kūṭātu. iṉṟum cerumār cantaikaḷuḷ nuḻaital kūṭātu. ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇarukkum nāyarukkum aṟupattu nāṉkaṭi tūrattil niṟṟal vēṇṭum.
cēṇiyaṉ: necavu toḻilāḷar cēṇiyareṉappaṭuvar. kāñcī purattil cēṇiyār iliṅka matattiṉarāvar.
cēṉaikkuṭaiyār: ivarkaḷ veṟṟilai payiriṭuvar. ivarkaḷ ilai eṉavum, koṭikkāṟ piḷḷaikaḷ eṉavum aṟiyappaṭuvar. mūppaṉ, piḷḷai eṉṉum paṭṭap peyarkaḷum ivarkaḷuḷ vaḻaṅkum. ivarkaḷ vīṭukaḷil paṟaiyar, ampaṭṭar, vaṇṇār uṇṇamāṭ ṭārkaḷ.
coṇṭi: ōriya vakuppiṉaruḷ kaḷ viṟpōr ivarkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ arici, paṉaṅkaṭṭi, paṉaṅkaḷ, iruppaip pū mutaliyavaṟṟi liruntu cārāyam vaṭippar. talaip pūppu eytiya peṇ, nāṉku ampukaḷai naṭṭuk kayiṟṟāṟ ṟoṭuttuk kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭattil viṭappaṭuvāḷ. ēḻāvatu nāḷ avaḷukku muḻukkāṭṭappaṭum. pūppaṭaiyumuṉ peṇkaḷukku maṇamākum. iṟantavarkaḷ putaikkappaṭu vārkaḷ. maraṇattīṭṭup pattu nāṭkaḷukkuṇṭu. peṇkaḷ macca māmicam uṇṇārkaḷ. kañcam pakutiyil peṇkaḷ pūppaṭainta piṉ maṇappar.
cōlakar: ivarkaḷ koccaik kaṉṉaṭa moḻi pēcum makkaḷ.
cōḻiyappaṭṭar: itu paṭṭap pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉarukku malaiyāḷattil vaḻaṅkum peyar.
cōṉakar: ivarkaḷ intut tāy tantaiyar marapil vanta mucalmāṉkaḷāvar. cōṉakaṉ eṉṉum peyar arāpiyaṉaik kuṟikkum. malaiyāḷattu māppiḷḷaimār cōṉaka māppiḷḷaimār eṉappaṭuvar. kirēkkaraik kuṟikkum yavaṉareṉṉum collukkup patil cōṉakar eṉṉum col vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cōṉār: ivarkaḷ marātti moḻiyil oru piriviṉarākiya koṅkaṇam pēcum taṭṭār. ivarkaḷiṉ cātit talaivaṉ mukkiyas taṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ; ivaṉ vaḻakkukaḷai viḷaṅkit tīrppaḷippāṉ. oru kōttirattil ivarkaḷ maṇañ ceytu koḷvatillai. ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar ceṭṭi. ceṉṉaiyil 408 pērukku oru taṭṭāṉuṇṭu. iṅkilāntil 1100-kku oru taṭṭāṉuṇṭu.
caurāṭṭirar: ivarkaḷ paṭṭunūṟkārar eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. ip peyar ivarkaḷ iruntu vanta caurāṭṭira nāṭu toṭarpuṭaiya peyar.
takkaṭō: ivarkaḷ malaiccātiyiṉarum, pirāmaṇarum kalantu utittōr . ivarkaḷ ceyappūrp pakkaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
taṅkar: marāṭṭiya iṭaiyar ip peyar peṟuvar.
taṅkalāṉ: ivarkaḷ paṟaiyaril oru piriviṉar. taṅkalāṉ eṉpataṟkuk kiṭṭa niṟkat takātavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ.
tacārikaḷ: ivarkaḷ oru vakai vaiṇavap paṇṭāraṅkaḷ. ivarkaḷai oru cātiyiṉar eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu.
taccanāṭaṉ mūppaṉ: itu kuṟiccāṉkaḷukkum nīlakirik kuṟumparkaḷiṟ cilarukkum vaḻaṅkum peyar.
taccaṉ: ivarkaḷ maravēlai ceyvōr. paṟaiyaril oru piriviṉarum ip peyar peṟuvar.
taṇṭappulaiyar: ivarkaḷ teṉ malaiyāḷattil vāḻum pulaiyaril oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ taṇṭak koṭiyai aṟuttup piṉṉiya āṭaiyai uṭuppar. ip pulaik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ illaṅkaḷākak pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru illattil uḷḷavarkaḷ atē kuṭumpattil maṇam muṭittal ākātu. ivarkaḷ kuṭiyirukkumiṭam maṉṟam eṉap paṭum. ivarkaḷ cūriyaṉaip pārttu “cūriyaṉaṟiya kaṇ keṭṭup pōka” eṉṟu collic cattiyañ ceyvar. ivarkaḷ aiñcu tampurākkaḷ eṉap pāṇṭavarai vaḻipaṭuvar; iṟantavarukkuk koḷḷik kuṭam uṭaippar; piṇattukku vāykkarici pōṭuvar. iṟantavariṉ āvi cāvār eṉappaṭum.
taṇṭar : malaiyāḷattilum , vaḷḷuva nāṭṭilum pālaik kāṭṭilum vāḻum īḻavarukku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. ivarkaḷiṟ peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavarai maṇappar. ūrāḷi eṉpatu ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ vēḷāṉ eṉavum paṭuvar. cātimāṉkaḷōṭu pēcum pōtu ivarkaḷ tammaik kuḻiyar (kuḻiyil vāḻvōr) eṉpar. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum neṟṟiyil piṟaiyum oru puḷḷiyum paccai kuttikkoḷvar. ivarkaḷuḷ kurumār mārttāṇṭa kuruppukkaḷ eṉap paṭuvar. kuruppukkaḷ ampaṭṭaru māvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ pattira kāḷi. peṇkaḷ ēḻu allatu eṭṭu vayatākavirukkum pōtu tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭattappaṭum. ik kaliyāṇam kaḻuttuk keṭṭi eṉappaṭum. maṇamakaṉ maccāmpi eṉappaṭuvāṉ. iṟappavarkaḷuḷ kuṭumpattil vayatiṉ mūttavar māttiram iṟantāl cuṭappaṭuvar ivarkaḷukku maraṇat tīṭṭu pattu nāḷ uṇṭu. iṟantavariṉ kiriyaikaḷ kaṭaṟkaraiyil naṭattap paṭum. iṟantavarkaḷukku eḷḷōṭu kalanta uṇavu koṭukkappaṭum. 
taṇṭāṉ: ivarkaḷ tīyar. kirāmattil talaimuṟaiyāka varum talaiyārikaḷ. talaiyāri aracaṉāl niyamikkappaṭuvāṉ.
tampalar: teluṅku pēcum kōyiṟ kurumār ip peyar peṟuvar. kōtāvari, kiruṭṭiṇā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇarka ḷākavum, teluṅku nāṭṭil cūttirarākavum karutappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ pūṇūlaṇintu koḷvar.
tampi: itu tiruvitāṅkūr nāyarukku vaḻaṅkum mariyātaip paṭṭappeyar. tiruvitāṅkūr aracariṉ piḷḷaikaḷum tampi eṉap paṭuvar.
tampirāṉ: tiruvāṭutuṟai, mayilam (teṉārkkāṭu) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷaip pārkkum paṇṭāraṅkaḷ tampirāṉ eṉappaṭuvar.
tampurāṉ: tiruvitāṅkūril vāḻum oru kūṭṭattiṉar tampurāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ irukkum iṭappeyarkaḷāl vēṟupaṭutti aṟiyappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ vaṭa malaiyāḷattiluḷḷa kōlāṭṭu nāṭṭi liruntu vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ araca kuṭumpattaic cērnta peṇkaḷuk kut tālik kaṭṭu allatu paḷḷikkaṭṭuc ceytu avarkaḷōṭu kaṇavar pōla vāḻvar. araca kuṭumpattiluḷḷa āṭavar cūttirap peṇṇaik koḷvar. peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉ iṟantuviṭṭāl allatu ētum kāraṇattai muṉṉiṭṭup pirintu viṭṭāl avaḷ iṉṉoru kōyiṟṟampurāṉaik koḷḷalām. ivarkaḷiṉ kurukkaḷ malaiyāḷap pōṟṟikaḷāvar. tiruvitāṅkūr araca kuṭumpattil mutaṟpeṇṇukku ilakkumi eṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatu peṇṇukkup pārvati eṉṟum peyariṭappaṭum.
tarumarācar: ivarkaḷ vaṭa ārkkāṭṭiluḷḷa iruḷaril oru piriviṉar.
talaiyāri: ivarkaḷ mutaṉmaiyāṉa kirāmak kāvalar. teluṅku nāṭṭil muttirācar kirāmak kāvalarkaḷāvar. avarkaḷ talāri vālu eṉappaṭuvar.
talaivar: itu paravariṉ paṭṭappeyar. cātit talaivaṉeṉpatu tirunelvēli muttukkuḷikārar talaivaṉukku vaḻaṅkum peyar.
tāci: nampūtirip peṇkaḷiṉ pirāmaṇarallāta paṇippeṇ ip peyar peṟuvāḷ. ivaḷ tēvatāciyil vēṟāṉavaḷ.
tāy: itu vaṭa intiyāviṉiṉṟum vanta caiṉariṉ paṭṭap peyar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum vaṇikarāvar.
tārvāṭ: ivarkaḷ marumakkaḷ tāyak kuṭumpattil oru tāy vaṭṭattaic cērntōr.
tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam: itu nāyarp peṇkaḷ paruvamaṭaiyu muṉ avarkaḷukku naṭattappaṭum kaliyāṇam. marumakkaḷ tāyamuṭaiya āṇpeṇ eṉpavarkaḷiṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷ taḷarntavai. ik kaliyāṇam tēvatācikaḷukkuc ceyyappaṭum kaliyāṇam pōṉṟatu.
tikamparar: ivarkaḷ muḻu nirvāṇam paricuttattukku aṭai yāḷameṉak koḷḷum caiṉar.
tirāviṭ: teṉṉintiya pirāmaṇar tirāviṭ eṉappaṭuvar.
tirumuṭi: ceṅkal vēlai ceyvōr tirumuṭiyāḷar eṉap paṭuvar. cēlam, kōyamputtūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ perum pālum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ oḻukkattaḷarvuṭaiyar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum vēṭṭuvar allatu kaikkōḷarāvar. kōyi lukku nērntu viṭappaṭum kaikkōḷap peṇkaḷ kaṭavuḷukkuk kaliyāṇam ceyyappaṭṭavarkaḷākak karutappaṭuvar.
tīpō: ivarkaḷ poṇṭāri vakuppil oru piriviṉar.
tīyaṉ: tīyarum īḻavarum malaiyāḷattil kaḷḷiṟakkum cātiyiṉar. vaṭamalaiyāḷattil tīyap peṇkaḷukku airōppiyar toṭarpiṉāl piḷḷaikaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyam uṇṭu. ākavē piḷḷaikaḷ tāy vaṭṭattaic cērntavarkaḷāvar. muṟkālat til peṇkaḷ airōppiyaruṭaṉ vāḻtal iḻivākak karutappaṭa villai. kalvi aṟivu ēṟappeṟṟa ik kālattil atu iḻivākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. īḻavarum tīyarum ilaṅkaiyiliruntu ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṉum aitīkam uḷḷatu. īḻattu makkaḷ cilar malaiyāḷattil vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷeṉṟum avarkaḷ varumpōtu teṉṉaiyai (teṟkē uṇṭākum maram) koṇṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum avarkaḷ tīvār eṉappaṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, tīvār eṉpatē tīyār, tīyar eṉṟu āyiṟṟeṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nila campantamāṉa āvaṇaṅkaḷil tīyar īḻavar eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. tīyar īḻavarukkut tāḻntavarākak karutappaṭuvar. oruvaṉ cātiyiliruntu taḷḷappaṭṭāl vaṇṇāṉ ampaṭṭaṉ mutaliyōr avaṉukkuk kaṭamai ceyya maṟuppārkaḷ. vaṇṇārap peṇkaḷ perumpālum tīya vakuppaic cērntavarka ḷāvar. pūppiṉā luṇṭākum tīṭṭu, piḷḷaiyaip peṟṟa tīṭṭu mutaliyaṉa, nīrāṭiyapiṉ vaṇṇātti koṭukkum “māttu” (māṟṟu) uṭuppatāl nīṅkum. īḻavar māttaip paṟṟi aṟiyārkaḷ. tīyaṉ īḻavaṉ camaitta uṇavai uṇpāṉ. īḻavaṉ tīyaṉ camaitta uṇavai uṇṇa māṭṭāṉ. tīyar kuṭiyirupput tarai eṉappaṭum. ovvoru taraikkum ovvoru nāyar atikāriyum. tīyat taṇṭaṉum, cōtiṭaṉum, ampaṭṭa ṉum palavakait toḻilāḷarumiruppar. maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭāraiyum maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭāraiyum porunta vaittu maṇam oḻuṅku ceyvōr caṅkati eṉappaṭuvar. tantaiyiṉ kuṭumpam illam eṉavum, tāyiṉ kuṭumpam kulam eṉavum paṭum. orē illattil maṇaṅkaḷ naṭappa tillai. teṉ malaiyāḷattil maṇamakaṉ maṟpōrukkuc celkiṉṟa vaṉaip pōla araiyil āṭaiyaik kaṭṭikkoṇṭum, kaiyil vāḷ, kēṭakam eṉpavaṟṟaip piṭittuk koṇṭum, ivvāṟu uṭuttik koṇṭa iraṇṭu tōḻar pakkattē varak kūttāṭik koṇṭum pōvāṉ. īḻavaṉ oru pōtum vāḷ koṇṭu pōvatillai. kaliyāṇattil mukkiya pakuti vāyil tuṟa pāṭṭu. vaṭamalaiyāḷattil ivarkaḷ neṟṟiyilum tōḷilum tirunīṟu pūcikkoḷvar; poṉ kaṭukkaṉ aṇivar. iṭakkai mōtira viralil veḷḷi allatu pittaḷai mōtira maṇivar. palar tāyattuk kaṭṭikkoḷvar. muṟkālat til teṉmalaiyāḷat tīyar muḻaṅkālukku mēl āṭai uṭuttārkaḷ. ellōrum kātu kuttikkoṇṭaṉar. kātil kaṭukkaṉum araiyil tāyattum aṇintārkaḷ. araikkumēl yātum aṇiyavillai. iṉṟum peṇkaḷ mārpai maṟaippatu aritu. īḻavap peṇkaḷ nīla āṭaiyaip peritumuṭuppar. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ mārpai maṟait tum āṭaiyai aṇikiṟārkaḷ. tīyarp peṇkaḷil cilar aṇiyum tōṭu avarkaḷukkuriyataṉṟu. muṟkālattil pala tīyac cakōtarar kūṭi oru maṉaiviyai maṇantu vāḻvārkaḷ. iv vakai nikaḻcciyaip paṟṟiya cāṉṟukaḷ eḻuttu mūlam uḷḷaṉa. teṉ malaiyāḷattil ivarkaḷukku makkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. pūppu aṭaiyumuṉ peṇṇukkut tālik kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭaipeṟum. iṟantavaṉiṉ talai teṟkē irukkumpaṭi piṇam kiṭattappaṭukiṟatu. kaipperuviral kaḷum kālperuviralkaḷum cērttuk kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kālmāṭu talaimāṭukaḷil viḷakkukaḷ vaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟantavariṉ uṭal koḷuttappaṭṭāl cāmpal kaṭalil allatu āṟṟil koṭṭappaṭu kiṟatu. āṇṭil oru muṟai iṟantavariṉ niṉaivunāḷ koṇṭāṭap paṭukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu vīṭṭiṉ naṭuvē viḷakkuk koḷutti vaittup pakkattē taṇṇīrum iḷanīrum vaikkappaṭum. ivarkaḷ māṭṭu iṟaicciyai uṇpatillai. 
turuvāḷar: ivarkaḷ kaṭṭuk koṭukkiṟa (kaliyāṇam porutti vaikkiṟa) cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷukku vēṇṭāṉ eṉpatum maṟupeyar.
tulāpāram: itu tiruvitāṅkūr aracarāl ceyyappaṭum oru vakait tāṉam. aracaṉ taṉatu niṟaiyuḷḷa poṉṉaip pirāmaṇarukkut tāṉañ ceyvāṉ.
tulukkar (turukkiyar) : ip peyar cila camayaṅkaḷil makamatiyaraik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkappaṭum
tēcikar: ivarkaḷ paṇṭāraṅkaḷuḷ oru piriviṉar.
tēcāri: vaṭa ārkkāṭṭil ovvoru iṭattilumuḷḷa talaiyāri tēcāri eṉappaṭuvaṉ.
tēvaṭiyāḷ: tēvatāci
tēvatāci: tācikaḷil ēḻu vakaiyiṉarkaḷuṇṭu. (1) tattam - taṉṉaik kōyilukku oppaṭaittavaḷ. (2) vikkirakam - taṉṉaik kōyilukku viṟṟavaḷ (3) pirittiya - taṉ kuṭumpa naṉmaikkāka kōyiṟ paṇiviṭai ceypavaḷ. (4) patti kāraṇamākak kōyilai aṭaipavaḷ (5) tāṉē virumpi vantu cērpavaḷ (6) alaṅkārañ ceytu aracarāl kōyilukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭavaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ vēlai vikkiraṅkaḷuk kuc cāmarai vīcutal, kumpa ārātti koṇṭu cellutal, kaṭavuḷ ulāvarum pōtu āṭippāṭutal eṉpaṉa. 1004il irācarācaṉ kaṭṭiya periya tañcāvūrk kōyiliṉ nāṉku vītikaḷilum nāṉūṟu taḻukkuc cērippeṇkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟuti yil kāñcīpurattil nūṟu āṭal mātar iruntārkaḷ. maturai, kāñcī puram mutaliya kōyilkaḷil iṉṉum perun tokaiyiṉar kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷukkuk kōyil varuvāyi liruntu māṉiyaṅ kiṭaikkiṟatu. aptūr iracāk (Abdul Rasak) eṉṉum turukkiya tūtar 15ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vicayanakarattiliruntār. ip peṇkaḷ mūlam kiṭaikkum varuvāy nakarak kāvalar (Police) campaḷam mutaliyavaṟṟukkuc ceṉṟateṉa avar kūṟiyuḷḷār. ip peṇkaḷukkum vāḷukkum allatu kaṭavuḷukkum kōyilil kaliyāṇam naṭattappaṭukiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷiṉ kaḻuttil avarkaḷ kulattiluḷḷa āṭavar tāli kaṭṭu kiṉṟaṉar. tāci kulattavarkaḷukku āṇkaḷaip pōlavē peṇkaḷuk kum cotturimai uṇṭu. tāci kula āṭavar naṭṭuvar eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ tamatu peyaruṭaṉ piḷḷai eṉpataic cērttu vaḻaṅkuvar. tācikaḷil valaṅkai, iṭaṅkai eṉa iru pirivukaḷuṇṭu. valaṅkaiyiṉar iṭaṅkaik kammāḷar vīṭukaḷil pāṭavō āṭavō māṭṭārkaḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ kammāḷat tācikaḷ eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. teluṅku nāṭṭil kammāḷar pōkam allatu cāṇi eṉappaṭuvar. oriya nāṭṭut tācikaḷ kuṉi eṉappaṭuvar. pellāriyiṉ mēṟkut tālukkāvilum ataṉ ayalēyuḷḷa tārvar, maicūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum pōyar, piṉā cūs mutaliyavarkaḷuḷ āṇ kuḻantai illāta kuṭumpaṅkaḷuḷ oru peṇ kuḻantai tāciyākak kōyilukku viṭappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu caṭṭamuṇṭu. tiruvitāṅkūrt tācikaḷ tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷ, kuṭikkāri (vīṭṭuk kuriyavaḷ) allatu peṇkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. āṭavar tēvaṭi yāṉ allatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷāḷaṉ eṉappaṭuvar. tiruvitāṅ kūrt tācikaḷ vīṭukaḷil cēvikkap pōvatillai. tēvaṭiyāḷ mutumai yaṭaintuviṭṭāl tōṭu kaḷaiyum caṭaṅku naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. itu aracaṉatu araṇmaṉaiyil naṭakkum. piṉpu avaḷ tāykkiḻavi ākiviṭu vāḷ. kēraḷattil muṟakkuṭi ciṟappukkuṭi eṉa iruvakait tācikaḷ uḷar. muṟakkuṭip peṇkaḷ mukkiya kālaṅkaḷil māttiram cēvippar. tiruvitāṅkūrt tācikaḷiṉ cotturimai peṇkaḷaic cārkiṉṟatu.
tēvar: putuccēriyil kaṇṇāṭip poruḷkaḷil vāṇikañ ceyyum teluṅkar tēvar eṉappaṭuvar. kirāma tēvataikaḷukkup pūcai ceyyum pūcārikaḷum tēvar eṉappaṭuvar. maṟavariṉ paṭṭappeyar tēvaṉ. teṉ kaṉṉaṭattiṟ kaḷḷiṟakkuvōr tēvaṟukaḷu (tēvariṉ makkaḷ) eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ ilaṅkaiyiliruntu vantavarkaḷākak karutappaṭuvar.
tēvāṅkar: ceṉṉai mākāṇam muḻumaiyilum kāṇappaṭum tuḷuvum kaṉṉaṭamum pēcum necavut toḻilāḷar ip peyar peṟuvar. kōyamputtūrp pakutiyil ivarkaḷ ceṭṭukkāraṉ eṉappaṭu vārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ cauvēsvari eṉṉum teyvattai vaḻipaṭuvar. iv vaḻipāṭu kāḷi, turkkai vaḻipāṭṭiṉ vēṟupāṭākum. ivarkaḷ peritum caiva matattiṉar; iliṅkaṅkaṭṭik koḷvar. nāṭṭumakkaḷ pala erutukaḷai vaḷarppar. erutu iṟantuviṭṭāl tukkam koṇ ṭāṭip palar piṉtoṭarntu cella ataṉai eṭuttuc ceṉṟu putaippar.
tēvātikkar: ivarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭattil kōyiṟṟoṇṭu ceyyum kaṉṉaṭam pēcum makkaḷ. mayūravarmaṉ, pūcai māttiram pirāmaṇar ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṟṟa vēlaikaḷ stāṉikar, tēvātik karkaḷ ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟum vitittāṉ. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷiṟ palar uḻavuttoḻil ceykiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷiṉ cotturimai peṇ kaḷaic cārvatu.
toṇṭamāṉ: ivarkaḷ tirunelvēlip pakutiyil vāḻum cuṇṇāmpukkārar. ivarkaḷ putukkōṭṭaiyiliruntu tirunelvēli yiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya kaḷḷaril oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷil toṇṭa māṉ, tōlmēstiri eṉa iru piriviṉaruṇṭu.
tommārar: ivarkaḷ ōriṭattil taṅkātu tiriyum kaḻaik kūttar. ivarkaḷ teluṅku marāṭṭiyar; intustāṉi pēcuvar. ivarkaḷ cātippaṟaiyarilum uyarntavar. āriyak kūttāṭikaḷ eṉpatum ivarkaḷukkup peyar. kaḻaik kūttut toṭaṅkumuṉ ivarkaḷ cāṇiyi ṉāl piḷḷaiyār piṭittuvaittu ataṉai vaḻipaṭuvar.
tōṭar( tōtar) : ivarkaḷ nīlakiri malaiyil vāḻvar; erumai kaḷai vaḷarppar; tamiḻ pēcuvar. ivarkaḷukkuc curuṇṭu toṅkum taṭitta talaimayirum aṭarnta tāṭiyum uṇṭu. peṇkaḷ pūppaṭainta piṉ paccai kuttikkoḷvar. peṇkaḷ māṭṭut toḻuvattil eriyum koḷḷik kaṭṭaiyait toṭṭāl tīṭṭu uṇṭākum. tōtariṉ toḻuvattukkup pirāmaṇaṉ cellutal ākātu. tōtar perum pālum mōril cōṟu ākkuvar. peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavarai maṇappar. tōtappeṇ orutti oruvaṉukku maṉaiviyākumpōtu avaḷ avaṉ cakōtarar ellārukkum maṉaivi eṉpatu viḷaṅkik koḷḷattakkatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil kaṇavar cakōtararāyirāviṭṭālum orē kūṭṭat taic cērntavarāyiruppar. iraṇṭu allatu pala cakōtarar pala peṇkaḷait tamakkup potumaṉaiviyarāka vaittiruppar. iṟanta variṉ uṭal cuṭappaṭum. appoḻutu cuṭalaiyil erumai paliyiṭap paṭum.
tōṭṭi: ivarkaḷ cakkiliyaruḷ oru piriviṉar. tañcāvūrt tōṭṭiyāṉ allatu kampaḷattāṉ paṉṟivaḷarttal , pāmpu piṭittal, piccaiyeṭuttal mutaliya toḻilkaḷaic ceyvāṉ. tōṭṭi tantai yiṉ cakōtari makaḷai allatu tāymāmaṉ makaḷaik kaliyāṇam ceyya lām. ik kaṭṭuppāṭu iruppatāl ciṟuvarukku vaḷarnta peṇkaḷ kaliyāṇam muṭikkappaṭuvar. appoḻutu maṇamakaṉiṉ tantai peṇ ṇōṭu cērntu piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟuvāṉ. tōṭṭiyāṉ pirāmaṇaṉ vīṭṭil uṇṇamāṭṭāṉ. itaṟkuk kāraṇam pulappaṭavillai. peṇkaḷ kuṭumpa vaṭṭattukkuḷ puriyum viyapicāram kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. tōṭṭikaḷiṉ kuṭittalaivaṉ ūr nāyakaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ uṟaviṉarukkut tamatu tayavaik koṭuttal kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. vāyilil oruvaṉiṉ ceruppu iruppataik kaṇṭāl kaṇavaṉ uḷḷē cella māṭṭāṉ. ivarkaḷ iṟantavaraik kal naṭṭu vaḻipaṭuvar.
tōṇi: tōṇi pēḻai pōṉṟatu. 70 aṭi nīḷamum 20 aṭi akalamum 11 aṭi āḻamumuṭaiyatu. aṭi taṭṭaiyāka virukkum. aṭippakuti 7 aṭi akalamuṭaiyatu. teṉṉintiyāvil aṭikkappaṭṭa īya, cempu nāṇayaṅkaḷil tōṇi poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
ṭōpi(Dhobi): itu vaṇṇāṉukku intiyā muḻuvatilum vaḻaṅkum peyar. itu kaḻuvu eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatu. tāva eṉṉum camakkiruta aṭiyākap piṟantatu. ivarkaḷ ātiyil oricā mākāṇatti liruntu vantārkaḷ. maṇak kālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ ēḻu vīṭukaḷiliruntu ēḻu kuṭattil taṇṇīr koṇṭuvantu maṇamakaṉaiyum peṇṇaiyum muḻukkāṭṭuvārkaḷ. maṇakkiriyaiyil iruvariṉ iṭakkaiyum cērttuk kaṭṭappaṭum. pantaliliruppōr iruvar mītum mañcaḷaiyum ariciyaiyum eṟivārkaḷ.
tōriyar: ivarkaḷ kōyamputtūr, cēlam māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ muṟkālattil mīṉ piṭippōrum pallakkuc cumappōrumāka iruntaṉar. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ veṟṟilai payiriṭu var. ivarkaḷiṉ cātittalaivaṉ ecamāṉaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. ivaṉ ivarkaḷiṭaiyē uḷḷa vaḻakkukaḷai viḷaṅkit tīrppaṉ.
nakarattār: ivarkaḷ ceṭṭikaḷil oru piriviṉar. nakarameṉpatu paṭṭiṉam. ivarkaḷ muṉpu kāñcīpurattiliruntārkaḷeṉpatu aitīkam. ivarkaḷiṉ vēlaiyāḷ cātippiḷḷai eṉappaṭuvaṉ. vitavaikaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyvatillai.
naṅkuṭi vēḷāḷaṉ: ivarkaḷ tirunelvēliyiṟ pala pākaṅ kaḷilē vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kōṭṭai vēḷāḷarirukkum ciṟīvaikuntak kōṭṭaikku naṅkuṭi vēḷāḷap peṇkaḷ pōka aṉumatikkappaṭuva tillai. ivarkaḷuḷ oruvar iṟantupōṉāl ac ceytiyai ampaṭṭaṉ aṟivippāṉ. akkiṉi eṉṉum makāmuṉi tavamceytu koṇṭirukkum pōtu teyvap peṇkaḷ nīrāṭavantārkaḷeṉṟum muṉivar avarkaḷaik kātalittu mūṉṟu kumāraraip peṟṟār eṉṟum avarkaḷai vēḷāḷarkaḷ vaḷarttārka ḷeṉṟum avarkaḷē cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷ eṉṟum aitīkaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
nampiṭi: ivarkaḷ malaiyāḷattil kāṇappaṭum nampūtirikaḷ pōṉṟa oru kūṭṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ pūṇūlaṇivar. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam ivarkaḷ contac cātiyiṉarāl ceyyappaṭum. nampūtirimārum, ic cāti āṭavarum nampiṭip peṇkaḷaic campantaṅ koḷvar. nampiṭi āṭavar nāyarp peṇkaḷaic campantam vaikkalām. ivarkaḷ viruntukaḷil nampūtirika ḷōṭu iruntu uṇpar. peṇkaḷ maṉōlapāṭu eṉappaṭuvar.
nampūtiri: kēraḷa uṟpatti eṉṉum nūl malaiyāḷa moḻiyi luḷḷatu. atu paracurāmar kaṉṉiyākumariyiliruntu varuṇaṉai nōkkit tavañ ceytu kaṉṉiyākumari mutal kōkarṇam varaiyiluḷḷa nilattaip peṟṟāreṉṟum, paracurāmarāṟ kuṭiyēṟṟappaṭṭavarkaḷē nampūtirikaḷ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷiṉ urimai vaḻi marumakkaḷ tāyam. nampūtirip peṇkaḷ veḷiyē cellumpōtu tammai vaikkāṉ kuṭaiyāl maṟaittukkoḷvar. viricāli eṉṉum nāyarp peṇ avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉē celvāḷ. nampūtirip peṇkaḷ veḷiyē cellumpōtu tammaik kaḻuttu mutal kālvaraiyum tuṇiyāl pōrttu maṟaittukkoḷvar. ivarkaḷ nakai aṇital kūṭātu. ivarkaḷaip paracurāmar koṇṭu vantu kuṭiyēṟṟiṉār eṉṉum katai nampattakkataṉṟu. ivarkaḷ ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭaiyil malaiyāḷattil kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷākalām. tālami, peripuḷūs eṉpōr pirāmaṇar malaiyāḷattiṟ kuṭiyēṟi yiruntamaiyai ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. mēṟkuc cāḷukkiya aracar valimai peṟṟirunta ki.pi. 4ām, 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil iṅku vantu taṅkiya pirāmaṇar nampūtirikaḷōṭu kalantu oṉṟupaṭṭiruttal kūṭum. cāḷukkiyarukkup paṉṟik koṭi uṇṭu. ātaliṉ cāḷukkiyap pirāmaṇar kuṭiyēṟiya iṭam paṉṉiyūr (paṉṟiyūr) eṉappeyar peṟṟirukkalām. cēvūrp (civaṉūr) pirāmaṇar caiva camayattiṉarāvar. ivarkaḷ cāḷukkiyarukkup piṉ vanta cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷiṉ camayattaiyē piṉpaṟṟiṉārkaḷākalām. perumāḷeṉṉumaracar cēranāṭṭukku ayalē iruntu vantavarkaḷāka lām. ivarkaḷ tamatu conta nāṭṭai viṭṭuvantamaiyāl ivarkaḷ nampūtirip peṇkaḷai maṇantukoḷḷa nampūtirikaḷ maṟṟa vakuppup peṇkaḷaic campantaṅ koṇṭārkaḷākalām. ākavē marumakkaḷ tāyam avaciyamāyiṟṟu. kēraḷattiṉ cila pakutikaḷ atikārikaḷāl āḷappaṭṭatu. vaḷḷuva nāṭallāta maṟṟaiya iṭaṅkaḷil nampūtirikaḷ atikārikaḷāka viruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.
nampūtirimār kōyiṟ pūjai ceyvar. ivarkaḷ aracariṉ tūta rākavum celvar. malaiyāḷattil paṭṭarp pirāmaṇar aṅkumiṅkum alaintu tirivar. avarkaḷukku uṇavu nampūtirikaḷ illaṅkaḷilum kōvilakaṅkaḷilum kōvilkaḷilum kiṭaikkum. nampūtirikaḷukku ēṭu (vētam) paṭittalum paṭippittalum, piccai, ōtu, aṭukkaḷai katavu (ellāp pirāmaṇarum kuḷikkumiṭattil niṉṟu kuḷittal) āṭu (vēḷvi) canti, kuruvāyiruntu kōyiṟ kiriyai ceytal, araṅku, panti (ellāp pirāmaṇarōṭu miruntu uṇṇutal) pōṉṟa urimaikaḷ uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ talaimayirai neṟṟikku muṉṉāl toṅkumpaṭi muṭintuviṭuvar. nallanāḷ pārttu mukattaiyum talaiyaiyum maḻittuk koḷvar. maṉaivi karuppavatiyāṉāl nampūtiri mayirai vaḷaraviṭuvāṉ. ivarkaḷukku aṭarnta mayiruṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷuṭ cila tōtariṭaiyē kāṇappaṭuvaṉa pōlvaṉa. ataṉāl ivarkaḷ tōtaril oru piriviṉar eṉṟu karutappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ viral nakaṅkaḷai nīḷamāka vaḷaraviṭuvar. āṭavariṉ uṭukkum vakai “taṭṭut tūkkuka” eṉappaṭum. nampūtirikaḷ miritaṭi tarippar. peṇkaḷ uṭukkum vakai “okkum koluttum vaccut tūkkuka” eṉappaṭum. āṭavar kātil tuḷaiyiṭṭuk kuṇṭalamaṇivar. peṇkaḷ cūṭṭu eṉṉum kātaṇi ceruttāli mutaliyavaṟṟai aṇivar. avarkaḷ mūkkait tuḷaiyiṭṭu aṇiyum aṇikaḷai aṇivatillai; nīrāṭiyapiṉ neṟṟiyil cantaṉat tāl mūṉṟu kuṟikaḷ vaippar. atu ampulik kuṟi eṉappaṭum. avarkaḷ oru pōtum kuṅkuma maṇivatillai; mañcaḷ kuḷippatu millai. āṭavar kai viralil pavittiram eṉṉum āḻi aṇivārkaḷ; pūṇūlil yantirat takaṭu aṭaitta kūṭṭait toṅkaviṭuvārkaḷ. paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatup paiyaṉ kavarimāṉ tōl vārai iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāl pōṭṭiruppāṉ. iraṇṭu talaippukaḷum at tōliṉālēyē poruttit taikkappaṭṭirukkum. ivarkaḷ tirumaṇattukkup piṉ pūṇūlai māṟṟik koḷvatillai. teṉṉintiya pirāmaṇar ōr āṇṭukku oru muṟai pūṇūlai māṟṟuvar. nampūtiri irukkum vaḷavuk kup peyaruṇṭu. vīṭṭiṉ vaṭakiḻakkil toḻuvam irukkum. atu kōcālai eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷ iruppataṟkup perumpālum māṉ tōlaip payaṉpaṭuttuvar. nampūtiriyiṉ mūtta kumāraṉ kāraṇavaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. avaṉ uṇavai uṇṭa piṉ eḻumpōtu iṭakkaiyāl ilaiyait toṭuvāṉ;
